Robustness is usually restored either by building equivariance into the architecture or by retraining with augmentation, both of which require changing or retraining the model. Test-time canonicalization instead leaves the classifier untouched. It undoes the transformation of each input, mapping it to a canonical form near the training distribution before classification. Existing canonicalizers, however, rely on a narrow set of logit-based energy scores and bespoke search procedures, leaving the design space of scoring functions and optimizers unexplored. We reframe canonicalization as out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, which lets any OOD score serve as the energy minimized over transformations. Across benchmarks ranging from handwritten characters and sketches to natural images and 3D point clouds, we systematically evaluate around twenty OOD scores and nine search algorithms, finding that distance-based scores paired with random search and local refinement perform best overall. Because canonicalizing an already-aligned input can hurt accuracy, we add a gated mechanism that transforms an input only when its OOD score indicates this is needed, preserving most in-distribution accuracy while retaining the robustness gains on transformed inputs. Code is available at github.com/johschm/its.

</details>


### [37] [Deep Learning Approaches for 3D Medical Scene Completion: From Geometric Modeling to Generative Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24180)
*Afifa Khaled,Said Jadid Abdulkadir,Majdy Mohamed Eltayeb Eltahir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2016-2026年间三维场景补全领域的研究进展，从SSCNet的体素语义补全范式演进至结合生成扩散先验与3D高斯溅射实时渲染的最新范式，涵盖了体素网格、点学习、隐式神经场、Transformer、扩散网络及渲染感知3D高斯基元等多种表示范式的演变，并提出了分类体系和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维场景补全在计算机视觉与机器人领域具有广泛应用（如自动驾驶、增强现实），但该领域近十年发展迅速，表示范式不断更迭，亟需一篇系统性综述来梳理发展脉络、建立分类体系，并为下一代系统开发指明方向。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，对2016-2026年间该领域的文献进行系统收集与分析，基于表示范式（体素网格、点学习、隐式神经场、Transformer、扩散网络、3D高斯基元）构建分类学框架，并对各类贡献进行综合比较。

Result: 形成了三维场景补全领域的完整分类体系，明确了从体素语义补全到生成式扩散先验+高斯溅射实时渲染的演进路径，并识别了当前尚未解决的技术挑战。

Conclusion: 三维场景补全领域经历了多轮范式革命，表示能力与渲染效率持续提升；当前面临的主要挑战仍待攻克；未来应基于本研究提出的研究议程推进下一代系统的开发。

Abstract: Three-dimensional scene completion has evolved as a major problem in computer vision and robotics, and its applications are diverse, including autonomous navigation and augmented reality. In this study, a systematic review has been conducted to compile the research contributions made in the last ten years, i.e., 2016 to 2026, which has revolutionized the field from the voxel semantic completion paradigm represented by SSCNet to the latest paradigm that combines generative diffusion priors with real-time rendering using a Gaussian splatting technique. The evolution in representation paradigms, such as voxel grids, point learning, implicit neural fields, transformer networks, diffusion networks, and the latest paradigm based on rendering-aware 3D Gaussian primitives, has been discussed in this study. A comprehensive analysis has been carried out on the contributions made in the last ten years, and a taxonomy has been developed to provide a clear idea about the contributions made in the field. The study has also discussed the research contributions made in the field, along with the challenges that still need to be addressed. Finally, the study has presented a research agenda that will provide a clear idea about the directions that can be followed in the development of the next-generation system

</details>


### [38] [Towards Fast and Effective Long Video Understanding of Multimodal Large Language Models via Adaptive Quasi-Gaussian Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24187)
*Kun Zhang,Chenxin Fang,Tao Chen,Baiyang Song,Yunhang Shen,Yiyi Zhou,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaQ，一种基于拟高斯采样的自适应、无需训练的视频帧选择方法，通过动态调整3-σ区间实现鲁棒且高效的帧采样，显著提升多模态大模型在长视频理解任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解中，多模态大模型面临巨大的计算和内存开销。现有关键帧选择方法采用硬采样策略，存在灵活性低、噪声大的问题。

Method: 将视频帧选择定义为拟高斯采样问题，受高斯分布3-σ规则启发提出AdaQ方法：针对局部查询采用较小的3-σ区间，全局查询采用较大的3-σ区间，实现自适应、无需训练的帧采样。

Result: 在4种多模态大模型和3种嵌入模型上进行实验，仅用64帧即可使Qwen3-VL-8B平均超越GPT4o 15.8%，优于默认MLLM和现有SOTA关键帧选择方法，且仅需设置1个超参数。

Conclusion: AdaQ是一种鲁棒且高效的训练无关帧选择方法，能显著提升长视频理解性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long video understanding remains a daunting challenge for \emph{Multimodal Large Language Models} (MLLMs) due to the excessive computation and memory footprint. Thus, \emph{keyframe selection} is often adopted to mitigate this shortcoming, which however still suffers from low flexibility and high noise due to its hard sampling principle. In this paper, we define video frame selection as a problem of \emph{Quasi-Gaussian Sampling}, and propose an adaptive and training-free approach termed \textbf{\emph{AdaQ}}. Inspired by the $3$-$σ$ rule of Gaussian distribution, the objective of AdaQ is to achieve the optimal $3$-$σ$ interval for different examples, \emph{i.e.}, a smaller $3$-$σ$ interval for the local query and a larger one for the global query, thereby facilitating robust and adaptive frame sampling. To validate AdaQ, we apply it to four MLLMs with three embedding models. The extensive experimental results not only show its obvious performance gains over the default MLLMs and the SOTA keyframe selection methods, \emph{e.g.}, helping Qwen3-VL-8B outperform GPT4o by 15.8\% on average by using only 64 frames, but also confirm its superior robustness and high efficiency for long-video understanding, \emph{e.g.}, \textbf{only 1 hyper-parameter} needs to be set. \textbf{Our code project} is given at \href{https://github.com/Zkayovo-xmu/AdaQ}{https://github.com/Zkayovo-xmu/AdaQ}.

</details>


### [39] [From Open Waters to Enclosed Cabins: ProteusVPR for Cross-Scene Visual Place Recognition in Maritime Perception and Cabin Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24234)
*Zexi Chena,Zitai Huang,Qiwen Gu,Zhiqi Li,Shengli Dong,Chenlei Wang,Junqiao Zhao,Hongdong Wang,Bing Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProteusVPR，一种面向海上自主机器人巡检的两阶段检索-精炼视觉定位框架。第一阶段使用标准VPR模型进行初检，第二阶段通过几何-视觉估计网络融合检索图像与两帧时序前帧，利用几何描述子、局部仿射坐标系和相机方位角编码实现精确定位。在自建的XHZ数据集（8K全景船舶数据集）上，该方法将平均定位误差降低60%以上，有效解决了跨场景海上环境下的视觉定位难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上巡检机器人在船舶环境中面临显著的跨场景感知偏移：开阔甲板纹理稀疏、光照剧烈变化，封闭舱室结构重复、视觉模糊度高。现有的VPR方法主要针对城市或室内场景设计，无法可靠地泛化到这些差异巨大的海上场景，因此需要专门针对船舶环境的高精度视觉定位方法。

Method: ProteusVPR采用两阶段检索-精炼框架：第一阶段使用任意标准VPR模型进行初始图像检索；第二阶段引入几何-视觉估计网络，融合检索图像与两帧时间上相邻的前序帧，结合几何描述子、局部仿射坐标系和相机方位角编码实现精确定位。同时构建了XHZ数据集（8K全景船舶数据集），包含多楼层舱室结构、甲板过渡区域，并严格划分查询-数据库。

Result: 在XHZ数据集上的大量实验表明，ProteusVPR在多个VPR骨干网络上均能持续提升定位精度，平均降低定位误差超过60%，为具有挑战性的跨场景海上环境提供了有效且鲁棒的精确视觉定位解决方案。

Conclusion: ProteusVPR有效解决了海上自主巡检中跨场景视觉定位的挑战，通过两阶段检索-精炼框架显著提升了定位精度，为船舶机器人在复杂环境中的自主导航提供了可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic inspection in maritime environments presents unique challenges for Visual Place Recognition (VPR) due to cross-scene perceptual shifts. Robots navigating ship-borne environments must transition between visually distinct domains: open decks with sparse textures and severe illumination changes, and enclosed cabins with repetitive structures and high visual ambiguity. Existing VPR methods, designed primarily for urban or indoor scenes, fail to generalize reliably across these starkly different scenarios. To address this, we propose ProteusVPR, a two-stage retrieval-refinement framework. The first stage employs any standard VPR model for initial image retrieval. The second stage introduces a geometric-visual estimation network that fuses the retrieved image with two temporally preceding frames, incorporating geometric descriptors, a local affine coordinate system, and camera azimuth encoding to achieve precise localization. To support this task, we introduce the XHZ dataset, an 8K-panoramic ship-borne dataset collected from an operational vessel, featuring multi-floor cabin structures, deck transition zones, and strict query-database separation for rigorous evaluation. Extensive experiments on the XHZ dataset demonstrate that ProteusVPR consistently improves the localization accuracy across multiple VPR backbones, reducing mean localization error by over 60\% on average and that ProteusVPR offers an effective and robust solution for precise visual localization in challenging, cross-scene maritime environments.

</details>


### [40] [Co-occurring associated retained concepts in Diffusion Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24192)
*Miso Kim,Georu Lee,Yunji Kim,Hoki Kim,Jinseong Park,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE分数用于量化扩散模型概念遗忘中良性共现概念的保留程度，并基于此提出ReCARE框架，通过自动构建CARE集（共现关联保留概念词汇表）在训练中保护这些概念，实现仅擦除目标概念而保留共现概念，在多个任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的概念遗忘方法在移除目标概念（如裸体）时，会连带抑制与之共现的良性概念（如人物），导致模型生成能力受损。亟需一种能够明确量化并保护这些共现概念的方法。

Method: 提出CARE分数作为通用度量，直接量化共现关联保留概念的保留程度。在此基础上提出ReCARE框架：（1）自动从目标图像中提取良性共现token构建CARE集；（2）在训练中利用该词汇表进行稳定遗忘，仅擦除目标概念。

Result: 在裸体、梵高风格、丁鳜对象等多个目标概念上，ReCARE在鲁棒概念擦除、整体效用和CARE保留三方面达到整体最优性能。

Conclusion: ReCARE有效平衡了目标概念擦除与共现概念保留之间的矛盾，为扩散模型的安全遗忘提供了更精细化的解决方案。

Abstract: Unlearning has emerged as a key technique to mitigate harmful content generation in diffusion models. However, existing methods often remove not only the target concept, but also benign co-occurring concepts. As illustrated in Fig.1, unlearning nudity can unintentionally suppress the concept of person, preventing a model from generating images with person. We define these undesirably suppressed co-occurring concepts that must be preserved CARE (Co-occurring Associated REtained concepts). Then, we introduce the CARE score, a general metric that directly quantifies their preservation across unlearning tasks. With this foundation, we propose ReCARE (Robust erasure for CARE), a framework that explicitly safeguards CARE while erasing only the target concept. ReCARE automatically constructs the CARE-set, a curated vocabulary of benign co-occurring tokens extracted from target images, and leverages this vocabulary during training for stable unlearning. Extensive experiments across various target concepts (Nudity, Van Gogh style, and Tench object) demonstrate that ReCARE achieves overall state-of-the-art performance in balancing robust concept erasure, overall utility, and CARE preservation.

</details>


### [41] [Inclusive Interactive Collisions for Multi-View Consistent Compositional 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24206)
*Chang Liu,Mingwen Shao,Xiang Lv,Xinyuan Chen,Lingzhuang Meng,Qiao Zhang,Zhengyi Gong,Jinghao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出I2C-3D方法，通过交互碰撞策略和多视图自适应得分蒸馏采样，实现多视图一致的组合式3D资产生成，解决了现有方法在组合式3D场景生成中的物体交互不合理和跨视图不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D生成方法主要生成单一物体，难以生成多物体组合式3D资产，且存在跨视图不一致问题，因为得分蒸馏采样在每个单视图上执行导致跨视图幻觉。

Method: 提出I2C-3D方法：(1) Inclusive Interactive Collisions策略引导高斯原语在合理的交互区域出现，确保组合场景中物体以物理合理且视觉一致的方式交互；(2) Multi-View Adaptive Score Distillation Sampling通过调制跨视点的实例token和空间token的注意力图，从预训练扩散模型中蒸馏多视图一致性先验和布局先验。

Result: I2C-3D不仅能生成高保真多视图一致的组合式3D资产，还支持灵活的3D编辑，促进复杂场景生成。实验表明在生成质量和多视图一致性上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的交互碰撞策略和多视图自适应蒸馏方法，I2C-3D有效解决了组合式3D生成中的物体交互和多视图一致性问题，并支持灵活编辑，为复杂场景生成提供了新方案。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in 3D generation have advanced notably with the development of text-to-image diffusion model. However, existing methods remain two practical challenges: (1) They primarily generate single 3D object, but struggle to generate multi-object compositional 3D assets due to the lack of the modeling for Gaussian primitives in reasonable interactions. (2) They often suffer from cross-view inconsistency during 3D optimization, as Score Distillation Sampling inherently performs on each single view, inevitably resulting in cross-view hallucinations. To solve above issues, we propose I2C-3D, a novel optimization-based method to generate multi-view consistent compositional 3D assets with reasonable interactions. Specifically, we propose an Inclusive Interactive Collisions strategy to guide Gaussian primitives appearing in reasonable interaction regions naturally, thereby ensuring objects in the compositional scene interact in a physically plausible and visually coherent way. Additionally, to enhance multi-view consistency, Multi-View Adaptive Score Distillation Sampling is devised to distill multi-view consistency prior and layout prior from pre-trained diffusion model by modulating attention map of instance token and spatial token across viewpoints. Benefiting from above elaborate designs, I2C-3D not only generates high-fidelity multi-view consistent compositional 3D assets but also supports 3D editing flexibly, facilitating complex scene generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate our I2C-3D outperforms existing methods in generation quality and multi-view consistency.

</details>


### [42] [Compact Object-Level Representations with Open-Vocabulary Understanding for Indoor Visual Relocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24767)
*Zhaopeng Cui,Jiarui Hu,Jingbo Liu,Boming Zhao,Xiyue Guo,Boyin Feng,Haocheng Peng,Yujun Shen,Hujun Bao,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenReLoc是一个仅利用场景中物体信息（语义、布局、几何）进行室内视觉重定位的系统，通过多模态开放词汇匹配、基于DIOU的参考帧选择和双路径2D ICP损失，实现了高精度和高召回率的相机位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内视觉重定位方法主要依赖低层视觉方案，难以感知场景语义和构成，限制了可解释性和适用性。本文探索如何将场景中的丰富物体信息（语义、布局、几何）组织成结构化地图表示，并仅利用物体单元驱动相机重定位任务。

Method: 1) 提出多模态机制，利用基础模型集成开放词汇语义知识，实现有效的2D-3D物体匹配；2) 设计面向物体的参考坐标系作为位置先验，并基于Distance-IoU (DIOU)提出参考帧选择策略，支持可扩展场景；3) 提出双路径2D Iterative Closest Pixel (ICP)损失，由物体形状引导，确保稳定准确的位姿优化。

Result: 在多个数据集上，OpenReLoc实现了优越的重定位召回率和精度。

Conclusion: OpenReLoc通过结构化物体地图表示和物体级推理，有效结合了场景理解与精确位姿估计，为室内视觉重定位提供了一种更具可解释性和适用性的新范式。

Abstract: Indoor visual relocalization plays a critical role in emerging spatial and embodied AI applications. However, prior research was predominantly devoted to low-level vision schemes, struggling to perceive scene semantics and compositions, which limits both interpretability and applicability. In this paper, we explore the issue of how to organize rich object information in a scene, including semantics, layout, and geometry, into a structured map representation, thereby utilizing object units exclusively to drive the camera relocalization task. To this end, we propose OpenReLoc, a camera relocalization system designed to provide scene understanding and accurate pose estimation capabilities. Leveraging recent foundation models, we first introduce a multi-modal mechanism to integrate open-vocabulary semantic knowledge for effective 2D-3D object matching. Additionally, we design object-oriented reference frames as position priors, paired with a reference frame selection strategy based on the Distance-IoU (DIOU), enabling extension to scalable scenes. Moreover, to ensure stable and accurate pose optimization, we also propose a dual-path 2D Iterative Closest Pixel loss guided by object shape. Experimental results demonstrate that OpenReLoc achieves superior relocalization recall and accuracy across various datasets. Our source code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [43] [MorVess: Morphology-Aware Pulmonary Vessel Segmentation Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24214)
*Fuyou Mao,Yifei Chen,Beining Wu,Lixin Lin,Jinnan Dai,Zhiling Li,Yilei Chen,Yaqi Wang,Hao Zhang,Yan Tang,Huiyu Zhou,Feiwei Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MorVess是一种融合可微几何先验与大规模基础模型（SAM）的形态感知分割框架，通过联合预测血管掩膜、距离图和厚度图，并采用轻量级2.5D适配器和全局-局部融合模块，在肺CT数据集上实现了对小血管和拓扑完整性的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习分割模型主要优化二元掩膜，缺乏显式几何约束，难以恢复连续的管状形态和精细的血管连通性，小分支易丢失、拓扑完整性难以保持。

Method: MorVess联合预测血管掩膜、距离图和厚度图，提供血管边界、中心线一致性和平滑直径过渡的显式监督；采用轻量级2.5D适配器桥接3D空间上下文与2D SAM表示；全局-局部融合模块聚合多层级语义与几何线索以实现高保真拓扑重建。

Result: 在两个肺CT基准上，MorVess在Dice、clDice和HD95指标上均表现优异，显著改善了小血管恢复和全局连通性。

Conclusion: 将几何智能嵌入预训练视觉模型为精确血管分析和临床可靠的结构量化提供了一种有原则且可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Accurate pulmonary vessel segmentation remains challenging due to the sparse, tortuous, and multi-scale nature of vascular structures, where small branches are easily lost and topology integrity is difficult to preserve under voxel-wise supervision. Existing deep segmentation models primarily optimize binary masks, lacking explicit geometric constraints, thus struggling to recover continuous tubular morphology and fine vascular connectivity. In this study, we introduce MorVess, a morphology-aware segmentation framework that integrates differentiable geometric priors with large-scale foundation model adaptation to achieve fine-grained vascular parsing. MorVess jointly predicts vessel masks, distance maps, and thickness maps, providing explicit supervision for vascular boundaries, centerline consistency, and smooth diameter transitions. A lightweight 2.5D adapter bridges 3D spatial context and 2D SAM representations, while a global-local fusion block aggregates multi-level semantics and geometric cues for high-fidelity topology reconstruction. Across two challenging pulmonary CT benchmarks, MorVess delivers superior Dice, clDice, and HD95 scores, substantially improving small-vessel recovery and global connectivity. These results demonstrate that embedding geometric intelligence into pretrained vision models offers a principled and scalable pathway toward precise vessel analysis and clinically reliable structural quantification. Our source code is available at https://github.com/MaoFuyou/MorVess.

</details>


### [44] [Geometry-Instructed Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24225)
*Chirui Chang,Xiaoyang Lyu,Yi-Hua Huang,Haoru Tan,Shizhen Zhao,Yikang Ding,Jianmin Bao,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GIVE是一个基于几何指令的视频编辑框架，通过统一的对象状态表述（深度框+方向框）实现物体级别的几何编辑（平移、旋转、缩放、复制、删除），并利用可扩展的图形引擎生成成对训练数据，在保持时序一致性和次级效果（阴影、反射）的同时，实现了可靠的生成式视频几何编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物体级几何编辑（平移、旋转、缩放、复制、删除）是数字内容创作的常规操作，但在生成式视频编辑中仍不可靠。关键在于如何在视角和时间变化下清晰指定目标物体的3D状态变化，同时一致地更新依赖于几何的次级效果（如阴影和反射）。

Method: 提出GIVE框架，核心创新包括：1）统一对象状态表述，通过两种视频对齐的几何流（深度框编码粗略3D位置和范围，方向框提供外观无关的朝向线索）描述编辑前后目标物体状态；2）构建可扩展的图形引擎管线，执行物体级编辑程序并渲染受控的前后对比对，隔离几何编辑的同时保持次级效果与变换一致。

Result: 实验表明GIVE能在统一框架下生成具有时序连贯性和一致次级效果的忠实几何编辑，并在野外视频上展现出良好的迁移能力。

Conclusion: GIVE通过几何指令驱动的统一框架，成功解决了生成式视频编辑中物体级几何变换的可靠性问题，为视频编辑中的3D状态变化指定与次级效果一致性维护提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Object-level geometric edits, including translating, rotating, scaling, duplicating, or removing an object, are routine operations in digital content creation (DCC) workflows, yet they remain unreliable in generative video editing. The key challenge lies in specifying the target object's 3D state change unambiguously across viewpoint and time, while consistently updating geometry-dependent secondary effects such as shadows and reflections. We introduce GIVE, a geometry-instructed video editing framework that represents edits through a unified object-state formulation. Two video-aligned geometry streams describe the target object before and after editing: a depth-box encoding coarse 3D placement and extent, and an orientation-box providing an appearance-agnostic orientation cue. Together, these streams provide a compact pre/post geometric specification for object-state transitions. To provide paired supervision for learning these edits, we build a scalable graphics-engine pipeline that executes object-level edit programs and renders controlled before/after pairs, isolating the intended geometric edit while keeping secondary effects consistent with the transformation. Experimental results demonstrate that GIVE produces faithful geometric edits with temporal coherence and consistent secondary effects across operators in a unified framework, and shows promising transfer to in-the-wild videos. Project page: https://geometry-instructed-video-editing.github.io/give/

</details>


### [45] [FiCA: Feed-forward instant Gaussian Codec Avatars from a Single Portrait Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24232)
*Kim Youwang,Zhengyu Yang,Liuhao Ge,Yu Rong,Timur Bagautdinov,Su Zhaoen,Nir Sopher,Jovan Popović,Teng Deng,Tae-Hyun Oh,Chen Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FiCA是一种前馈式、即时的高斯编码虚拟化身生成管线，能从单张肖像图像生成逼真、可驱动的3D高斯虚拟化身，无需测试时优化，效果优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像生成逼真且可驱动的虚拟化身极具挑战性，因为有限的视觉信息难以准确推断人头的3D外观与几何结构。

Method: 将人类视觉基础模型与扩散模型结合，学习从部分观测到完整3D网格的生成映射；提出前馈式网格精炼网络增强保真度与身份保持；利用通用先验模型将生成网格解码为3D高斯集合，实现实时表情驱动。

Result: 生成的虚拟化身能忠实表示多样身份，视觉效果优于近期竞争方法。

Conclusion: FiCA作为前馈式方案，无需测试时优化即可生成高质量、可驱动的3D高斯虚拟化身，在身份保真度和视觉质量上具有优势。

Abstract: We introduce FiCA, a Feed-forward, instant Gaussian Codec Avatar generation pipeline that creates lifelike avatars from a single portrait image. Generating a photorealistic and drivable avatar from just a single image is significantly challenging due to the limited visual information available to accurately infer the 3D appearance and geometry of human heads. To address this, we develop a novel system that combines human-centric vision foundation models with a diffusion model. This system is designed to fully exploit partial visual observations to generate lifelike human avatars. Our proposed diffusion model learns a generative mapping from these partial observations to complete and authentic 3D mesh reconstruction. Additionally, we introduce a feed-forward mesh refinement network that enhances the fidelity and identity preservation of the generated avatars, eliminating the need for person-specific test-time optimization. By leveraging a universal prior model that decodes a generated mesh into a set of 3D Gaussians, we generate a photorealistic 3D Gaussian avatar, capable of being driven with novel expressions in real-time. Our experiments demonstrate that the avatars generated by our feed-forward approach faithfully represent diverse identities and surpass the visual quality of avatars produced by recent competing methods.

</details>


### [46] [Latent Visual States for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24233)
*Xiuwei Chen,Wentao Hu,Yongxin Wang,Zisheng Chen,Likui Zhang,Kun Xiang,Jianhua Han,Hui-Ling Zhen,Jingyuan Zou,Hang Xu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EVA框架，通过原生生成连续潜视觉表征（Latent_slot tokens）来替代传统离散输出调用外部工具的方式，实现端到端协同优化，并针对过渡窗口中的策略偏差提出D-GSPO解耦优化方法，在多个基准测试中取得显著性能提升和推理效率改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型多模态模型依赖生成离散输出（如代码或边界框坐标）来调用外部工具，这一过程存在刚性依赖和高延迟问题，限制了模型的效率与灵活性。

Method: 提出EVA框架，原生生成连续潜视觉表征（Latent_slot tokens）作为推理过程中的中间视觉思维，与离散文本token进行端到端联合训练；针对联合优化导致的过渡窗口策略偏差，提出D-GSPO（解耦优化）方法；并构建EVA-230K高质量图文交错CoT数据集用于监督微调。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的大量实验表明，EVA在显著提升性能的同时，提高了推理效率。

Conclusion: 连续潜视觉表征作为中间视觉思维的有效性得到验证，解耦优化方法解决了联合训练中的策略偏差问题，EVA框架为多模态推理提供了一种更高效的内生式视觉推理路径。

Abstract: The integration of visual evidence has significantly enhanced the capabilities of large multimodal models. However, this integration predominantly relies on generating discrete outputs (etc., code or box coordinates) to invoke external tools, a process that introduces rigid dependencies and substantial latency. To overcome these limitations, we propose {EVA} (LatEnt Visual StAtes), a novel framework that natively generates continuous latent visual representations. These internal representations manifest as an adaptive sequence of Latent\_slot tokens, serving as intermediate visual thoughts during the reasoning process. These Latent\_slot tokens are then trained end-to-end with the discrete text tokens. This co-optimization, notably, causes extreme policy deviation in the 'transition window' following the Latent\_slot tokens. We develop D-GSPO (Decouple-GSPO) to target this root cause by decoupling the optimization of latent and discrete components. To support SFT, we construct EVA-230K, a high-quality text-image interleaved CoT dataset encompassing a diverse range of real-world scenes, documents, charts and OCR tasks. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks confirm that EVA achieves significant performance gains while enhancing inference efficiency.

</details>


### [47] [M^2C-EvDet: Multi-Domain Multi-Order Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation for Event-based Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24248)
*Wei Bao,Siqi Li,Shouan Pan,Yi Xie,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出M²C-EvDet，一种基于频率学习和超图计算的多域多阶跨模态知识蒸馏框架，通过自适应频率解耦特征蒸馏(AF²D²)和多阶关系蒸馏(MORD)两个模块，缩小事件检测与基于帧的检测之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件数据固有的稀疏表示和不足的视觉语义导致事件检测与帧基检测之间存在显著性能差距，现有知识蒸馏方法仅关注空间视觉语义或成对关系信息，在更复杂场景下性能受限。

Method: 提出M²C-EvDet框架，包含两个核心模块：(1)自适应频率解耦特征蒸馏(AF²D²)，基于频率学习进行特征解耦；(2)多阶关系蒸馏(MORD)，基于超图计算建模高阶关系。

Result: 在需要高时间分辨率和宽动态范围的复杂场景下，该方法有效缓解了跨模态差异，提升了事件检测性能。

Conclusion: 通过多域多阶的跨模态知识蒸馏，M²C-EvDet在弥补事件数据与帧数据间的语义鸿沟方面优于仅关注空间语义或成对关系的方法。

Abstract: Event-based object Detection (EvDet), as a biologically inspired visual perception paradigm, demonstrates superior performance in scenarios demanding high temporal resolution and a wide dynamic range. Nevertheless, the inherent sparse representations and inadequate visual semantics of event data result in a considerable performance disparity between EvDet and frame-based object detection. Previous works attempt to alleviate this cross-modal discrepancy through knowledge distillation, yet they only focus on spatial visual semantics or pair-wise relational information, thus limiting performance in more complex scenarios. To address this challenge, this paper proposes M^2C-EvDet, a Multi-domain and Multi-order Cross-modal knowledge distillation framework for EvDet. Built upon frequency learning and hypergraph computation, M^2C-EvDet integrates two specialized modules: Adaptive Frequency-Decoupled Feature Distillation (AF^2D^2) and Multi-Order Relational Distillation (MORD).

</details>


### [48] [TuringViT: Making SOTA Vision Transformers Accessible to All](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24253)
*Qiman Wu,Hanlin Chen,Lyujie Chen,Rui Xin,Jianlei Zheng,Mingyuan Wang,Jiahui Hu,Da Zhu,Yuecheng Ma,Yuhua Wei,Yizhao Wang,Hua Zhou,Yuheng Zhang,Anhua Liu,Shaman Tang,Yue He,Pengfei Diao,Shuang Su,Haotong Xin,Weichao Huang,Hang Zhang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TuringViT 通过图灵线性注意力 (TLA)、VISTA-Curation 数据构建和原生动态分辨率预训练三大设计，仅用 10% 的数据量即可超越主流开源 ViT，实现高效的高分辨率处理与下游 VLM 部署，并提供了可复现的低成本训练管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM/VLA 系统常直接使用 SigLIP2 等现成 ViT 作为视觉编码器，但下游任务对延迟、时序建模和 VLM 集成有定制化需求；同时，训练 SOTA 级 ViT 需海量图文数据，标准 softmax 注意力在高分辨率或动态分辨率下计算成本过高，迫使社区采用低分辨率预训练后再后处理适配。

Method: 1) 图灵线性注意力 (TLA)：用于高效的序列建模；2) VISTA-Curation：构建富含监督信号的图像-视频训练数据；3) 原生动态分辨率预训练：从一开始就支持灵活输入，无缝迁移到下游 VLM。

Result: 仅用 10% 的数据量即超越主流开源 ViT 基线；下游 VLM 性能更强；在高分辨率输入下延迟缩放显著更优；缩放定律分析显示性能随数据规模持续可预测提升，远未饱和。

Conclusion: TuringViT 提供了可复现的低成本训练管线，大幅降低了社区训练、定制和部署 SOTA 级 ViT 的门槛，已在 XPeng 的 AI 系统中作为统一视觉基础模型广泛应用。

Abstract: Modern VLMs and VLA systems commonly adopt off-the-shelf ViTs such as SigLIP2 as visual encoders, but diverse downstream requirements in latency, temporal modeling, and VLM integration often call for customized SOTA-level ViTs. Training such encoders remains beyond the reach of much of the community, as it requires massive image-text data, while standard softmax attention makes high-resolution or dynamic-resolution pretraining prohibitively costly and often forces low-resolution pretraining followed by post-hoc adaptation. TuringViT addresses these challenges with three key designs: Turing Linear Attention (TLA) for efficient sequence modeling, VISTA-Curation to construct supervision-rich image-video training data, and native dynamic-resolution pretraining that supports flexible inputs from the start and transfers seamlessly to downstream VLMs. As a result, TuringViT outperforms leading open-source ViT baselines with only 10% of the data, achieves stronger downstream VLM performance, and delivers substantially better latency scaling on high-resolution inputs. Our scaling-law analysis further shows that TuringViT continues to improve predictably with curated data scale, far from saturation. Its fast adaptation, hardware-friendly design, and efficient deployment have made it a unified visual foundation across XPeng's AI systems. More broadly, TuringViT provides a reproducible pipeline that dramatically lowers the cost for the community to train, customize, and deploy SOTA-level ViTs, moving toward making such Vision Transformers accessible to all.

</details>


### [49] [Social Structure Matters in 3D Human-Human Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24255)
*Zhongju Wang,Beier Wang,Yatao Bian,Pichao Wang,Zhi Wang,Daoyi Dong,Hongdong Li,Huadong Mo,Zhenhong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Solo-to-Social框架，采用"LLM规划者+运动执行者"的双层范式，将文本驱动的双人交互生成为社交结构建模与运动实现两个子问题，实现了相位一致、角色对齐和伙伴感知的3D人体交互运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到运动生成方法在单人运动上表现良好，但扩展到双人交互时面临挑战，因为交互需要建模社交结构（包括阶段进展、参与者角色和协调机制）。作者旨在解决如何将隐式的交互语义转化为可执行的、物理合理的双人协调运动。

Method: 提出Think with LLM, Move with Motion Skill的规划-执行范式。LLM规划器将交互语义分解为阶段、分配角色并与运动序列对齐；运动执行器通过LoRA微调、前一阶段自条件化和自我-相对伙伴条件化，将预训练的单人运动模型适配为双人协调运动生成模型。

Result: 生成的3D双人交互运动在相位一致性、角色对齐和伙伴感知协调方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: LLM擅长推理交互结构但不能直接生成动态运动，而通过将社交结构建模与运动实现分离，可以有效生成高质量的双人交互运动。

Abstract: Although text-to-motion generation has achieved strong progress in synthesizing realistic single-person motions from language, extending it to text-driven 3D human-human interaction (HHI) remains non-trivial, as HHI requires modeling the underlying \textbf{social structure} that governs phase progression, actor roles, and inter-actor coordination. In this paper, we formulate HHI generation as a social structure modeling and grounding problem: the model must first infer how an interaction unfolds and how the two actors coordinate their roles, and then realize this structure as continuous, physically plausible, and partner-aware 3D motion. To study how such structure should be modeled, we first examine the capability boundary of large language models (LLMs) for HHI generation. Our analysis shows that LLMs can \textit{think} by recovering phase decompositions and partner-aware roles, but cannot directly \textit{move}, as they fail to generate dynamic, physically plausible, and interaction-aware motion. This motivates our planner-executor paradigm, \textbf{Think with LLM, Move with Motion Skill}. The LLM planner converts implicit interaction semantics into motion-aligned social supervision by decomposing interactions into phases, assigning partner-aware actor roles, and aligning them with motion sequence. The motion executor then grounds the planned social structure into coordinated two-person motion by adapting a pretrained solo motion model with LoRA, previous-phase self-conditioning, and ego-relative partner conditioning. Together, our Solo-to-Social framework bridges social organization and motion realization, producing 3D HHI with improved phase consistency, role alignment, and partner-aware coordination.

</details>


### [50] [Trimming the Long-Tail of Visual World Modeling Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24256)
*Bingxuan Li,Yining Hong,Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiateng Liu,Zhenhailong Wang,Yue Guo,Yunzhu Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tailor-Bench是一个评估视觉世界模型模拟非常规物理交互能力的基准，通过三种场景模式（常规、非常规、不可能）和两种生成设置，揭示模型存在长尾物理建模鸿沟，依赖表面视觉模式而非真正理解物理原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理交互呈长尾分布，现有视觉世界模型虽能模拟常见物理交互，但缺乏对稀有的、非常规物理交互的泛化能力评估。需要系统性基准来检验模型是否真正内化并泛化物理原理，而非仅记忆表面模式。

Method: 提出Tailor-Bench基准，设计三种场景模式（常规、非常规、不可能）逐步挑战模型推理能力；并设计两种互补设置（预测生成和描述生成）在统一评估协议下进行评测。

Result: 模型性能从常规到非常规再到不可能场景持续下降，暴露出长尾物理建模鸿沟。图像模型无法正确实现状态变化，视频模型进一步存在时间一致性错误。

Conclusion: 当前视觉世界模型未能真正内化物理原理，其泛化能力局限于常见交互，依赖表面视觉模式，在非常规和小概率物理交互场景下表现显著退化。

Abstract: Physical interactions follow a long-tailed distribution: a set of common and regular interactions dominates human experience and visual data, while a broad spectrum of rare and irregular interactions remains underrepresented. Although recent visual world models, including image and video generation models, achieve impressive realism on existing benchmarks, they primarily focus on simulating common physical interactions. This raises a central question: Do current visual world models internalize and generalize physical principles? In this work, we introduce Tailor-Bench, a benchmark that challenges world models to simulate irregular physical interactions. To enable systematic evaluation, we design three scenario modes that progressively challenge model reasoning: Regular scenarios reflect common tool-task pairs, Unconventional scenarios replace conventional tools with attribute-compatible substitutes to test affordance generalization, and Impossible scenarios introduce attribute-violating tools to probe constraint awareness. Additionally, we design two complementary settings under a unified evaluation protocol: predictive generation requires inferring outcomes without guidance, while descriptive generation specifies the target outcome for faithful realization. Our experimental results reveal a clear long-tail gap in physical world modeling: performance degrades from Regular to Unconventional and Impossible scenarios, indicating limited generalization beyond common interactions. Failure analysis further shows that models rely on superficial visual patterns: image models fail to realize correct state changes, while video models further suffer from temporal inconsistencies.

</details>


### [51] [3DCarGen: Scalable 3D Car Generation via 3D-consistent Multi-view Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24257)
*Hongli Xiao,Youjian Zhang,Yaohui Jin,Xiaoguang Ren,Wenjing Yang,Long Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 3DCarGen提出了一种从单张真实世界图像生成高质量3D汽车模型的可扩展框架。它首先从固定视角合成多视图图像，通过前馈重建模型得到基于3D高斯泼溅的粗糙表示，然后利用该显式3D先验引导多视图扩散模型生成任意视角的3D一致图像，最后通过颜色-法线联合优化的快速网格重建算法恢复细节丰富的3D车辆模型。实验表明该方法在几何一致性和重建保真度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量3D车辆资产对自动驾驶仿真至关重要，但现有基于多视图扩散的单图像重建方法视点有限且存在跨视图几何不一致问题，导致真实场景重建保真度低。

Method: 1) 从单张输入图像合成固定视点图像集；2) 通过前馈重建模型得到基于3D高斯泼溅的粗粒度3D表示；3) 以显式3D先验为条件，多视图扩散模型生成任意视角的3D一致图像；4) 扩展快速网格重建算法，结合颜色-法线联合优化，从密集合成视点中恢复细节一致的3D车辆模型。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，3DCarGen相比现有方法实现了更强的几何一致性和重建保真度。

Conclusion: 3DCarGen通过可扩展的单视图3D汽车生成框架，有效解决了多视图扩散方法中视点有限和几何不一致的问题，为自动驾驶仿真提供了高质量3D车辆资产生成方案。

Abstract: High-quality 3D vehicle assets are essential for autonomous driving simulation. Although multi-view diffusion-based paradigms enable controllable single-image reconstruction, they typically produce limited viewpoints and exhibit cross-view geometric inconsistencies, thereby reducing reconstruction fidelity in real-world scenarios. In this work, we introduce 3DCarGen, a scalable single-view 3D car generation framework designed for real-world images by synthesizing an arbitrary number of 3D-consistent multi-view images. Specifically, given a single image as input, we first synthesize a set of images from fixed viewpoints. These images are then fed into a feed-forward reconstruction model, resulting in a coarse 3D representation based on 3D Gaussian Splatting. Conditioned on this explicit 3D prior, our multi-view diffusion model generates 3D-consistent images from arbitrary camera viewpoints. We further extend a fast mesh reconstruction algorithm by incorporating color-normal joint optimization to recover detailed and coherent 3D vehicle models from the synthesized dense views. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves robust geometric consistency and reconstruction fidelity compared to existing methods. Code and models will be released.

</details>

*Clément Dauvilliers,Claire Monteleoni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个能够处理多源异构、时间不规则、地理偏移的卫星数据的生成模型，用于热带气旋微波图像的空时插值。通过自监督训练（随机掩码并重构源），结合红外与微波数据，模型在CRPS指标上优于监督学习，功率谱更接近真实观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波卫星图像对监测热带气旋降水和强度至关重要，但重访时间长，可能遗漏风暴快速演化阶段；且来自不同仪器的微波数据高度异构（时间间隔不规则、地理不对齐、仪器特性不同），缺乏有效的插值方法。

Method: 提出首个生成模型，适用于跨样本变化、时间间隔不规则、地理错位、仪器特性多样的多源地理空间数据。采用自监督训练任务：随机掩码一个源并重建；结合红外和微波数据进行空时插值。

Result: 自监督训练相比监督训练显著降低CRPS；结合红外与微波数据优于仅用微波数据；生成模型的集成均值与确定性模型相当，但功率谱更接近真实观测。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了利用多微波仪器和红外观测在时间不规则情况下对气旋微波图像的生成式插值，为异构遥感数据融合提供了新范式。

Abstract: Microwave satellite imagery plays a crucial role in monitoring tropical cyclone precipitation and intensity worldwide, but suffers from long revisit times, potentially missing rapid storm evolution phases. While this raises the need for an interpolation method, it is made challenging by the high level of heterogeneity of microwave data coming from different instruments. In this work, we introduce the first generative model that can be applied to multiple geospatial sources that change across samples, occur at irregular time intervals, are misaligned geographically, and come from instruments with varying characteristics. We apply this model to the case of spatio-temporal interpolation of tropical cyclone microwave images from other microwave and infrared instruments. We train using a self-supervised task in which a random source is masked and reconstructed, and show that it leads to a significant decrease in Continuous Ranked Probability Score over supervised training. We show a further improvement by combining infrared and microwave data compared to microwave only. Using these improvements, the generative model produces an ensemble mean on par with that of a deterministic model, while generating a power spectrum significantly closer to that of true observations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first generative model that interpolates microwave images of cyclones by combining multiple microwave instruments and infrared observations at irregular time intervals.

</details>


### [53] [UniRED: Unified RGB-D Video Frame Interpolation with Event Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24282)
*Yinuo Zhang,Guangshun Wei,Yuanfeng Zhou,Yiran Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的多模态框架，融合RGB外观、深度几何和事件相机时间线索，通过双向光流估计和运动基元细化实现高帧率RGB-D视频插值，并构建了新的RGB-D-Event数据集来缓解三模态训练数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-D相机受限于低帧率，难以捕捉快速场景动态；已有的RGB视频插值方法在RGB-D场景中会产生模糊边界、可见伪影和几何一致性退化问题；仅依赖两帧边界的运动估计在复杂动态场景中本质上是欠约束的。

Method: 提出一个统一的多模态RGB-D视频插值框架：1）提取并融合RGB、深度和事件线索；2）通过运动基元细化估计RGB双向光流，通过Z轴细化估计深度流；3）通过双向warping和软混合合成目标RGB-D帧；同时构建了新的RGB-D-Event数据集。

Result: 在公开基准和自建数据集上的实验表明，该方法在RGB插值上实现了更优的光度保真度，在深度插值上实现了更强的几何精度，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 融合事件相机提供的超高频时间线索能够有效解决RGB-D视频插值中运动估计欠约束的问题，同时提升RGB和深度插值的质量。

Abstract: High frame-rate RGB-D videos are crucial for a variety of downstream tasks, including motion analysis, dynamic scene understanding, and 3D reconstruction. However, due to hardware and sensing constraints, practical RGB-D cameras are typically limited to low frame rates, making it difficult to capture rapid scene dynamics. Existing video interpolation methods have achieved strong performance on RGB data, but they are not readily applicable to RGB-D scenarios, where they often yield blurry boundaries, visible artifacts, and degraded geometric consistency. Furthermore, motion estimation from only two boundary frames is inherently under-constrained in complex dynamic scenes. Event cameras, by contrast, provide asynchronous measurements with ultra-high temporal resolution, offering dense motion cues. In this paper, we propose a unified multimodal framework for RGB-D video interpolation that jointly exploits RGB appearance, depth geometry, and event-based temporal cues. Specifically, it first extracts and fuses RGB, depth and event cues, then estimates bidirectional flow with motion basis refinement for RGB and Z-axial refinement for depth, and finally synthesizes the target RGB-D frame via bidirectional warping and soft blending. In addition, we construct a new RGB-D-Event dataset to alleviate the scarcity of tri-modal training data. Extensive experiments on a public benchmark and the proposed dataset demonstrate that our method achieves superior photometric fidelity for RGB interpolation and stronger geometric accuracy for depth interpolation than existing approaches.

</details>


### [54] [ActiveScope: Actively Seeking and Correcting Perception for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24292)
*Yajing Wang,Chao Bi,Junshu Sun,Shufan Shen,Zhaobo Qi,Shuhui Wang,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ActiveScope，一种无需训练即可增强多模态大语言模型在高分辨率图像中细粒度感知能力的框架，通过语义锚点定位（SAL）和干扰抑制精化（ISR）两个模块，分别解决语义偏差和上下文主导问题，在多个高分辨率图像理解基准上达到SOTA性能（如V* Bench 96.34%准确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在细粒度高分辨率图像感知中表现不佳，而现有的训练无关方法通常依赖注意力定位或粗到细搜索，容易受到干扰物误导，且在多个目标定位时失败。作者将其归因于两个问题：上下文主导（Contextual Dominance，显著干扰物盖过目标注意力导致定位不准确）和语义偏差（Semantic Bias，全局语义使模型聚焦于最显著概念，导致多目标场景中定位不完整）。

Method: 提出ActiveScope，包含两个模块：（1）语义锚点定位（SAL）：利用细粒度语义锚点独立定位关键目标，缓解语义偏差；（2）干扰抑制精化（ISR）：通过抑制对显著干扰物的注意力来优化定位，克服上下文主导。

Result: 在高分辨率图像理解基准上，ActiveScope超越现有训练无关方法，例如在V* Bench上达到96.34%准确率。

Conclusion: 主动搜索与自我纠正范式在提升MLLM细粒度感知方面具有显著优势，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive vision-language understanding, yet still struggle with fine-grained perception in high-resolution images. While existing training-free methods typically rely on attention-based localization or coarse-to-fine search, they are often misled by distractors and fail to locate multiple targets. Our investigation attributes these failures to Contextual Dominance, where salient distractors overwhelm target attention and cause inaccurate localization, and Semantic Bias, where global semantics cause the model to fixate on the most salient concept, resulting in incomplete localization in multi-object scenarios. Built on these insights, we propose ActiveScope, a training-free framework that enhances MLLMs by actively seeking and correcting perception. ActiveScope features two modules. The Semantic Anchor Localization (SAL) utilizes fine-grained semantic anchors to independently localize key targets, thereby mitigating semantic bias. The Interference-Suppressed Refinement (ISR) refines localization by suppressing attention on salient distractions to overcome contextual dominance. Extensive experiments on high-resolution image understanding benchmarks demonstrate that ActiveScope outperforms existing training-free methods (e.g., 96.34 percent accuracy on $V^{*}$ Bench), validating the superiority of the active search and self-correction paradigm. Our code is available at https://github.com/jasmine-ww/ActiveScope.

</details>


### [55] [Hierarchical Spatial and Channel Aggregation for Cross-domain Few-shot Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24296)
*Sujun Sun,Mingwu Ren,Haofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 跨域小样本分割任务中现有方法仅关注风格差异带来的特征分布偏移，忽略了不同域间类别语义粒度和判别属性的显著差异，导致支持-查询匹配中的语义过对齐和属性过对齐。本文提出双层次聚合网络（DHANet），包含层次空间聚合（HSA）模块缓解语义过对齐、层次通道聚合（HCA）模块缓解属性过对齐，以及在线概率语义库（OPSB）在推理时构建类别概率分布并采样伪原型补充支持信息。在四个目标域数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CD-FSS方法主要集中在缓解由风格差距引起的特征分布偏移，但忽略了不同域之间类别语义粒度和判别属性的显著差异，导致支持-查询匹配中出现语义过对齐和属性过对齐两种关键退化问题。

Method: 提出DHANet，包含三个核心模块：(1) 层次空间聚合(HSA)模块——沿空间维度进行多尺度区域聚合，生成层次化语义增强特征以缓解语义过对齐；(2) 层次通道聚合(HCA)模块——沿通道维度进行多尺度属性聚合，生成层次化属性增强特征以缓解属性过对齐；(3) 在线概率语义库(OPSB)——推理时从查询预测中逐步构建和更新类别概率分布，采样多个伪原型作为额外支持信息以缓解支持样本不足问题。

Result: 在四个目标域数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过双层次聚合（空间维度的语义增强和通道维度的属性增强）以及在线概率语义库提供的伪原型补充，有效解决了跨域小样本分割中两类过对齐问题，显著提升了模型在目标域上的泛化分割能力。

Abstract: Cross-domain Few-shot Segmentation (CD-FSS) aims to learn generalizable segmentation capability from abundant annotated samples in the source domain, enabling accurate segmentation of novel classes in the target domain with only a few annotated samples. Existing CD-FSS methods mainly focus on mitigating feature distribution shifts caused by style gaps while ignoring significant differences in class semantic granularity and discriminative attributes across domains, leading to two key degradations in support-query matching: semantic over-alignment and attribute over-alignment. To this end, we propose the Dual Hierarchical Aggregation Network (DHANet), which comprises three key modules. First, the Hierarchical Spatial Aggregation (HSA) module performs multi-scale region aggregation of pixel features along the spatial dimension, generating hierarchical semantic-enhanced features to alleviate semantic over-alignment. Additionally, the HCA module conducts multi-scale attribute aggregation along the channel dimension, generating hierarchical attribute-enhanced features to mitigate attribute over-alignment. Finally, we propose the Online Probabilistic Semantic Bank (OPSB), which progressively constructs and updates class probability distributions from query predictions during inference, and samples multiple pseudo-prototypes as additional support information to mitigate insufficient support. Extensive experiments on four target-domain datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [56] [Training-free Cross-domain Few-shot Segmentation via Robust Semantic Representation and Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24297)
*Sujun Sun,Mingwu Ren,Haofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的训练无关框架，基于自监督视觉编码器DINOv3，通过语义感知特征重融合（SAFR）、自适应支持增强（ASE）和混合原型匹配（HPM）三个模块，在跨域小样本分割任务中实现了SOTA性能，避免了训练带来的计算开销和过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域小样本分割方法依赖训练或微调过程，计算成本高且易过拟合；引入通用视觉基础模型后性能提升有限甚至因过拟合而下降。作者希望消除可训练参数，避免训练开销和过拟合风险。

Method: 提出一个基于自监督视觉编码器DINOv3的训练无关框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 语义感知特征重融合（SAFR），识别并重新融合强调语义模式的特征，增强语义判别性；(2) 自适应支持增强（ASE），通过鲁棒的查询信息聚合缩小支持集与查询集之间的语义差距；(3) 混合原型匹配（HPM），集成来自不同原型的匹配结果以适应跨域语义复杂度变化。

Result: 在四个目标域数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在无需任何训练的情况下达到了跨域小样本分割任务的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 消除可训练参数的训练无关框架有效避免了训练开销和过拟合问题，基于DINOv3自监督编码器结合三个设计模块能够显著提升跨域小样本分割的性能。

Abstract: Cross-domain Few-shot Segmentation (CD-FSS) aims to transfer knowledge learned from source domain to distinct target domains, segmenting unseen target classes with only a few annotated samples. Although existing methods have made significant progress, they still rely on training or fine-tuning processes, which incur high computational costs and risk overfitting. We observe that when powerful and general-purpose vision foundation models are incorporated into these methods, their performance shows only marginal improvement or even degrades due to overfitting. To address this, we eliminate trainable parameters and propose a training-free framework to avoid both training overhead and overfitting. Built upon the self-supervised vision encoder DINOv3, our framework addresses cross-domain challenges through three core modules. First, the Semantic-aware Feature Re-fusion (SAFR) module identifies and re-fuses features that emphasize semantic patterns, generating representations with enhanced semantic discriminability. Additionally, the Adaptive Support Enhancement (ASE) module narrows semantic gaps between support and query through robust query information aggregation. Finally, the Hybrid Prototype Matching (HPM) module integrates matching results from diverse prototypes to adapt to varying semantic complexity across domains. Extensive experiments on four target domain datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in CD-FSS without any training.

</details>


### [57] [MM-TRELLIS: Point-Cloud Guided Multi-Modal 3D Vehicle Generation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24301)
*Hongli Xiao,Youjian Zhang,Yucai Bai,Chaoyue Wang,Yaohui Jin,Xiaoguang Ren,Wenjing Yang,Long Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MM-TRELLIS提出了一种多模态原生3D生成方法，融合多视图图像与LiDAR点云数据，用于从自动驾驶场景中生成高质量、几何一致的3D车辆模型，在Waymo数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆生成方法未能充分利用多模态传感器数据（多视图图像和LiDAR点云），且依赖神经渲染重建导致网格质量低；原生3D生成模型虽取得进展，但无法处理任意多视图输入和在野驾驶图像。

Method: 1) 多视图图像作为条件输入循环送入原生3D生成模型；2) LiDAR点云在测试时提供几何精度和跨视图一致性的引导；3) 去噪过程中先对齐引导点云与模型先验，再约束生成几何与引导点云一致；4) 基于3D高斯泼溅的不透明度提出体素滤波策略，抑制漂浮物并生成干净网格。

Result: 在Waymo数据集上的综合实验表明，MM-TRELLIS在高保真3D车辆生成方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了多模态传感器融合能够有效提升原生3D生成模型在自动驾驶场景中的车辆生成质量，实现几何精确且视觉一致的3D重建。

Abstract: Recovering realistic 3D vehicle models from autonomous driving scenes is crucial for synthesizing training data and building simulation environment. However, most existing vehicle generation methods fail to fully exploit multimodal sensors i.e. multi-view images and LiDAR point clouds) and rely on neural rendering based reconstruction, leading to low-quality mesh. Recently, native 3D generative models have made significant progress, yet they are not built for arbitrary multi-view inputs and often struggle with in-the-wild driving images. In this work, we present MM-TRELLIS, a multi-modal version of TRELLIS for in-the-wild 3D vehicle generation that integrates LiDAR and image sensors from autonomous driving datasets into native 3D generative models. Specifically, multi-view images are cycled as conditioning inputs, while LiDAR point clouds provide test-time guidance to ensure geometric accuracy and cross-view consistency. During denoising, we first align the guidance point cloud with the model priors, then enforce consistency between the generated geometry and the guidance point cloud. Finally, we introduce a voxel filtering strategy based on the opacity of 3D Gaussian Splatting to suppress floaters and produce clean meshes. Comprehensive experiments on Waymo dataset demonstrate our method outperforms existing methods in high-fidelity 3D vehicle generation. Code is available at https://github.com/HongliXiao/MM-TRELLIS.

</details>


### [58] [TrOCR for Medieval HTR: A Systematic Ablation Study with Cross-Dataset Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24302)
*Sachin Sharma,Michele Flammini,Federico Simonetta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了三种微调策略（对比度归一化、数据增强、层冻结）对TrOCR模型适应中世纪手写文本识别的影响，在13世纪意大利手稿上达到8.03% CER，发现浅层冻结无害、深层冻结有害，CLAHE可被优化替代，解码器冻结阈值跨数据集更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的HTR模型（如TrOCR）在现代化文本上预训练，迁移到中世纪手写本文本时存在显著的视觉域差异，微调策略的选择对识别精度影响大但缺乏系统研究。

Method: 在13世纪意大利手稿I-CT 91 "Cortonese"和公开READ-16基准上，通过控制实验网格，系统评估对比度归一化（CLAHE）、数据增强、层冻结深度三种可调微调策略对CER的影响，并利用Grad-CAM和交叉注意力图进行错误模式诊断。

Result: 最佳配置在Cortonese上达8.03% CER；冻结至多3层编码器或6层解码器不显著损害精度，更深冻结则逐渐有害；去除CLAHE后CER为7.84%，与专用基线相当；解码器冻结阈值跨数据集更鲁棒，而联合冻结需重新验证。

Conclusion: 浅层冻结是安全的，CLAHE等预处理可通过优化替代，解码器冻结策略具有更好的迁移性，微调策略需根据数据集特性进行验证。

Abstract: Fine-tuning transformer-based handwritten text recognition (HTR) models on medieval manuscripts is challenging because these models are pre-trained on modern text and must adapt to a very different visual domain. This paper studies how three controllable fine-tuning choices (contrast normalization, data augmentation, and layer freezing) affect recognition accuracy when adapting TrOCR to small historical datasets. We run controlled experiments on a 13th-century Italian manuscript (I-CT 91 "Cortonese") and replicate the same experimental grid on the public READ-16 benchmark as robustness evidence. On Cortonese, our best configuration achieves 8.03% character error rate (CER). Statistical comparisons across 13 configurations show that freezing up to three encoder layers or six decoder layers does not significantly harm accuracy, while deeper freezing becomes progressively detrimental. Removing contrast normalization (CLAHE) yields 7.84% CER, comparable to a domain-specialized baseline, suggesting strong optimization can reduce reliance on image preprocessing. Cross-dataset validation on READ-16 shows that decoder freezing thresholds transfer more robustly than encoder thresholds, and combined freezing strategies require dataset-specific re-validation. Finally, we use Grad-CAM gradient attributions and decoder cross-attention maps to diagnose error patterns and failure modes revealed by the ablations. Source code is available at https://github.com/LaudareProject/TrOCR-analysis

</details>


### [59] [REDI-Match: Rotation-Equivariant Distillation for Efficient and Robust Dense Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24330)
*Yinji Ge,Guixu Zheng,Wulong Guo,Qian Feng,Xu Wu,Kai Zhou,Xinyuan Liu,Fei Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REDI-Match提出了一种旋转等变蒸馏（REDI）范式，通过将大视觉基础模型的非等变语义表示蒸馏到轻量级的严格旋转等变编码器中，并配备熵驱动的空间对齐模块，在多个基准上实现了新SOTA，尤其在SatAst数据集上位姿精度提升13.89%，速度比RoMa v2快1.9倍（~41 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VFM在处理严重平面内旋转时面临两难困境：数据驱动方法需要低效的参数扩展来隐式学习旋转，而严格等变网络缺乏现代VFM的语义能力。当前框架通常冻结VFM，将旋转泛化的全部负担转嫁给下游解码器，导致架构瓶颈。

Method: 提出REDI-Match框架，核心包括：(1) REDI范式：将VFM的非等变语义表示蒸馏到轻量级严格旋转等变编码器中，利用等变几何架构约束高维语义；(2) 熵驱动的空间对齐模块：通过评估离散旋转假设，显式锁定规范坐标系，消除全局歧义后再进行连续精细化对齐。

Result: 在多个基准上达到新SOTA，SatAst数据集上位姿精度绝对提升13.89%，推理速度比RoMa v2快1.9倍，在单张RTX 4090上实现~41 FPS的实时推理。

Conclusion: REDI-Match通过旋转等变蒸馏范式有效解耦了旋转泛化与语义表示，打破了VFM在密集特征匹配中处理严重平面内旋转的架构瓶颈，实现了精度与速度的双重提升。

Abstract: Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) have significantly advanced dense feature matching, yet severe in-plane rotation remains a critical challenge. Existing solutions face a fundamental dilemma: data-driven methods require inefficient parameter scaling to implicitly learn rotations, whereas strictly equivariant networks lack the semantic capacity of modern VFMs. Consequently, current frameworks typically freeze VFMs and shift the entire burden of rotation generalization to the downstream decoder. To break this architectural bottleneck, we propose REDI-Match, an efficient framework driven by a novel Rotation-Equivariant Distillation (REDI) paradigm. Instead of relying on rotation data augmentation to establish rotational correspondences, REDI distills the non-equivariant semantic representations of a VFM into a lightweight, strictly rotation-equivariant encoder, leveraging an equivariant geometric architecture to constrain robust high-dimensional semantics. To fully exploit these features, we equip the decoder with an entropy-driven spatial alignment module. By evaluating discrete rotation hypotheses, this mechanism explicitly locks onto the canonical coordinate system, eliminating global ambiguity before continuous refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that REDI-Match establishes a new state-of-the-art (SOTA) across multiple benchmarks. Notably, it achieves a 13.89% absolute pose accuracy improvement on the highly challenging SatAst dataset while operating 1.9x faster than the current SOTA (RoMa v2), enabling real-time inference (~41 FPS) on a single RTX 4090 GPU. Code: https://github.com/YinjiGe/REDI-Match.

</details>


### [60] [UniTranslator: A Unified Multi-modal Framework for End-to-end In-Image Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24333)
*Jiahao Lyu,Pei Fu,Zhenhang Li,Shaojie Zhang,Jiahui Yang,Yu Zhou,Can Ma,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniTranslator是一个统一的图像内机器翻译（IIMT）框架，通过引入理解-生成对齐模块（UGAM）和空间掩码解码器（SMD），解决了多模态模型中理解与生成冲突以及空间位置不对齐的问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统一多模态模型直接应用于图像内机器翻译时存在两大问题：一是理解与生成冲突（翻译结果与文本监督不一致），二是空间位置不对齐（渲染文本与目标文本区域不匹配）。

Method: 提出UniTranslator框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 理解-生成对齐模块（UGAM），用于弥合理解与生成之间的表征差距，促进翻译内容预测与文本渲染的语义一致性；(2) 空间掩码解码器（SMD），通过在文本区域上施加像素级监督，改善生成时的空间定位、几何对齐和布局可控性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，UniTranslator在多种语言方向和复杂真实世界布局场景中均取得了最优性能，并揭示了翻译理解与图像生成之间存在强烈的相互增强效应。

Conclusion: 统一多模态学习在图像内机器翻译任务中具有显著优势，理解与生成可以相互促进；代码已开源。

Abstract: In-Image Machine Translation (IIMT) aims to translate scene text in an image and render the translated text back into the original regions while preserving the overall visual appearance. Recent unified multimodal models provide a promising solution by combining visual-text understanding and image generation within a single framework. However, directly adapting such models to IIMT remains challenging. In particular, they often suffer from understanding-generation conflicts, where the translation inferred during understanding is inconsistent with the text supervision used in generation, and spatial position misalignment, where the rendered text does not accurately match the target text regions. To address these issues, we present UniTranslator, a unified multimodal framework for IIMT that tightly couples translation understanding and text editing. Specifically, we introduce an Understand-Generation Alignment Module (UGAM) to bridge the representation gap between understanding and generation, encouraging semantic consistency between translated content prediction and text rendering. We further propose a Spatial Mask Decoder (SMD) with pixel-level supervision over text regions to improve spatial grounding, geometric alignment, and layout controllability during generation. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that UniTranslator achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse language directions and complex real-world layouts. Moreover, our results reveal a strong mutual reinforcement effect between translation understanding and image generation, highlighting the advantage of unified translation multimodal learning. Code is available at https://github.com/SeerRay-Lab/Unitranslator.

</details>


### [61] [Ill-Posed by Design: Probing Evidence Use in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24335)
*Boaz Meivar,Shaked Perek,Shani Shvartzman,Eli Schwartz,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将单目物体尺寸估计作为评估视觉语言模型证据选择的病态诊断任务，通过反事实分析拆解6个证据通道，发现即使最大规模模型也落后于纯文本LLM，且模型未能有效利用场景几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反事实分析在良态任务（多个线索独立支持同一答案）中诊断价值有限，需要更有效的诊断设置来评估VLM的证据选择能力。

Method: 构建Metric VQA数据集（10,813个Objectron维度查询+331个野外真实测量场景），对12个开源VLM进行反事实分析，拆解6个视觉和语言证据通道（目标类别先验、目标外观、局部上下文、图像表观尺寸、场景几何、语言提示）。

Result: 最大VLM（Qwen3-VL-235B等）在野外场景中落后于纯文本LLM；目标身份是最关键线索，目标像素和局部上下文仅对部分模型有帮助，表观尺寸影响预测但缺乏方向性读取，场景几何信息基本未被利用；LoRA微调可学习任务但未学会利用几何信息。

Conclusion: 单目尺寸估计作为病态任务能有效诊断VLM的证据选择缺陷，当前模型在整合场景几何等复杂线索方面存在根本性不足。

Abstract: Counterfactual analysis is widely used to study evidence use in vision-language models, but its diagnostic value is limited on well-posed tasks: when several cues independently support the same answer, removing one may not change the prediction. We propose monocular metric object-size estimation as an ill-posed diagnostic setting for evidence selection: because physical size cannot be determined from a single uncalibrated image, models must rely on imperfect cues category priors, target appearance, local context, apparent image size, and scene geometry. We assemble Metric VQA ($10{,}813$ dimension queries from Objectron and $331$ tape-measured in-the-wild scenes) and evaluate $12$ open-weight VLMs ($3$--$397$\,B parameters) with counterfactual analysis decomposing six visual and language evidence channels. Even the largest VLMs tested (Qwen3-VL-235B, Qwen3.5-397B, InternVL3.5-241B) trail a text-only frontier LLM on the in-the-wild split. The diagnostic analysis shows: target identity is the most load-bearing cue, target pixels and local context help only some models, apparent size shifts predictions without a directional readout, and global scene geometry is largely unused. We analyze LoRA fine-tuning as an actionable intervention specific to metric estimation: while the task is learnable, the models do not learn to leverage scene geometry.

</details>


### [62] [TIGER: Taming Identity, Geometry, and Generative Priors for High-Quality Face Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24336)
*Yang Zhou,Wenxue Li,Peng Zhang,Yifei Chen,Fei Wang,Daiguo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TIGER提出了一种三先验融合框架（身份先验、几何先验、生成先验），通过解耦3D几何引导和单步整流流生成，配合三阶段渐进训练策略，实现了高性能的人脸视频修复，在身份保真度和时间稳定性上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部视频修复方法难以同时解决身份偏移、视角纠缠的引导以及感知真实性三个关键挑战。

Method: 提出TIGER框架：(1) 身份先验：将主体判别性嵌入注入潜在空间以锚定身份；(2) 几何先验：将2D参考线索提升到解耦的3D参数空间，通过跨源参数融合构建几何锚点；(3) 生成先验：利用视频生成模型通过单步整流流实现高效生成；(4) 三阶段渐进训练策略：逐步优化结构保真度、纹理重建和分布级真实性。

Result: 在身份保真度和时间稳定性上达到SOTA性能，提供了高质量、高效且身份一致的面部视频修复效果。

Conclusion: TIGER通过结构化三先验融合框架有效解决了FVR中的身份偏移、视角纠缠和感知真实性问题，实现了高效且身份一致的高质量面部视频修复。

Abstract: Face Video Restoration (FVR) aims to recover high-fidelity facial videos from degraded input while preserving identity and semantic consistency across frames. Existing methods often struggle to simultaneously address three key challenges: identity shift, viewpoint-entangled guidance, and perceptual realism. To tackle these issues, we propose TIGER, a structured tri-prior fusion framework that Tames Identity, Geometry, and gEnerative pRiors for high-quality FVR. Specifically, an Identity Prior is first established by injecting subject-discriminative embeddings into the latent space, effectively anchoring the subject's identity against severe degradations. Then, to provide temporally consistent structural guidance for dynamic videos, TIGER constructs a Geometry Prior by lifting 2D reference cues into a disentangled 3D parameter space, creating a geometric anchor through cross-source parameter fusion. Moreover, to achieve maximum efficiency without compromising realism, we harness the video generation model's Generative Prior through a one-step rectified flow. We further design a progressive three-stage training optimization strategy that refines structural fidelity, textural reconstruction, and distribution-level realism to ensure robust optimization. We also construct a large-scale FVR dataset to facilitate robust training and standardized evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TIGER achieves state-of-the-art performance in both identity fidelity and temporal stability, delivering a high-quality, efficient and identity-consistent FVR. Project page: https://yzhoulv.github.io/Tiger/.

</details>


### [63] [Open-Vocabulary BEV Segmentation with 3D-Aware Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24353)
*Hojun Choi,Seulbin Hwang,Dae Jung Kim,Kisung Kim,Hyunjung Shim,Jinhan Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OVBEVSeg，一种基于几何感知的开放词汇BEV分割框架，通过高斯溅射逆投影和三维几何约束（伪标签、联合优化、几何蒸馏），在nuScenes数据集上实现了对新类别的开放集识别，性能超越闭集方法15.3 mIoU，推理速度提升2.5倍，显存仅为投影方法的0.22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BEV感知方法局限于闭集场景，无法应对真实世界中不可预测的开放类别；而将视觉-语言模型（VLM）的2D语义提升到BEV空间时存在严重的3D几何不一致问题。

Method: 提出OVBEVSeg框架，包含三个渐进阶段：（1）基于可靠3D投影的2D-to-BEV伪标签生成，用于开放词汇泛化；（2）联合2D-BEV逐场景优化，加入BEV结构约束保证3D几何一致性；（3）3D几何蒸馏，实现在线推理效率。核心采用高效的高斯溅射（Gaussian Splatting）逆投影机制。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，未见类别上超出闭集方法15.3 mIoU；无需新类标注即可与使用40%标注的自监督/半监督基线竞争；推理速度提升2.5倍，显存仅为投影方法的0.22倍。

Conclusion: OVBEVSeg首次实现了高效且几何一致的开放词汇BEV分割，为BEV感知从闭集走向开放集提供了可行路径，同时保持了实时性和低资源消耗。

Abstract: Bird's-eye view (BEV) perception fuses multi-camera images into a unified top-down representation for autonomous driving. Despite recent progress, state-of-the-art methods remain confined to closed-set scenarios, making them vulnerable to unpredictable real-world environments. In this work, we introduce open-vocabulary BEV segmentation (OVBS), which leverages vision-language models (VLMs) to recognize categories beyond the training set while maintaining precise BEV perception and real-time efficiency. A key challenge in OVBS lies in the 3D geometric inconsistency inherent in the ill-posed lifting of 2D VLM semantics into BEV. To address this, we propose OVBEVSeg, a geometry-aware OVBS framework that enhances efficient Gaussian splatting (GS)-based unprojection by leveraging robust 3D geometric constraints across three progressive stages: (1) 2D-to-BEV pseudo-labeling via reliable 3D projection for OV generalization; (2) joint 2D-BEV per-scene optimization with BEV structural constraints for 3D geometric consistency; and (3) 3D geometric distillation for online efficiency. On the nuScenes dataset, OVBEVSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming closed-set methods by 15.3 mIoU on unseen categories. Remarkably, even with no novel-class ground-truth labels, it remains competitive with self- and semi-supervised baselines trained with up to 40% of ground-truth annotations. Furthermore, it achieves 2.5x faster inference with only 0.22x the memory consumption of projection-based methods. Project page: https://hchoi256.github.io/projects/ovbevseg/.

</details>


### [64] [SignNet-1M: Large-Scale Multilingual Sign Language Video Dataset with Downstream Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24361)
*Zhewen He,Junyi Hu,Haomian Huang,Zhenhua Li,Yu-Shen Liu,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignNet-1M是一个大规模增强手语数据集，覆盖ASL、CSL和DGS三种语言，通过3D高斯泼溅合成新视角、扩散模型进行场景/身份编辑以及后渲染增强，显著提升了手语模型在真实世界分布偏移下的鲁棒性，同时保持强分布内性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语模型通常在受约束条件下训练，缺乏视角、背景和手语者身份多样性，导致在真实世界分布偏移下鲁棒性差。

Method: 通过三个轴合成现实变化：(i) 基于3D高斯泼溅的新视角渲染（旋转和缩放）；(ii) 基于扩散模型的场景/身份编辑（背景替换和手语者替换，同时保留手部运动和语言内容）；(iii) 后渲染增强（模拟捕捉和压缩伪影，如姿态/时间扰动和视频级损坏）。

Result: 跨骨干网络的实验表明，使用SignNet-1M训练在跨视角、跨背景、跨身份和后渲染偏移下持续提升泛化能力，同时保持强分布内性能。

Conclusion: 提供数据集、完整增强流程和基准套件，证明合成数据增强是提升手语模型鲁棒性的有效策略。

Abstract: Sign language models are typically trained on datasets captured under constrained conditions, with limited viewpoint, background, and signer-identity diversity, leading to poor robustness under real-world distribution shifts. We introduce SignNet-1M, a large-scale augmented dataset spanning ASL, CSL, and German Sign Language (DGS). SignNet-1M synthesizes realistic variations along three axes: (i) novel-view rendering (rotation and zoom) via 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), (ii) scene/identity editing via diffusion models for background replacement and signer substitution while preserving sign motion and linguistic content, and (iii) post-rendering augmentations that emulate capture and compression artifacts (e.g., pose/temporal perturbations and video-level corruptions) to better match in-the-wild recordings. Beyond data release, we provide a unified benchmark suite across downstream tasks (e.g., translation and recognition) and ablations that isolate each augmentation component. Experiments across backbones show that training with SignNet-1M consistently improves generalization under cross-view, cross-background, cross-identity, and post-rendering shifts, while maintaining strong in-distribution performance. The dataset, full augmentation pipeline, and benchmark are available at https://signnet.chatsign.ai/.

</details>


### [65] [Structural Kolmogorov-Arnold Convolutions: Learnable Function on the Values or the Filter Shape as Parameter-Efficient Alternative to Per-Edge Convolutional KANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24371)
*Stefano Mereu,Oleksandr Kuznetsov,Gabriele Marchello,Alessandro Galdelli,Emanuele Frontoni,Adriano Mancini,Ferdinando Cannella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将卷积KAN中的可学习函数从每个核条目转移到卷积结构上，设计了三种变体（SV-KAN、AG-KAN、RF-KAN），其中RF-KAN和SV-KAN在CIFAR-10/100上以约0.4M参数达到最优性能，远优于传统卷积和逐边KAN；研究表明学习到的滤波器形状是关键成分，去除后准确率下降超40个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卷积KAN将可学习函数附着在每个核条目上，导致参数量大且易过拟合。作者认为可学习函数更适合放在卷积结构层面而非每条边上，以此减少参数、提升效率。

Method: 沿单一轴组织设计空间：函数作用于像素值或滤波器形状。提出三种实现：SV-KAN（共享值函数+静态空间滤波器）、AG-KAN（共享值函数+内容自适应高斯门控）、RF-KAN（可学习函数放在滤波器形状上，用定向脊轮廓和局部振荡Morlet小波基构建滤波器）。

Result: RF-KAN和SV-KAN在CIFAR-10上达88.47±0.10%和88.20±0.31%，CIFAR-100上达64.40±0.19%和64.57±0.30%，约0.4M参数；性能优于普通卷积和各类逐边KAN（包括Gram变体），参数量约为其五分之一。

Conclusion: 在小规模参数下，形状模型（RF-KAN）和最简值模型（SV-KAN）表现最优；RF-KAN的增益源于局部振荡小波基和内容自适应性；去除学习到的形状后准确率下降超40点，证明学习到的形状是该尺度下的关键成分。

Abstract: Convolutional Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace the fixed weights of a convolutional kernel with learnable univariate functions. The dominant formulation attaches one such function to every kernel entry and lets it act on pixel values, expressive but parameter-heavy and prone to overfitting. We argue that the learnable functions are better placed in the \emph{structure} of the convolution than on each edge, and we organise the design space along a single axis: whether the function acts on the pixel \emph{values} or on the filter \emph{shape}. We study three realisations. SV-KAN applies one shared univariate function to the values and leaves the spatial filter free and static, aa classical convolution with a single learnable shared activation. AG-KAN keeps the shared value function but supplies the spatial structure through a content-adaptive Gaussian gate. RF-KAN instead moves the learnable functions onto the filter shape, building each filter from oriented ridge profiles expanded in a localised oscillatory (Morlet) wavelet basis with content-adaptive amplitudes. Under a matched four-layer protocol with in-run references and three seeds, RF-KAN and SV-KAN reach $88.47\pm0.10\%$ and $88.20\pm0.31\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $64.40\pm0.19\%$ and $64.57\pm0.30\%$ on CIFAR-100, at about $0.4$M parameters. At this matched scale the shape model and the simplest value model meet at the top, both above a plain convolution and every per-edge KAN we tested, including the official Gram variant, at roughly a fifth of the parameters. A controlled study attributes the RF-KAN gain to an intrinsically localised oscillatory basis and to content adaptivity, and an ablation that removes the learned shape entirely, leaving only the shared value function, collapses accuracy by over forty points, identifying the learned shape as the load-bearing ingredient at this scale.

</details>


### [66] [MATCH: Flow Matching for Multi-View Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24375)
*Mathis Kruse,Melissa Schween,Bodo Rosenhahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MATCH是一种基于Flow Matching的多视角异常检测方法，通过ODE公式估计似然度来检测异常，在Real-IAD和MANTA-Tiny数据集上达到SOTA性能，且能在消费级硬件上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂工业对象需要从多个视角分析来检测异常，现有方法在多视角异常检测领域仍有改进空间。

Method: 基于Flow Matching的ODE公式估计似然度，利用FM模型的架构灵活性将不同空间尺寸的特征转换到正态分布，并通过省略昂贵的散度项确保实时性。

Result: 在Real-IAD和MANTA-Tiny数据集上，异常检测和分割均达到SOTA性能，在消费级硬件上可实时运行。

Conclusion: MATCH是首个基于Flow Matching的多视角异常检测方法，兼顾性能与效率，适用于实时生产场景。

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in industrial objects is an important topic for increasing production efficiency. More complex objects often require the analysis of several view points, which has led to the field of multi-view anomaly detection. We present MATCH, the first multi-view anomaly detection method based on Flow Matching (FM). With the ODE formulation of Flow Matching, we can estimate likelihoods and thereby derive an anomaly score to detect anomalies in multi-view image data at object, image, and pixel-level. The architectural flexibility of FM models allows us to efficiently transform features of different spatial sizes to the normal distribution. We evaluate thoroughly on the already established Real-IAD data set and are also the first to provide a comprehensive evaluation of popular anomaly detection methods for the MANTA-Tiny data set. MATCH achieves state-of-the-art performance in both anomaly detection and segmentation, all while running on consumer-level hardware. By omitting the costly divergence term needed for likelihood estimation, we ensure that MATCH is usable in real-time production scenarios. Lastly, several ablation studies are conducted to validate the methodological choices.

</details>


### [67] [Modality-Aware Out-of-Distribution Detection for Multi-Modal Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24404)
*Lars Doorenbos,Duc Manh Vu,Serdar Ozsoy,Juergen Gall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态与单模态预测之间存在有趣关系，据此提出了一种适用于多模态场景的检测器，该检测器结合模态间信号与特征空间分数，经归一化后形成混合检测器，在MultiOOD基准上平均性能优于现有方法，强调了推理时显式考虑不同模态对多模态OOD检测的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态信息能提升动作识别模型的性能，但它如何增强模型鲁棒性（尤其是多模态分布外检测）仍未被充分探索。现有方法虽在训练时考虑OOD检测，但推理时仍使用为单模态设计的现成检测器，丢弃了重要信息。

Method: 首先发现多模态与单模态预测之间的有趣关系，基于此信号构建专为多模态场景设计的后验检测器；再结合特征空间分数（检测多模态空间中的离流形样本），并通过多模态logits进行归一化，形成混合检测器。

Result: 在MultiOOD基准的多个数据集上，该方法平均性能优于现有最先进方法，且与现有训练时方法兼容，能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 推理时显式考虑不同模态对多模态OOD检测至关重要，所提混合检测器有效结合了模态间信号与特征空间信息，为多模态OOD检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: The incorporation of additional modalities into action recognition models increases their performance across a wide range of settings. However, how this additional information can contribute to making the models more robust remains underexplored, particularly for the case of multi-modal out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. While methods exist that regularize the multi-modal training process with OOD detection in mind, they still apply off-the-shelf OOD detectors designed for the uni-modal case during inference, discarding important information. Based on an interesting relationship we find between the multi-modal and uni-modal predictions, we propose to use this signal to build a post-hoc detector explicitly designed for the multi-modal scenario. We combine this new source of information with a feature-space score, which detects off-manifold samples in the multi-modal space, and normalize them by the multi-modal logits. In doing so, the proposed hybrid detector is compatible with existing training-time approaches and consistently improves performance. Experiments on a wide range of established datasets from the MultiOOD benchmark show that, on average, our approach outperforms the state of the art. Our results show the importance of explicitly considering the different modalities at inference time for multi-modal OOD detection.

</details>


### [68] [EgoSAT: A Comprehensive Benchmark of Egocentric Streaming Interaction Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24422)
*Yijia Lei,Jinzhao Li,Yichi Zhang,Jiacheng Hua,Yin Li,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoSAT，首个面向流式场景的自我中心视频推理基准，包含1,997个视频和4,800个问答对，评估VLM在回顾、在线和前瞻推理中的能力，发现现有模型存在严重的"自信错误"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心视频理解基准缺乏对流式交互场景的建模，无法评估模型在帧序列动态到达时对过去、现在和未来事件的统一推理能力。

Method: 构建EgoSAT基准，统一回顾（已发生事件）、在线（正在发生事件）和前瞻（未来动作）三类推理任务于同一流式框架，设计4,800个高质量问答对，区分模型的可回答性并诊断其置信度校准情况。

Result: 现有开源和闭源VLM在回顾和前瞻推理上均表现不佳，且存在严重的置信度误校准——模型信心无法跟踪问题的内在可回答性，产生大量"自信但错误"的行为。

Conclusion: 流式自我中心视频理解仍是一个重大挑战，现有VLM在时序推理和置信度校准方面存在显著不足，亟需进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce EgoSAT, the first comprehensive benchmark for egocentric video reasoning in streaming settings, designed to evaluate the capabilities of modern vision-language models (VLMs). The benchmark targets streaming interaction understanding, where video frames arrive sequentially and models must continuously interpret evolving visual context. EgoSAT unifies several previously distinct tasks within a single streaming framework. In this formulation, queries about completed events correspond to retrospective reasoning, queries about ongoing activities require online understanding, and queries about future actions involve prospective anticipation. This unified setting requires models to reason about the past, present, and future while operating under the constraint that only previously observed frames are available. EgoSAT contains 1,997 unique videos spanning 165 hours of egocentric footage and around 4,800 high-quality question-answer pairs, carefully designed to probe reasoning across varying temporal contexts. Using this benchmark, we evaluate a diverse set of both open-weight and closed-weight VLMs, providing a systematic assessment of their ability for streaming interaction understanding. By distinguishing answerability and conducting diagnostics on confidence of models, we find existing models not only struggle with prospective and retrospective modeling, but also exhibit severe mis-calibration: confidence often fails to track inherent answerability, leading to dangerous "confidently wrong" behaviors. Project page: https://leiyj23.github.io/EgoSAT/

</details>


### [69] [Transformation Behavior of Images in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24430)
*Christian Zöllner,Mozzam Motiwala,Aysel Ahadova,Gerrit Anders,Robert Hüneburg,Jacob Nattermann,Matthias Kloor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同编码器网络（通用和病理专用）对组织病理学图像经过经典变换后潜在空间表征的鲁棒性。发现变换后的嵌入向量虽比随机嵌入更接近原始图像，但并非完全不变，且通用与病理专用网络存在显著差异，解释了数据增强的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究经典图像变换对组织病理学编码器网络潜在空间的影响，评估编码器对标签无关变换的不变性程度，理解为何变换增强仍能提升下游任务性能。

Method: 使用Lunit Inc.、Bioptimus（病理专用）和Meta（通用）提供的编码器网络，对H&E染色切片图像（来自结直肠组织数据集和TCGA）施加标准数据变换，通过比较原始与变换后图像的嵌入向量距离以及随机嵌入距离来评估方差。

Result: 原始与变换后图像的嵌入向量距离显著小于随机嵌入距离，表明具有一定鲁棒性，但未达到完全不变性；通用编码器与病理专用编码器之间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 现有编码器网络无法完全消除变换效应，这解释了变换增强方法仍能提升性能的原因；不同预训练范式（通用vs病理专用）的网络在变换鲁棒性上存在本质差异。

Abstract: Training of neural networks for histopathology classification tasks typically relies on data encoding into latent space, which reduces complexity and improves performance. There are several encoder networks available, either pretrained on general image datasets such as ImageNET, or specifically on histopathological images. Training of encoder networks should be adapted to downstream tasks, allowing encoding of biologic/diagnostic content while rendering networks invariant to label-irrelevant transformations.
  This paper investigates the effect of classical image transformation on the latent space, using networks provided by Lunit Inc. and Bioptimus, both focusing on pathological images, and by Meta Research Team. We assess variance of embeddings resulting from standard data transformations by comparing original and transformed image embeddings and by contrasting them with random, unrelated embeddings, using image tiles from hematoxylin/eosin-stained sections available in a colorectal tissue dataset and the publicly accessible TCGA dataset.
  Our findings show that embeddings of original and transformed images are closer to each other than to random embeddings, indicating robustness to transformations. However, they are not fully invariant, revealing that the encoder networks do not completely neutralize transformation effects in latent space, explaining why transformation-mediated augmentation of datasets can improve performance. Significant differences were observed between general and histopathology-specific encoder networks.

</details>


### [70] [MedPCFM: Improving Medical Point Cloud Completion by Integrating Point Transformers and Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24433)
*Kamil Kwarciak,Marek Wodzinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PCFM，一种基于PTv3骨干网络和流匹配（Flow Matching）的连续时间生成模型，用于医学点云补全。在SkullFix、SkullBreak和下颌骨缺损数据集上，PCFM在生成性能上达到SOTA，且采样步数远少于扩散模型，推理速度最高提升7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学点云补全对解剖结构重建和临床工作流至关重要，但该领域的生成建模研究尚不充分。

Method: 采用连续时间生成建模（流匹配Flow Matching），以PTv3为骨干网络；同时构建了确定性编码器-解码器基线（PTv3）和扩散补全基线（PCDiff，含PVCNN和PTv3两种去噪器）进行对比。

Result: PCFM（PTv3）与确定性PTv3基线性能相当，在所有数据集上生成性能达SOTA；采样步数远少于扩散模型；最佳操作点时PTv3带来明显吞吐量提升，PCFM相比PVCNN骨干加速最高7倍；增大模型规模和点分辨率可带来持续性能增益。

Conclusion: 流匹配方法在医学点云补全中兼具高生成质量和高效推理的优势，PTv3作为骨干网络相比PVCNN有显著速度优势。

Abstract: Medical point cloud completion is important for anatomical reconstruction and downstream clinical workflows, yet generative modeling in this setting remains insufficiently studied. We investigate completion through continuous-time generative modeling and introduce PCFM, a PTv3-backed flow matching approach for medical point cloud completion. We evaluate on SkullFix and SkullBreak, and additionally on the more recent Mandibular Defect dataset. We build strong baselines by adapting PTv3 to a deterministic encoder-decoder completion model and by instantiating diffusion completion (PCDiff) with both PVCNN and PTv3 denoisers. PCFM with PTv3 is competitive with the deterministic PTv3 baseline and achieves state-of-the-art generative performance across datasets, while requiring substantially fewer sampling steps than diffusion. At the best operating points, PTv3 also yields clear throughput gains, providing up to a 7$\times$ speed-up for PCFM compared to a PVCNN backbone. Finally, we study empirical scaling trends by varying model size and point cardinality, showing consistent gains with higher point resolution and informative trade-offs across model scales.

</details>


### [71] [S1-Omni-Image: A Unified Model for Scientific Image Understanding, Generation, and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24441)
*Qingxiao Li,Zikai Wang,Qingli Wang,Nan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S1-Omni-Image是一个面向科学图像理解、生成和编辑的统一多模态模型，采用"思考-再生成"范式，在科学推理骨干S1-VL-32B上耦合图像生成模块，构建了314K样本的训练数据集SciGenEdit，在多项科学图像生成和编辑基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用图像生成模型难以满足科学图像任务对科学语义、结构关系、领域知识和任务意图的理解需求，亟需一个统一框架来同时支持科学图像的理解、生成与编辑。

Method: 基于科学多模态推理骨干S1-VL-32B，采用think-before-generate范式，先产生任务导向的推理轨迹、文本答案和任务特殊token，再将其隐含状态注入生成模块以条件化图像生成或编辑。统一框架支持科学图像理解、生成（逻辑图、关系对比、数据图表、科学可视化）和编辑（多轮插图编辑、医学/地理图像分割、医学图像翻译、超分辨率）。

Result: 在GenExam和TechImage-Bench上优于开源模型；在MSD、cigRockSEM、SynthRAD2025、IXI四个编辑基准上达到SOTA；在科学图像理解评估上保持稳定性能。

Conclusion: S1-Omni-Image显著提升了科学图像生成和编辑能力，同时保留了继承自S1-VL-32B的科学图像理解能力，为科学领域提供统一的多模态解决方案。

Abstract: We present S1-Omni-Image, an open-weight unified multimodal model for scientific image understanding, generation, and editing.
