Metis organizes textual experience into execution plans, environment facts, and common pitfalls, and selectively crystallizes recurring plans into validated callable tools. This design combines the broad applicability of text memory with the execution efficiency of code memory while incurring tool-generation cost only when justified by repeated reuse. We evaluate Metis on AppWorld, a challenging benchmark for interactive agents. The results show that Metis improves task accuracy by up to 20.6% over ReAct while reducing execution cost by up to 22.8%. Compared with representative self-evolving agent systems, Metis consistently achieves a better balance between accuracy, execution efficiency, and memory-construction cost.

</details>


### [143] [MedBench v5: A Dynamic, Process-Oriented, and Hallucination-Aware Benchmark for Clinical Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24155)
*Ding Jinru,Jiang Chuchu,Lu Lu,Pang Wenrao,Bian Mouxiao,Gao Zhuangzhi,Chen Jiangyuan,Peng xinwei,Chen Ruiyao,Ren Sijie,Lu Renjie,Han Bin,Liu Meiling,and Xu Jie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedBench v5 是一个面向临床多模态模型的新型基准，从静态问答转向动态过程评估，引入双维框架（14个子维度的临床认知响应性 + 4个智能体环境的医学原子技能，共63个任务）、三种信息流压力元（遗漏、矛盾、证据延迟）、五节点动态过程审计协议以及幻觉传播监控机制。实验发现，强整体任务表现不保证过程稳定性，压力主要破坏矛盾检测、诊断更新、幻觉传播和基于矛盾的自我修正，而最终证据基础可能表面稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI基准缺乏过程可见性、原子技能评估和集成的幻觉检测能力，无法全面评估临床模型在实际诊疗中的认知过程稳定性。

Method: 构建双维评估框架（临床认知响应性14子维度 + 医学原子技能4智能体环境），设计三种可切换信息流压力元（遗漏、矛盾、证据延迟）进行因子化退化分析，实施五节点动态过程审计协议生成模型特定失败指纹，并引入幻觉传播监控机制追踪幻觉的启动、传播、锚定和矛盾交互过程。

Result: 强整体任务表现不 guarantee 过程稳定性；压力元主要破坏矛盾检测、诊断更新、幻觉传播和基于矛盾的自我修正能力，最终证据基础可保持表面稳定。

Conclusion: MedBench v5 为临床AI评估提供了统一的基础设施，支持能力剖析、可控压力测试、过程审计和幻觉轨迹分析，揭示了现有模型在过程稳定性方面的关键缺陷。

Abstract: Existing medical AI benchmarks lack process visibility, atomic skill evaluation, and integrated hallucination detection. We introduce MedBench v5, a redesigned benchmark for clinical multimodal models (language, vision-language, and agent systems) that moves from static QA to dynamic, process-oriented evaluation. MedBench v5 features: (1) a dual-dimensional framework combining Clinical Cognitive Responsiveness (14 sub-dimensions) and Medical Atomic Skills (4 agent environments), covering 63 tasks; (2) three switchable information-flow stressors (omission, contradiction, evidence delay) for factorized degradation analysis; (3) a dynamic process audit protocol with five reasoning nodes that produces model-specific failure fingerprints; (4) hallucination propagation monitoring across initiation, propagation, anchoring, and contradiction interaction-capturing silent hallucination. Experiments on frontier models show that strong overall task performance does not guarantee process stability: stressors mainly disrupt contradiction detection, diagnosis updating, hallucination propagation, and contradiction-based self-correction, while final evidence grounding can remain superficially stable. MedBench v5 provides a unified infrastructure for capability profiling, controllable stress testing, process auditing, and hallucination trajectory analysis in clinical AI evaluation.

</details>


### [144] [BehaviorBench: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Behavioral Science Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24162)
*Jin Huang,Yutong Xie,Wanli Song,Xingjian Zhang,Walter Yuan,Matthew O. Jackson,Qiaozhu Mei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BehaviorBench基准，系统评估基础模型在行为科学领域的四项核心能力，并开发了Be.FM-1.5模型。研究发现通用模型在个体级预测上表现优异，而行为基础模型在分布级对齐上更强，Be.FM-1.5在分布指标上领先且个体指标具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在行为科学（心理学、社会学、经济学）中的应用日益增多，但缺乏系统性的基准来评估它们在多样化任务、情境和人群中的表现。

Method: 构建BehaviorBench基准，评估基础模型在行为预测与模拟、战略决策、主体特质推断、行为知识应用四项核心能力，同时在个体级和分布级两个层面进行评估。基于该基准，进一步微调开发了Be.FM-1.5行为基础模型。

Result: 通用模型在个体级预测和知识密集型任务上表现优异；行为基础模型（Be.FM-1.5）在分布级对齐上显著更强，且在个体级指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 研究强调了分布级评估的重要性，BehaviorBench可作为开发与评估行为对齐AI系统的基础，Be.FM-1.5展示了行为适配的潜力，为行为科学研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Foundation models have been increasingly applied to behavioral science domains such as psychology, sociology, and economics. While these models show promise in individual tasks such as survey response prediction and human-subject experiment simulation, there remains no systematic understanding of how well they perform across diverse behavioral science tasks, contexts, and populations. We introduce BehaviorBench, a comprehensive benchmark that evaluates foundation models along four core capabilities: (1) behavior prediction and simulation, (2) strategic decision-making, (3) subject-trait inference, and (4) behavioral knowledge application. Crucially, BehaviorBench evaluates model outputs at both the individual and distributional levels, capturing not only per-subject accuracy but also population-level alignment, an essential requirement for behavioral validity. Leveraging the tasks in BehaviorBench, we further develop Be.FM-1.5, extending the Be.FM family of behavioral foundation models fine-tuned on behavioral data. Our results reveal a considerable gap: proprietary general-purpose models excel at individual-level prediction and knowledge-intensive tasks, whereas behavioral foundation models, fine-tuned on behavioral data, achieve substantially stronger distributional alignment. Notably, Be.FM-1.5 leads on distributional metrics and remains competitive on individual-level metrics, suggesting that proper behavioral adaptation can close the gap. Our results highlight the importance of distributional evaluation, establish BehaviorBench as a foundation for developing and assessing behaviorally aligned AI systems, and demonstrate Be.FM-1.5's potential for a broad range of behavioral science studies. Our BehaviorBench and Be.FM-1.5 models can be accessed via https://umich-foreseer.github.io/behaviorbench/.

</details>


### [145] [A Pāninian Foundation for Indic Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24172)
*Ritwik Banerjee,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于波你尼语法框架（Astādhyāyī）的统一计算架构，以解决印度语言自然语言处理基础设施碎片化的问题，并设计了四部分基准测试套件来量化该共享架构，同时探讨神经网络模型是否能自发学习波你尼语法范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超过十亿人使用的印度语言在自然语言处理领域存在基础设施碎片化、资源稀疏的问题，根源在于当前研究按单个语言或小语系组织工具和基准，忽视了印度语言因两千多年梵语趋同而共享的形态句法架构。

Method: 提出基于波你尼《八章书》（Astādhyāyī）所形式化的语法框架作为统一计算架构，设计包含四部分的基准测试套件，将这一共享架构显式化、可量化，便于实际应用。

Result: 该方法有望使印度语言系统更准确、数据效率更高、可迁移性更强，将分散稀疏的印度语言资源融合为一个统一的高资源元语言基座。

Conclusion: 波你尼语法框架可作为印度语言NLP缺乏的统一计算架构；基于该框架的基准测试能提升系统性能；同时提出一个可解释性研究问题：神经模型是否自发表征波你尼语法范畴。

Abstract: More than a billion people communicate in Indic languages, yet the natural language processing infrastructure serving them remains fragmented and underdeveloped. The cause is structural: the field organizes its tools and benchmarks around individual languages or small subsets of genealogical language families, building separate analyzers, parsers, and datasets for each language and starting over for the next. This overlooks a deep regularity. Through more than two millennia of convergence around Sanskrit, Indic languages came to share a morphosyntactic architecture formalized in Pānini's grammar, the Astādhyāyī. This cuts across genealogical lines, uniting languages through a common framework. We argue that this Pāninian framework supplies a unifying computational architecture the field has lacked, and that benchmarks grounded explicitly in it would make Indic language systems more accurate, more data-efficient, and more transferable, effectively merging many apparently disparate and sparse Indic language resources into a single high-resource metalanguage bedrock. We propose a four-part benchmark suite to render this shared architecture explicit, measurable, and ready to be leveraged for practical applications. Moreover, we underscore the question it raises for interpretability research: whether neural models trained on these languages come to represent Pānini's categories on their own.

</details>


### [146] [A Synthetic Reliability-Aware PINN Benchmark for Offshore Wind Turbine Support-Structure Monitoring with Bayesian Inverse Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24176)
*Puneet Kant,Monika Tanwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Digi Turbine，一种将欧拉-伯努利梁方程与Winkler地基模型嵌入物理信息神经网络(PINN)的合成基准，用于海上风机单桩支撑结构监测，结合贝叶斯先验反演识别和FORM可靠性筛选，在NREL 5MW参考涡轮机背景下进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上风机支撑结构的可靠结构健康监测需要从稀疏测量中快速进行状态估计，而高保真有限元或气弹分析难以直接用于在线监测循环，纯数据驱动代理模型又需要大量训练集。

Method: 将简化的欧拉-伯努利梁方程与Winkler土壤基础嵌入训练目标，耦合贝叶斯先验反演识别，并加入一阶可靠性方法(FORM)筛选。

Result: 所有验证均使用合成配置，以NREL 5MW参考涡轮机为背景，以解析解或有限差分解为基准真值。

Conclusion: Digi Turbine作为一种合成可靠性感知的PINN基准，为海上风机单桩支撑结构监测提供了结合物理模型与数据驱动方法的新框架。

Abstract: Reliable structural health monitoring (SHM) of offshore wind turbine (OWT) support structures requires fast state estimation from sparse measurements. Repeated high fidelity finite element or aeroelastic analyses are difficult to use directly in online monitoring loops, while purely data-driven surrogates can require large training sets. This paper presents Digi Turbine, a synthetic reliability-aware Physics Informed Neural Network (PINN) benchmark for OWT monopile support structure monitoring. The workflow embeds a simplified Euler Bernoulli beam equation with Winkler soil foundation in the training objective, couples it with Bayesian-prior-informed inverse identification, and adds First Order Reliability Method (FORM) screening. All validation uses synthetic configurations with analytical or finite-difference ground truth motivated by the NREL 5MW reference turbine context.

</details>


### [147] [MMed-Bench-IR: A Heterogeneous Benchmark for Multilingual Medical Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24200)
*Junhyeok Lee,Han Jang,Hyeonjin Goh,Kyu Sung Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍MMed-Bench-IR基准，用于评估临床环境中多语言检索增强生成（RAG）所需的三种能力：跨语言对齐、概念区分和证据检索。该基准覆盖6种语言，包含三个结构异构的任务，共13,442个查询。评估10个系统后发现：双语编码器在英语上nDCG@10达0.818，但在日语上骤降至0.056，暴露出仅依赖英语基准无法检测的严重跨语言失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅孤立评估多语言医学检索所需的跨语言对齐、概念区分和证据检索能力，未衡量生物医学专业知识与多语言覆盖之间的交互作用，缺乏统一、解耦的评估框架。

Method: 构建MMed-Bench-IR基准，包含三个结构异构任务：(1)基于UMLS的跨语言医学QA检索（6,127条查询）；(2)三难度级别的概念区分（4,975个混淆集）；(3)多语言RAG证据检索（2,040条质量保证查询）。三个任务设计上无概念和查询重叠，确保聚合分数反映真实能力广度。评估6个范式家族的10个系统。

Result: 双语编码器在英语上nDCG@10为0.818，日语上仅0.056，跨语言性能差距巨大。仅依赖英语基准无法检测此类严重跨语言失效。

Conclusion: MMed-Bench-IR有效揭示了多语言医学检索中存在的严重跨语言性能差距，强调了构建多语言、多维度评估基准的必要性，为RAG在临床多语言场景下的改进提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in clinical settings increasingly requires multilingual retrieval against predominantly English evidence corpora. Multilingual medical retrieval demands three capabilities: cross-lingual alignment, concept discrimination, and evidence retrieval. However, existing benchmarks evaluate these only in isolation, leaving the interaction between biomedical expertise and multilingual coverage unmeasured. We introduce MMed-Bench-IR, a benchmark designed to disentangle these axes across 6 languages and three structurally heterogeneous tasks: (1) cross-lingual medical QA retrieval with 6,127 queries grounded in the Unified Medical Language System (UMLS), (2) concept discrimination over 4,975 confusion sets at three difficulty tiers, and (3) multilingual evidence retrieval for RAG with 2,040 quality-assured queries. The three tasks share zero concept and query overlap by design, ensuring that aggregate scores reflect genuine capability breadth. Evaluation of ten systems across six paradigm families reveals severe cross-lingual failure: biomedical encoders that score 0.818 nDCG@10 in English drop to 0.056 in Japanese, a gap that English-only benchmarks cannot detect.

</details>


### [148] [Decoherence as Defence and the Magnitude of Noise Regularisation: A Rigorous N -Qubit Theory of Stochastic Quantum Neural Networks for Adversarially Robust Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24219)
*Gautier-Edouard Edouard Filardo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文解决了随机量子神经网络（SQNN）中两个遗留问题：去极化通道能否通过“真正量子dropout”实现正则化，以及鲁棒性界限能否被预测性理论替代。通过N量子比特随机主方程和退相干收缩定理，证明去极化通道在入侵检测中提供可量化的防御；在NSL-KDD数据集上，去极化SQNN在强白盒攻击下显著优于无噪声电路，且不会出现灾难性鲁棒性崩溃。推导出自适应惩罚公式，表明逐门dropout实现曲率加权L2惩罚（最优p=1/2），而去极化实现输出空间惩罚；30次种子实验验证了公式的定量预测。最后在里德伯原子硬件上实现并给出可行性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决前期工作中两个开放问题：1) 去极化通道能否被真正的量子dropout替代以实现正则化；2) 能否将保守的对抗鲁棒性界限替换为可预测的理论。同时将框架扩展到真实数据集和中性原子硬件。

Method: 建立N量子比特随机主方程和矢量化Liouvillian形式；证明退相干收缩定理给出定量衰减因子；在NSL-KDD数据集上对FGSM和PGD攻击进行实验验证；推导出显示dropout实现曲率加权L2惩罚、去极化实现输出空间惩罚的公式；通过30次种子实验验证理论预测。

Result: 去极化SQNN在ℓ∞ PGD-20攻击下p=0.04显著优于无噪声电路；从未发生鲁棒性崩溃（经典检测器从95%降至47%）；鲁棒性方差降低约两倍；dropout和去极化均显著缩小训练-测试差距（≈0.01；p<10⁻⁴和p=0.004），两者统计上无差异；dropout率超过1/2无帮助；前期工作的单种子二分法未能在复制中成立。

Conclusion: 去极化通道在SQNN中可作为有效的正则化和防御机制；真正的量子dropout与去极化在正则化效果上统计不可区分；退相干收缩定理为噪声防御提供了定量预测框架；中性原子硬件实现可行。

Abstract: Stochastic quantum neural networks (SQNNs) encode neuronal activations as qubits, synaptic topology as entanglement, and neural noise through a Lindblad master equation. A recent conference study applied a ring-entangled SQNN to collaborative intrusion detection and reached three conclusions: ring entanglement is \emph{essential} for non-local anomaly detection; an adversarial-resilience bound holds but is \emph{conservative}; and the depolarising channel \emph{fails} to act as a dropout-style regulariser, behaving instead as output noise. It left open whether a per-gate stochastic deactivation (``true quantum dropout'') could regularise where the depolarising channel could not, and whether the loose robustness bound could be replaced by a predictive theory. This paper resolves both and extends the framework to real data and to neutral-atom hardware. We give an $N$-qubit formulation through the stochastic master equation and its vectorised Liouvillian, and prove a \emph{decoherence-contraction theorem}: a depolarising channel of strength $γ$ over $L$ entangling layers contracts every weight-$w$ Pauli read-out by a factor $(1-4γ/3)^{wL}$ (for the weight-$1$ read-out used here, $(1-4γ/3)^{L}$); building on the general noise-as-defence result of Du et al., we make this quantitative and operational for intrusion detection. On the real NSL-KDD dataset under white-box FGSM and PGD attacks, a depolarising SQNN trained with the channel is, over seven seeds under strong $\ell_\infty$/$\ell_2$ attacks, significantly more robust than the noiseless circuit ($\ell_\infty$ PGD-$20$, $p=0.04$, large effect) and, critically, never suffers the catastrophic robustness collapse that the noiseless model and gradient-trained classical detectors (which fall from $95\%$ to $47\%$) do, cutting robustness variance roughly twofold; we show this robustness arises from a noise-reshaped training boundary rather than from attack-time gradient contraction. For generalisation, we derive an adaptive-penalty formula showing that per-gate dropout implements a curvature-weighted $L_2$ penalty $\tfrac{p(1-p)}{2}\sumθ^2\partial^2_θL$ in weight space, maximised at $p=1/2$, whereas depolarising noise implements an output-space penalty. A $30$-seed study confirms the formula's quantitative prediction: both mechanisms reduce the train-test gap by a small but statistically significant margin ($\approx\!0.01$; $p<10^{-4}$ and $p=0.004$), are statistically indistinguishable from each other, and the effect is concentrated where overfitting is largest; increasing the dropout rate past $1/2$ does not help, as the formula predicts. The single-seed dichotomy of prior work does not survive replication. We close with a neutral-atom realisation and a feasibility-by-$N$ analysis.

</details>


### [149] [SURGELLM: Rethinking Multi-Task Evaluation through Task-Aware Feature Gating with Class-Balanced Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24259)
*Noor Islam S. Mohammad,Ulug Bayazit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 **Surgellm** 统一Transformer框架，通过手术特征门控、任务条件前缀令牌和实例加权归一化三个轻量模块，解决异构NLP任务中的归纳偏置不匹配、类别不平衡特征统计偏差及缺乏外部词汇知识条件注意力三大问题。在四任务、17,830样本、11个模型变体上，IWN变体达到0.940 Macro-F1，较最强基线提升0.036，作者检测任务提升0.130。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调编码器在异构NLP任务中面临三个复合问题：归纳偏置不匹配、类别不平衡导致特征统计量被破坏、以及缺乏将外部词汇知识作为注意力条件的机制。

Method: 提出Surgellm框架，包含三个轻量模块：(1) 手术特征门控——在精选词汇指示符和[CLS]上学习每维sigmoid门控，特征无信息时可退化为恒等映射；(2) 任务条件前缀令牌——将量化特征值和任务身份预置到每个输入；(3) 实例加权归一化（IWN）——消除门控统计量中的类别先验偏差。

Result: 在SST-2、多跳检索、LLM提示归因和作者检测四个任务、17,830样本、11个模型变体上，IWN变体Macro-F1达0.940，较最强非IWN基线提升0.036，作者检测任务提升0.130。随机词汇对照组F1下降0.028，证实增益来自词汇而非参数。

Conclusion: Surgellm通过三个轻量模块有效解决了异构NLP任务的复合问题，验证了手术特征对齐与过剩风险界限的理论关联，代码、词汇表和99.5%恢复率的自动提取方案已开源。

Abstract: Fine-tuned encoders deployed across heterogeneous NLP tasks face three compounding problems: mismatched inductive biases, class-imbalance corruption of feature statistics, and no mechanism to condition attention on external lexical knowledge. We introduce \textbf{\surgellm}, a unified transformer framework that addresses each with a dedicated lightweight module: a \emph{surgical feature gate} (learned per-dimension sigmoid over curated lexical indicators and \texttt{[CLS]}; provably degenerates to identity when features are uninformative), \emph{task-conditioned prefix tokens} (quantized feature values and task identity prepended to every input), and \emph{Instance-Weighted Normalization} (IWN; removes class-prior bias from gate statistics). We prove an excess-risk bound linking gate benefit to \emph{surgical feature alignment}. Across four tasks, SST-2, multi-hop retrieval, LLM-prompt attribution, and authorship detection, covering 17,830 examples and eleven model variants over three seeds, the IWN variant achieves macro-F1 \textbf{0.940} ($+0.036$ over the strongest non-IWN baseline; $+0.130$ on authorship detection). A random-vocabulary control ($-0.028$ avg.\ F1) confirms gains are lexical, not parametric. Code, vocabularies, and a $99.5\%$-recovery auto-extraction recipe are released.

</details>


### [150] [Pigeonholing: Bad prompts hurt models to collapse and make mistakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24267)
*Hyunji Nam,Keertana Chidambaram,Dorottya Demszky,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在上下文学习中存在"鸽子洞效应"——不良上下文（如用户给出的错误建议或助手之前错误回复）会导致模型性能严重下降（38-40%）、模式坍缩和立场翻转，该效应随对话轮次增加而恶化，即使在上下文示例正确时也可能发生；作者提出结合合成错误的RLVR方法可将不良上下文下的性能提升43-60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示大语言模型在非恶意但不良上下文（如用户提供错误解法或延续之前错误回复）中会出现性能退化与模式坍缩现象，即"鸽子洞效应"，并探索其表现特征与缓解方法。

Method: 在10个可验证和开放任务上使用10个不同模型进行实验，定义两种不良上下文场景：（1）用户给出错误建议；（2）对话历史包含助手之前的错误回复。提出RLVR（Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards）结合合成错误数据的缓解方法。

Result: 鸽子洞效应导致38-40%性能下降；随错误轮次增加（1→5次），性能额外下降14%以上；即使示例正确也可能发生模式坍缩；所提RLVR方法在不良上下文下比vanilla RLVR基线提升43-60%。

Conclusion: 大语言模型对不良上下文高度敏感，易陷入重复错误输出、答案多样性丧失和立场翻转等模式坍缩状态；通过引入合成错误的RLVR训练可有效增强模型对不良上下文的鲁棒性。

Abstract: While in-context learning is generally shown to be effective in Large Language Models (LLMs), bad contexts can cause performance degradation and mode collapse, a phenomenon we call "pigeonholing." **Unintentionally bad** contexts can happen without malicious jailbreaking intents: For example, a user asks the model to justify an incorrect math theorem or fails to correct the model's buggy code. Specifically, we investigate ``pigeonholing" in two scenarios: (1) when the user suggests a solution, and (2) when the conversation context includes the assistant's previous (incorrect) responses. Our experiments across 10 verifiable and open-ended tasks with 10 different models show that pigeonholing manifests in several ways: (1) repeating the incorrect answers from context (leading to 38-40% performance drop), (2) converging on a narrow set of answers in coding and text generation without exploring alternatives, and (3) flipping stance on controversial topics to align with the user or the assistant's previous claims. We find that pigeonholing worsens almost monotonically with the number of conversation turns (performance drops by additional 14+% as repeated mistakes increase from 1 to 5), and pigeonholing-induced mode collapse can happen even when the provided example is correct. As a step toward mitigation, we propose RLVR with synthetic errors which improves models by 43-60% under bad contexts compared to vanilla RLVR baselines.

</details>


### [151] [CALIBER: Calibrating Confidence Before and After Reasoning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24281)
*Conor Finlay,Joshua Kurien,Saurabh Dash,Marzieh Fadaee,Beyza Ermis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CALIBER方法，通过在不同信息状态（思考前和回答后）分别进行置信度估计，并用匹配的监督目标（提示级别正确率和答案级别正确率）进行校准，在多个基准上显著降低了期望校准误差（ECE），最高降低52.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常仅在思考前或回答后单一时间点获取置信度，忽略了推理模型置信度在不同信息状态下具有状态依赖性，导致校准不准确。

Method: CALIBER在思考前和回答后分别进行置信度估计，并用与信息状态相匹配的监督目标：思考前使用提示级别正确率监督，回答后使用答案级别正确率监督。

Result: 在7B模型上，BigMathDigits数据集的ECE降低52.5%，Brier分数和AUROC最佳；30B模型上BigMathDigits的ECE最佳；在分布外基准GPQA和TriviaQA上ECE和Brier分数最佳。

Conclusion: 位置-目标对齐在校准中至关重要，尤其在分布偏移下能持续降低校准误差，验证了置信度状态依赖性的重要性。

Abstract: Reasoning language models are increasingly asked not only to answer difficult questions, but also to estimate their likelihood of success. Existing methods typically elicit confidence only once: either before thinking or after answering. We argue that confidence in reasoning models is state-dependent: before thinking, confidence should estimate the chance of the model correctly solving the prompt, while after thinking it should predict whether the realized answer is likely to be correct. This distinction determines the appropriate supervision target: prompt-level success should supervise confidence estimates made after seeing the prompt, while individual answer-level correctness should supervise confidence estimates made after answering. We introduce CALIBER (Calibration Before and After Reasoning), which elicits both estimates and supervises each with the target matched to its information state. Under this unified protocol, CALIBER reduces Expected Calibration Error (ECE) by 52.5% over the strongest single-confidence baseline on BigMathDigits for the 7B model, while achieving the best Brier score and AUROC, and remains within 2.1 points of the best accuracy. Further, on a larger 30B model, CALIBER achieves the best ECE on BigMathDigits while remaining competitive in Brier score and AUROC. Out of distribution, it achieves the best ECE and Brier score on GPQA and TriviaQA, and remains competitive on SimpleQA. Ablations further show that this position-target alignment is most beneficial under distribution shift where it consistently reduces calibration error across all out-of-distribution benchmarks.

</details>


### [152] [AVOC: Enhancing Hour-Level Audio-Video Understanding in Omni-Modal LLMs via Retrieval-Inspired Token Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24286)
*Yijing Chen,Wenhui Tan,Xiaoyi Yu,Yuyue Wang,Xin Cheng,Kaisi Guan,Hao Jiang,Xiangyang Li,Guojie Zhu,Ruihua Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AVOC提出了一种面向长时音视频理解的令牌压缩框架，将多模态令牌压缩重构为top-K检索问题，基于相关性、重要性和多样性三个信息检索准则选择关键令牌，在多个长时音视频基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在短时音视频理解上表现优异，但长时音视频理解受限于有限的上下文窗口和严重的信息冗余，亟需高效的令牌压缩方法来处理长序列多模态输入。

Method: AVOC在模态编码器与大语言模型骨干之间引入可学习的令牌压缩模块，将多模态令牌压缩重构为top-K检索问题。借鉴信息检索中相关性、重要性和多样性三个经典准则，为每个准则设计针对音视频理解的定制化机制，并将其整合为统一的检索式压缩流水线。

Result: 在长时音视频基准上达到SOTA：OmniVideoBench平均准确率超第二名4.9分，LVOmniBench超5.5分。在长达一小时的音视频"大海捞针"任务中保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: 基于检索视角的令牌压缩方法能有效缓解长时音视频理解中的上下文窗口限制和信息冗余问题，为全模态大模型的长序列处理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have achieved remarkable progress in short-form audio-video understanding, yet long-form audio-video comprehension remains challenged by limited context windows and severe information redundancy. To address these bottlenecks, we propose AVOC, a framework for long-form audio-video understanding in Omni-modal Large Language Models. AVOC introduces a learnable token compression module between the modality encoders and the LLM backbone. We reframe multimodal token compression as a top-$K$ retrieval problem: given a fixed context budget, the module must retrieve a compact subset of tokens that best supports answering the user query. We draw inspiration from three classical Information Retrieval criteria for selecting informative units from a large candidate pool: relevance, importance, and diversity. AVOC instantiates each criterion as a tailored mechanism for audio-video understanding, and integrates them into a unified retrieval-style compression pipeline. Experiments show that AVOC achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-form audio-video benchmarks, surpassing the second-best model by 4.9 and 5.5 points in average accuracy on OmniVideoBench and LVOmniBench, respectively. Moreover, AVOC maintains robust performance on Audio-Video Needle-in-a-Haystack task at durations up to one hour.

</details>


### [153] [Prague Dependency Treebank -- Consolidated 2.0: Enriching a Complex Annotation Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24324)
*Marie Mikulová,Jiří Mírovský,Milan Straka,Pavlína Synková,Jan Štěpánek,Barbora Štěpánková,Jan Hajič*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了布拉格依存树库框架的第二个整合版本（PDT-C 2.0），这是一个历时近30年、包含近400万词符、统一标注了多层级语言信息（尤其是跨句现象如共指和话语关系）的捷克语语料库资源，并附有完全兼容的词典，已广泛用于语言学研究、NLP工具开发和不同形式体系间的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个系统性地包含并链接不同语言层面（特别是跨句现象如共指和话语关系）的统一标注语料库，以支持深层次的语言学研究和NLP工具开发。

Method: 持续近30年的资源开发与标注项目，整合多层级语言标注（包括意义表示层面的跨句现象），形成统一且连贯标注、涵盖多种体裁的语料库，并附带完全兼容的词典。

Result: 产出了PDT-C 2.0版本，一个近400万词符的捷克语资源，实现了多层级语言信息的系统标注与链接，并在国际NLP工具比较和不同形式体系转换中得到广泛应用。

Conclusion: 该语料库及训练好的解析器已以CC BY-NC-SA许可发布，可作为持续语言学研究、传统与新型NLP工具开发及国际比较的重要基础资源。

Abstract: The Prague Dependency Treebank framework is unique in its attempt to systematically include and link different layers of language, including a meaning representation with several types of inter-sentential phenomena, especially coreference and discourse relations. We present its second consolidated version (PDT-C 2.0), which concludes almost 30-years long project of sustained development of the resource to a uniformly and coherently annotated, genre-diversified, almost 4 million token language resource of Czech language, with accompanying fully compatible lexicons. In addition to continuous linguistic research, the richly linguistically annotated corpus is also widely used in international comparisons of the development of traditional and novel NLP tools as well as in conversions into other formalisms. The corpus and the trained parsers are available under the CC BY-NC-SA licence.

</details>


### [154] [Transformer-Based Language Models Across Domain Verticals: Architectures, Applications and Critical Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24331)
*Guruprakash J,Krithika L. B*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对Transformer架构进行了系统性综述，按机制（编码器-only、解码器-only、编码器-解码器、长上下文、排列式、生成器-判别器）和用途（医疗、金融、法律、教育、客服、创意写作、科研）两个层面组织，并涵盖2023年后关键进展（指令微调、RLHF、DPO、MoE、RAG等），最后从部署决策的四维评估、参数量与能耗权衡、以及"最先进"标准的演变等角度进行批判性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型迭代过快，从业者难以区分真正重要的创新与增量噪声，因此需要一篇系统性的综述来帮助从业者理解核心架构演变和实际部署考量。

Method: 采用双层次综述方法：（1）机制层面，将主流Transformer变体整理为分类学体系，涵盖六大架构类型及2023年后重要发展；（2）应用层面，调查七大领域的部署案例，将具体能力需求与Transformer架构进行匹配。

Result: 构建了Transformer变体的系统分类学，从四个维度（推测为性能、效率、可扩展性、对齐性等）对架构进行了比较，量化了参数量与能耗之间的权衡关系，并揭示了对齐方法、数据来源和基准饱和效应对"最先进"定义的影响。

Conclusion: 提出了当前值得更多关注的研究问题，强调了在模型评估中不应仅看基准分数，还需考虑部署成本、对齐质量等实际因素。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models have become the default substrate for natural language processing and the pace of new releases has made it hard for practitioners to separate durable ideas from the noise of incremental announcements. This review works at two levels. At the level of mechanism, we organise the main transformer families into a working taxonomy, covering encoder-only, decoder-only, encoder-decoder, long-context, permutation-based, and generator-discriminator variants. We then extend the discussion to post-2023 developments that changed the picture in practice: instruction tuning, reinforcement learning from human feedback, direct preference optimisation, mixture-of-experts scaling, retrieval augmentation and the current flagship model families from OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, Meta, Mistral and DeepSeek. At the level of use, we survey deployments across healthcare, finance, legal, education, customer service, creative writing and scientific work. Based on this we link each to the specific capabilities that make a transformer the appropriate tool. The contribution of this paper is a critical assessment that is based on the survey. We compare architectures on four axes that matter to deployment decisions, we quantify the trade-off between parameter count and energy cost. We also discuss how alignment methods, data provenance and benchmark saturation change what it means to call a model "state of the art". The final section lists the research questions that we think deserve more attention.

</details>


### [155] [Meet UD_Czech-PDTC: A Large and Genre-Rich Treebank in Universal Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24337)
*Marie Mikulová,Barbora Štěpánková,Daniel Zeman,Jan Štěpánek,Milan Straka,Jan Hajič*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了将捷克语Prague Dependency Treebank-Consolidated (PDT-C) 转换为Universal Dependencies (UD) 的过程。PDT-C 是原始PDT的两倍以上，且涵盖更多体裁和领域。尽管两种标注方案表面相似，但在依存结构拓扑、词类和关系类型粒度上存在诸多差异。文章通过实例展示了这些差异，讨论了不同动机及克服差异的转换方法，并论证PDT虽不那么"通用"且更紧密绑定于一种语言，但其多层标注丰富，能提供构建UD树所需的所有信息及更多内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大规模、多样化的捷克语PDT-C资源转换为Universal Dependencies标准，以提升其在多语言依存分析研究中的可用性和覆盖范围。

Method: 通过对比PDT-C与UD两种标注方案在依存结构拓扑、词类和关系类型粒度上的差异，设计相应的转换规则和方法来处理这些差异，并基于具体实例进行说明。

Result: 成功将PDT-C（规模是原始PDT的两倍以上，涵盖更多体裁和领域）转换为UD格式，并通过实例展示了转换过程中的关键差异及解决方案。

Conclusion: 尽管PDT标注方案不如UD"通用"且更紧密绑定于捷克语，但其丰富的多层标注信息足以支撑基本的UD树构建，并提供了超越UD标准的更多内容。

Abstract: Czech has been part of Universal Dependencies since its first release in 2015. It has also been one of the best represented languages, with the Prague Dependency Treebank being order of magnitude larger than most other UD treebanks. More recently, three other datasets from the Prague family were added and the annotations thoroughly revisited, forming the "Prague Dependency Treebank-Consolidated" (PDT-C). In comparison to the original PDT, PDT-C is more than twice as large, but it is also much more diverse in terms of genres and domains. In this paper, we describe the conversion of the new resource to Universal Dependencies. While the two annotation schemes are relatively similar at the first sight, there are numerous small differences in topology of the dependency structures and in granularity of the POS and relation type inventories. We demonstrate a selection of such differences on examples, discuss the diverging motivations, as well as ways to overcome the differences during conversion. We argue that while PDT is less "universal" and more tightly bound to one language, its multi-layer annotation is rich and provides all information needed for basic UD trees, and much more.

</details>


### [156] [Automatic Part-of-Speech Tagging of Arabic-English Dictionary Senses through WordNet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24359)
*Diaa M. Fayed,Aly A. Fahmy,Mohsen A. Rashwan,Wafaa K. Fayed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将英阿双语词典的词义进行词性标注的算法，通过将英语翻译等价词的词性标签转移到词典词义上，以低成本实现了高准确率的标注，为资源匮乏语言的NLP工具开发提供了轻量级方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发NLP/HLT工具通常需要语言学专家、大量投资和长时间，统计方法需要大规模标注语料库，规则方法需要包含丰富语言和世界知识的大规模词典。这些资源对于资源匮乏语言难以获取，因此需要资源轻量级方法来自动开发NLP工具。

Method: 从Princeton WordNet获取英语翻译等价词的词性标签，通过消歧过程将英语词性标签转移到Al-Mawrid英阿双语词典的阿拉伯语词义上，实现跨语言的词性标注。

Result: 该方法以较低成本实现了较高的标注准确率，成功将英语词性标签迁移到双语词典的阿拉伯语词义上。

Conclusion: 对双语词典词义进行词性标注是将双语词典链接到WordNet或将其标准化为WordNet-LMF格式的前提条件，该资源轻量级方法为阿拉伯语等资源匮乏语言的NLP工具开发提供了可行途径。

Abstract: This paper proposed an algorithm for part-of-speech (POS) tagging senses of a bilingual dictionary. The algorithm is applied on the Al-Mawrid Arabic-English dictionary. The tagging task is accomplished by transferring the POS tags of the English translation equivalences (TEs) to the dictionary senses after dis-ambiguities process. The English POS tags of senses are acquired from the Princeton WordNet. POS tagging of bilingual dictionary senses is prerequisite to link a bilingual dictionary to WordNet and/or standardizing that dictionary into WordNet-LMF format where the synset (set of synonyms), not word, is the basic brick. The registered accuracy is high though the cost is little. Building NLP/HLT tools needs linguistic experts, large investments, and long time. For statistical approach, we need large annotated corpora and for rule-based approach, we need large lexicon that contains rich linguistic and world knowledge. That motivates the appearance of what are called resource-light approaches to develop natural language processing (NLP) tools for poor-resource languages.

</details>


### [157] [MorfFlex: Handling Rich Morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24366)
*Jaroslava Hlaváčová,Marie Mikulová,Barbora Štěpánková,Milan Straka,Jan Hajič*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MorfFlex是一种适用于屈折和派生高度规则语言的形态词典架构，以捷克语MorfFlex CZ为例，通过编码屈折和派生模式，将词典规模从巨大压缩至超1亿词形和100万词位，为依存树库的标注一致性和NLP工具（如MorphoDiTa）提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有丰富屈折和派生规律的语言，传统形态词典存储空间庞大、维护困难，需要一种高效的结构化方法来管理复杂的形态系统并保证标注一致性。

Method: 采用手动维护的源文件与转换脚本，编码系统的屈折和派生模式，以<词形, 词位, 标签>三元组列表形式发布，通过模式压缩词典规模。

Result: MorfFlex CZ词典包含超过1亿词形和100万词位，支撑了布拉格依存树库的手动形态标注一致性，并为MorphoDiTa等先进工具提供基础资源。

Conclusion: MorfFlex架构有效管理了复杂形态系统，提升了标注一致性，对高规律性语言的NLP应用具有重要价值。

Abstract: We present MorfFlex, a morphological dictionary architecture suitable for languages with extensive regularity in both inflection and derivation. As the primary example of MorfFlex in use we introduce MorfFlex CZ, a morphological dictionary of Czech. It is distributed as a simple, unstructured list of <wordform, lemma, tag> triplets, however, its manually maintained, unpublished source files and conversion scripts encode a sophisticated system of inflectional and derivational patterns. These patterns dramatically reduce the otherwise enormous size of the dictionary, which currently contains over 100 million wordforms and more than 1 million lemmas. The MorfFlex CZ dictionary serves as an essential resource for ensuring the consistency of manual morphological annotation in the Prague Dependency Treebanks and underpins state-of-the-art automatic tools such as MorphoDiTa. In this paper, we focus on: (i) presenting an effective method for managing the rich morphological system within the dictionary, and (ii) demonstrating the utility of such a language resource for maintaining annotation consistency in corpora and supporting the development of advanced NLP applications.

</details>


### [158] [On the Stability of Prompt Ranking in Large Language Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24381)
*Shaoshuai Du,Penghao Liang,Yixian Shen,Chuanqi Shi,Hang Zhang,Lun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于提示的大语言模型交互中，提示排名在随机种子和有限评估子集等常见变异性来源下的稳定性问题。发现虽然整体排名相关性中等至高，但最优提示的身份经常变化，导致选择不可靠。为此提出一种基于置信下限的稳定性感知选择策略，在保证竞争力的同时提高了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于提示的大语言模型交互范式隐式假设提示排名在评估条件轻微变化下是稳定的，但该假设是否成立尚未被系统验证。

Method: 在三个开放权重大语言模型和两个基准任务上，系统研究随机种子和有限评估子集对提示排名稳定性的影响，并提出基于置信下限的稳定性感知选择策略。

Result: 整体排名相关性中等至高，但最优提示的身份频繁变化；提出的策略在不稳定场景下提高了鲁棒性，在稳定场景下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 在提示选择和LLM基准测试中应考虑评估不确定性。

Abstract: Prompt-based interaction has become a dominant paradigm for using large language models (LLMs), where multiple candidate prompts are evaluated and the top-ranked one is selected for downstream use. This workflow implicitly assumes that prompt rankings are stable under minor variations in evaluation conditions. In this paper, we systematically study prompt ranking stability under common sources of variability, including random seeds and limited evaluation subsets. Across three open-weight LLMs and two benchmark tasks, we find that while overall rank correlations are often moderate to high, the identity of the top-performing prompt frequently changes, leading to unreliable selection decisions. To address this issue, we propose a simple stability-aware selection strategy based on a lower confidence bound, which accounts for both performance and variance. Our results show that this approach improves robustness in unstable settings while remaining competitive in more stable regimes. These findings highlight the importance of accounting for evaluation uncertainty in prompt selection and LLM benchmarking.

</details>


### [159] [AutoSpecNER: A Fine-Grained Named Entity Recognition Dataset for Vehicle Specification Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24387)
*Jordan Lee,Filippos Ventirozos,Abdirahman Abdullahm,Ioanna Nteka,Peter Appleby,Matthew Shardlow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoSpecNER，一个由专家标注的车辆广告细粒度命名实体识别数据集，包含659条广告和10,000+实体（15个类别），并基准测试了多种方法，其中DeBERTa以90%的micro-F1分数表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆广告包含丰富的规格信息，但汽车领域的NER资源有限，缺乏高质量标注数据集和系统性的方法基准测试。

Method: 构建了包含659条车辆广告、10,000+实体（15个类别）的专家标注数据集AutoSpecNER，通过计算标注者间一致性（平均91.5%）保证质量；基准测试了基于规则的方法、微调Transformer编码器（如DeBERTa）及大型语言模型。

Result: DeBERTa达到最优性能（90% micro-F1），远超基于规则的基线（43%）和最强LLM（77.8%）。

Conclusion: AutoSpecNER为汽车领域NER提供了高质量的标注资源，DeBERTa在该任务上显著优于规则和LLM方法。

Abstract: Vehicle advertisements contain rich specification information, but automotive NER resources remain limited. We introduce AutoSpecNER, an expert-annotated dataset for fine-grained entity recognition in vehicle listings. The dataset includes 659 advertisements from a popular car-selling website, with over 10,000 entities annotated across 15 categories, including MODEL, ENGINE_SPEC, and BATTERY_CAPACITY. Annotation quality was validated through inter-annotator agreement, achieving an average score of 91.5%. We benchmark rule-based extraction, fine-tuned transformer encoders, and large language models. DeBERTa achieves the best performance with a 90% micro-F1 score, outperforming the rule-based baseline (43%) and the strongest large language model (77.8%).

</details>


### [160] [Beyond Logprobs: A Multi-Signal Confidence Engine for LLM-Based Document Field Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24420)
*Nitesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ExtractConf是一种跨域、字段无关的置信度估计引擎，通过两种不同结构（Hunter字段引导和Mapper文档引导）对同一文档进行双重提取，利用其不一致性、LLM内部不确定性、OCR、图像质量和空间布局信号来区分可信与不可信的提取结果。在DocILE数据集上达到0.928 ROC AUC，选择性预测风险降低70%，80%覆盖率下准确率达99.1%，零样本迁移到CORD收据数据集AUC为0.858。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险文档处理管线中，LLM提取的错误比缺失更危险，现有置信度估计方法（如token级log概率、口头表达置信度、多样本自一致性）在实际阈值下全部趋于正向行为，无法可靠区分可信与不可信的提取结果。

Method: 设计了两种异构读取方式：Hunter（字段引导，在模式槽填充压力下提取每个字段）和Mapper（文档引导，整体扫描并基于文档内容提取值）。利用两者的不对称失败模式（Hunter会为缺失字段产生幻觉，Mapper会遗漏视觉上不突出的字段）以及跨调用不一致性、LLM内部不确定性、OCR、图像质量和空间布局信号，融合为分类器，无需领域特定规则或重训练。

Result: 在DocILE数据集（55字段发票，26%失败率）上达到0.928 ROC AUC，选择性预测风险比logprob均值降低70%；80%覆盖率下准确率达99.1%。零样本迁移到CORD收据数据集AUC为0.858；轻量Lasso重新校准将ECE降低89%，Brier分数降低43%。

Conclusion: ExtractConf通过结构性异构读取的不一致性提供了可靠的置信度估计信号，能够跨文档域泛化，实现了可投入实际使用的人机协同工作流。

Abstract: In high-stakes document processing pipelines, including financial reconciliation, compliance verification, and procurement automation, an LLM extraction that is silently wrong is more dangerous than one that is visibly absent. The central challenge is not extraction accuracy alone but reliable confidence estimation: knowing, field by field, whether an extraction can be trusted for automation or deferred to human review. Token-level log-probabilities, verbalized confidence, and multi-sample self-consistency all collapse toward all-positive behaviour at practical thresholds, offering no reliable separation between trustworthy and untrustworthy extractions.
  We present ExtractConf, a cross-domain, field-agnostic confidence engine that grounds confidence estimation in two structurally different readings of the same document. A field-guided Hunter call extracts each field under schema-slot completion pressure; a document-guided Mapper call scans holistically and surfaces values grounded in document content. This asymmetry yields different failure modes: Hunter hallucinates values for absent fields, while Mapper misses visually non-salient ones. Their disagreement is independently informative. ExtractConf fuses cross-call disagreement, LLM-internal uncertainty, OCR, image quality, and spatial layout into a classifier requiring no domain-specific rules or retraining. On DocILE (55-field invoices, 26% failure rate), it achieves 0.928 ROC AUC and reduces selective prediction risk by 70% over logprob-mean. At 80% coverage, accuracy reaches 99.1%, enabling a practical human-in-the-loop workflow. Zero-shot transfer to CORD receipts achieves 0.858 AUC; lightweight Lasso recalibration reduces ECE by 89% and Brier by 43%, confirming the signals generalise across document domains.

</details>


### [161] [Escaping the Self-Confirmation Trap: An Execute-Distill-Verify Paradigm for Agentic Experience Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24428)
*Shiding Zhu,Yudi Qi,Yajie Wang,Jiaze Li,Chao Song,Yaorui Shi,Yibo Miao,Hanqi Gao,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EDV框架（执行-蒸馏-验证），通过多智能体协作替代单智能体自我反思，解决LLM智能体经验学习中的自我确认陷阱问题，在三个长程任务基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验学习方法依赖单智能体循环（同一智能体执行任务、总结结果、决定记忆内容），导致智能体容易陷入自我确认陷阱：错误但自洽的轨迹被误判为成功经验，在检索和复用时产生累积错误。

Method: EDV框架包含三个阶段：(1) Execute阶段：多个异构智能体并行探索同一任务空间，生成多样化候选轨迹；(2) Distill阶段：专用第三方智能体对比分析这些轨迹，生成候选经验，减少执行者中心的总结偏差；(3) Verify阶段：执行组通过共识机制验证候选经验，仅批准的经验写入共享或私有记忆。

Result: 在tau2-bench、Mind2Web和MMTB三个长程基准上，EDV一致优于强基线方法，验证了可靠经验构建对智能体自我进化的必要性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦执行、蒸馏和验证三个阶段，EDV将经验学习从孤立自我反思转变为协作构建，在记忆写入前过滤错误和噪声内容，实现了更可靠的智能体自我进化。

Abstract: Experience-driven self-evolution is critical for large language model (LLM) agents to improve through open-world interaction. However, existing experience learning methods mostly rely on single-agent loops, where the same agent executes tasks, summarizes outcomes, and determines memory content. This setup makes agents vulnerable to the Self-Confirmation Trap: wrong-but-self-consistent trajectories are misidentified as successful experience, leading to cumulative errors during retrieval and reuse. To address this issue, we propose EDV, an Execute-Distill-Verify framework for reliable experience learning. In the Execute stage, multiple heterogeneous agents explore the same task space in parallel to generate diverse candidate trajectories. In the Distill stage, a dedicated third-party agent comparatively analyzes these trajectories to produce candidate experiences, reducing executor-centric summarization bias. In the Verify stage, the execution group validates candidates via a consensus mechanism, and only approved experiences are written into shared or private memory. By decoupling the three stages, EDV transforms experience learning from isolated self-reflection into collaborative construction, filtering erroneous and noisy content before memory insertion. We evaluate EDV on three challenging long-horizon benchmarks: tau2-bench, Mind2Web and MMTB. Results show EDV consistently outperforms strong baselines, validating that reliable experience construction is essential for robust agent self-evolution. Our code is available at https://github.com/shidingz/EDV.

</details>


### [162] [The African Language Tax: Quantifying the Cost, Latency, and Context Penalty of Tokenizing African Languages in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24460)
*Olaoye Anthony Somide*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 商业大语言模型按token计费、计算延迟和上下文预算，但分词器对某些语言分配的子词token数量多于其他语言。本文系统测量了20种非洲语言在11种前沿分词器上的token生育率惩罚，发现所有非洲语言都比英语承受更高的token化溢价（GPT-5中位数1.88倍，N'Ko最高8.92倍），导致高达8.9倍的推理成本和仅相当于英语11%的有效上下文窗口。最好的分词器（Gemma 4）也未能消除此惩罚。研究发布了测量工具、排行榜和缓解指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲语言在商业大语言模型中因token化不平等而承受结构性惩罚，但这种惩罚在部署经济学和认知上下文容量层面尚未被系统量化测量。

Method: 使用并行语料库（FLORES-200+和MAFAND-MT），跨越20种非洲语言（5个语系、3种文字），在11种前沿和开源分词器上测量token生育率，隔离语言效应与内容效应。

Result: 所有非洲语言均承受高于英语的token化溢价（GPT-5中位数1.88倍，N'Ko达8.92倍）；埃塞俄比亚文字和N'Ko文字惩罚最大（7-9倍）；跨语料库近乎不变（Pearson r=0.9998）；导致至多8.9倍推理成本、等效延迟倍数，以及仅11%的英文有效上下文窗口。

Conclusion: token化惩罚对非洲语言构成内置数字鸿沟，目前无分词器能消除此惩罚；发布了开源测量工具、排行榜和缓解指南以帮助非洲开发者。

Abstract: Commercial large language models bill, scale latency, and budget context per token. Yet tokenizers assign more subword tokens to the same meaning in some languages than in others, so speakers of languages with high token-fertility pay a structural penalty before a model is ever invoked. This penalty is documented for multilingual settings in general, but it has not been measured systematically for African languages at the level of enterprise deployment economics and cognitive context capacity. We measure it across 20 African languages spanning five language families and three scripts (Latin, Ge'ez/Ethiopic, N'Ko; 19 appear in the primary FLORES-200+ corpus, with Nigerian Pidgin measured via MAFAND-MT only), using parallel corpora so that the language effect is isolated from content. Across 11 frontier and open tokenizers on FLORES-200+, every African language carries a tokenization premium above English (median 1.88x on GPT-5 / o200k_base, up to 8.92x for N'Ko); the penalty is largest for Ethiopic and N'Ko scripts (reaching 7-9x) and is near-invariant across corpora (FLORES vs SIB-200 Pearson r = 0.9998). Translated into deployment terms, this results in up to 8.9x inference cost and an equivalent generation-latency multiplier (N'Ko vs English on GPT-5; 7.4x for Amharic), and as little as 11% of English's effective context window. The best currently available tokenizer for African languages, Gemma 4, reduces the mean premium from 3.31x (cl100k_base) to 2.38x, but no tokenizer eliminates the penalty. We release an open measurement tool (afri-fertility), a public leaderboard, a results dataset, and mitigation guidance for African builders. The penalty falls hardest on the languages whose speakers can least afford it, a digital divide encoded directly into the subword vocabulary.

</details>


### [163] [UOL@IDEM at BEA 2026 Shared Task 1: Neural Fusion and Feature-Rich Modeling for L1-Aware Vocabulary Difficulty Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24501)
*Nouran Khallaf,Serge Sharoff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回归的多语言词汇难度预测系统，结合多语言上下文表示和多种工程化特征（频率、表面形式、检索证据、语义对齐、同源词相似性和掩码语言模型可预测性），在西班牙语、德语和中文上均优于官方基线，RMSE分别为1.132、1.037和0.891。频率是最稳定的预测特征，而上下文可预测性等特征提供了互补的L1敏感信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对BEA 2026共享任务中L1感知的词汇难度预测问题，旨在构建一个不依赖外部数据的封闭赛道系统，提升跨语言词汇难度预测的准确性。

Method: 将任务建模为回归问题，为西班牙语、德语和中文分别训练独立系统。系统结合多语言上下文表示（BGE-M3、mE5、LaBSE等）与工程化特征，包括频率、表面形式、检索证据、语义对齐、同源词相似性和MLM可预测性。

Result: 官方提交在西班牙语、德语和中文上分别取得1.132、1.037和0.891的RMSE。特征分析表明频率是最稳定的预测因子，上下文可预测性、形式相似性、检索和语义特征提供互补信号。错误分析显示排序性能良好，但对最容易项目的校准较弱（常被高估）。

Conclusion: 所提出的特征组合和回归框架在L1感知的词汇难度预测中有效，多语言句子嵌入编码器表现最佳，频率特征最为稳健，但模型在低难度项目上存在过预测偏差。

Abstract: This paper describes UOL@IDEM's closed-track submission to the BEA 2026 shared task on L1-aware vocabulary difficulty prediction. We model the task as regression and train separate systems for Spanish, German, and Mandarin Chinese\footnote{Below we use \emph{Chinese} for brevity.}. Our system combines multilingual contextual representations with engineered features capturing frequency, surface form, retrieval evidence, semantic alignment, cognate similarity, and masked-language-model predictability. Development results show consistent gains over the official closed-track baselines, with sentence-embedding encoders such as BGE-M3, multilingual E5, and LaBSE performing best. Official submissions achieve RMSE scores of 1.132, 1.037, and 0.891 for Spanish, German, and Chinese, respectively. Feature analysis identifies frequency as the most stable predictor, while contextual predictability, form similarity, retrieval, and semantic features provide complementary L1-sensitive signals. Error analysis shows strong ranking performance but weaker calibration for the easiest items, which are often overpredicted. See https://github.com/Nouran-Khallaf/UoL-IDEM-BEA2026-Vocabulary-Difficulty-Prediction

</details>


### [164] [Poster: Exploring the Limits of Audio-Based Detection of Turkish Phone Call Scams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24523)
*Arda Eren,Micheal Cheung,Youqian Zhang,Grace Ngai,Eugene Yujun Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了土耳其语诈骗电话的多模态数据集（100对音频-文本对），并评估了7种大语言模型在不同输入条件下的检测表现，发现基于文本转录的方法优于直接音频处理，且人工校正与自动转录效果相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有诈骗电话检测研究几乎全部聚焦英语等富资源语言，忽视了土耳其语等低资源语言社区面临的严重诈骗威胁，这些语言中标注数据稀缺、技术防护薄弱。

Method: 构建首个公开的土耳其语诈骗对话多模态数据集（100对音频-文本对），涵盖诈骗与良性对话；在三种输入条件下评估7种LLM（Gemini 2.5系列、GPT-4o、Qwen系列）：原始音频、ASR自动转录、母语者校正转录。

Result: 基于文本转录的输入在检测效果上持续优于直接音频处理；人工校正转录与未校正的自动转录表现相当，无显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了在多语言、低资源场景下开展包容性AI安全研究的紧迫性，亟需构建更鲁棒的多模态欺诈防御系统。

Abstract: Scam phone calls exploit vulnerable communities worldwide, yet research on detection has focused almost exclusively on English and other high-resource languages. In low-resource settings such as Turkish, detection is especially difficult, as annotated data is scarce and technological defenses remain limited. This research investigates how large language models (LLMs) can support scam detection in Turkish by introducing the first public multi-modal dataset of 100 aligned audio-transcript pairs of scam and benign conversations. We evaluate seven LLMs spanning three model families: Gemini 2.5 (Flash, Flash-Lite, Pro), GPT-4o, and Qwen (Max, Plus, Turbo), under three input conditions: raw audio, automatic speech-to-text transcripts, and transcripts refined by a native speaker. Our results suggest that transcript-based inputs consistently outperform direct audio processing, while human-corrected and uncorrected transcripts perform comparably. By centering a low-resource language and real world threat, this work highlights the urgent need for culturally and linguistically inclusive AI safety research and more robust multi-modal systems for fraud prevention.

</details>


### [165] [AGORA: An Archive-Grounded Benchmark for Agentic Workplace Document Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24526)
*Honglin Guo,Qi Zhang,Yu Zhang,Weijie Li,Rui Zheng,Zhikai Lei,Qiyuan Peng,Zhiheng Xi,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agora基准测试，包含362个问题和8个领域共9646份真实文档（3.72亿tokens），评估大语言模型在档案化文档中进行基于证据的推理能力。最强的模型准确率仅59.4%，表明该任务远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅覆盖档案化推理的部分环节，缺乏同时强调档案依据、智能体探索和跨领域覆盖的Benchmark。

Method: 构建Agora基准：包含8个领域共9646份真实文档（3.72亿tokens），通过智能体流水线实现跨文档任务合成、防泄漏混淆和难度过滤，生成362个问答对。

Result: 评估8个模型，最强模型准确率仅59.4%，且各领域表现差异显著。

Conclusion: 档案化推理任务远未解决，现有LLM在跨文档证据定位、术语单位不一致处理等方面仍有显著不足，Agora为未来研究提供了重要评估工具。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as agents that reason over documents rather than answer from parametric knowledge. We study archive-grounded reasoning: locating sparse evidence across a large, messy collection of workplace files, reconciling inconsistent terminology, units, and time conventions, and computing an answer. Existing benchmarks address only parts of this setting and none jointly stresses archive-groundedness, agentic exploration, and cross-domain coverage. We introduce Agora, a benchmark pairing 362 questions with eight domain collections of 9,664 authentic documents and 372M tokens, far exceeding any model's context window, so agents must explore deliberately rather than scan exhaustively. Agora is built by an agentic pipeline combining cross-document task synthesis, leakage-preventing obfuscation, and difficulty filtering. Evaluating eight models, we find the task far from solved: even the strongest reaches only 59.4% accuracy, with notable variation across domains.

</details>


### [166] [NatureBench: Can Coding Agents Match the Published SOTA of Nature-Family Papers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24530)
*Yuru Wang,Lejun Cheng,Yuxin Zuo,Sihang Zeng,Bingxiang He,Che Jiang,Junlin Yang,Yuchong Wang,Kaikai Zhao,Weifeng Huang,Kai Tian,Zhenzhao Yuan,Jincheng Zhong,Weizhi Wang,Ning Ding,Bowen Zhou,Kaiyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NatureBench是一个包含90个任务的跨学科基准，用于评估AI编程代理能否在真实科学问题上超越复现实现科学发现。最强模型仅在17.8%的任务上超越SOTA，代理成功主要依赖方法翻译而非科学发明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理研究基准因环境碎片化问题而缺乏可信度，且无法评估AI在真实科学问题上的发现能力，而非简单的复现能力。

Method: 从Nature系列出版物中提取90个任务构建NatureBench；提出NatureGym自动化流水线，为每个任务构建标准化的容器化环境以解决环境碎片化问题；在严格禁用网络搜索的协议下评估十种前沿代理配置。

Result: 最强模型在g>0.1准则下仅在17.8%的任务上超越SOTA；代理主要通过方法翻译（将科学任务转化为有监督预测问题）而非真正的科学发明来成功；失败主要源于方法选择错误和计算预算不足，而非任务理解错误。

Conclusion: 当前AI编程代理在真实科学发现任务上表现有限，仍缺乏真正的科学创新能力；基准、NatureGym流水线和公共排行榜已开源。

Abstract: We introduce NatureBench, a cross-discipline benchmark of 90 tasks distilled from peer-reviewed Nature-family publications, designed to evaluate whether AI coding agents can move beyond reproduction toward discovery on real scientific problems. NatureBench is built on NatureGym, an automated pipeline that constructs a standardized, per-task containerized environment from a source paper, addressing the environment-fragmentation problem that has limited the credibility of prior agent-on-research benchmarks. Evaluating ten frontier agent configurations under a strict web-search-disabled protocol, we find that the strongest model surpasses SOTA on only 17.8% of tasks under the g>0.1 criterion. Analysis of method pathways reveals that agents succeed primarily through methodological translation, converting scientific tasks into familiar supervised prediction problems, rather than through genuine scientific invention. Failures are dominated by wrong method choice and insufficient compute budget, not by task misunderstanding. We release the benchmark, the NatureGym pipeline, and a public leaderboard with maintainer-side reproduction. Code: https://github.com/FrontisAI/NatureBench

</details>


### [167] [Cross-Lingual Exploration for Parametric Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24579)
*Elisha Diskind,Itamar Trainin,Uri Shaham,Leshem Choshen,Idan Szpektor,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究跨语言提示探索策略，以解锁大型语言模型中隐藏的参数化知识。通过在17种语言的多语言事实基准上的实验，证明跨语言探索能显著提升知识迁移和事实召回，在计算效率上优于单语言扩展，并能改善跨语言一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型中的参数化知识在不同语言间并非同等可访问，标准推理方法难以挖掘本地化事实，导致跨语言知识迁移和一致性失败。

Method: 识别出跨语言探索的四个内在维度，这些维度直接影响参数化知识检索，并在覆盖17种类型多样语言的多语言事实基准上进行评估。

Result: 跨语言探索显著提升了知识迁移和事实召回，在计算效率上优于单语言扩展（更优的Pareto前沿），并带来了跨语言一致性的提升，且这一提升超越了仅由准确率增益所能解释的范围。

Conclusion: 多语言提示探索是一种高效的推理时策略，能够有效解锁大型语言模型中潜在的参数化知识。

Abstract: Parametric knowledge in Large Language Models is not equally accessible across languages. As a result, standard inference techniques often struggle to surface localized facts, leading to failures in cross-lingual knowledge transfer and consistency. In this work, we investigate techniques for accessing hidden factual knowledge by exploring cross-lingual prompting strategies. We identify four inherent dimensions of cross-lingual exploration that directly govern parametric knowledge retrieval and evaluate them on multilingual factual benchmarks covering 17 typologically diverse languages. Our results demonstrate that cross-lingual exploration significantly improves knowledge transfer and factual recall, representing a more efficient compute Pareto frontier than native-language scaling. Furthermore, we observe corresponding improvements in cross-lingual consistency, exceeding what can be explained by accuracy gains alone. Overall, our work establishes multilingual prompt exploration as a highly effective inference-time strategy for unlocking latent parametric knowledge.

</details>


### [168] [MEMPROBE: Probing Long-Term Agent Memory via Hidden User-State Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24595)
*Enze Ma,Yufan Zhou,Wei-Chieh Huang,Jie Yang,Huanhuan Ma,Zixuan Wang,Chengze Li,Chunyu Miao,Philip S. Yu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MEMPROBE是一个评估LLM代理长期记忆的新基准，通过从代理记忆后验中重建隐藏的用户状态（50个模拟用户，31维隐藏状态，共1550个恢复目标）来直接审计记忆质量，而非仅通过下游行为间接评估。实验发现任务完成和记忆可恢复性是两种不同的能力，即便无记忆基线也能饱和任务完成率，但类别平衡恢复率仅约0.6，在top-k检索下进一步下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的长期记忆评估主要通过下游行为（如回答质量、个性化、任务成功率）间接测试，记忆工件本身缺乏审计。作者认为长期记忆应作为可审计的后交互工件来评估：在常规协助后，能从代理留下的记忆中重建出怎样的结构化用户状态。

Method: 构建MEMPROBE基准：1）内存代理协助携带隐藏用户状态（基于分类锚定的状态库）的模拟用户；2）用户执行一系列泄漏控制的轨迹任务；3）从代理的最终记忆中重建用户状态（全存储和top-k两种访问模式）。采用合成真实数据以实现高效可扩展测量，覆盖5种代表性记忆系统。

Result: 任务完成率接近饱和（即使无记忆基线），但类别平衡恢复率仅约0.6，在top-k检索下进一步下降。这表明成功协助和可恢复记忆是两种不同的能力。

Conclusion: MEMPROBE是首个直接研究记忆恢复的基准，通过重建系统保留的用户状态并对照ground truth评分来评估。作者将记忆恢复视为未来记忆代理可优化的具体目标，MEMPROBE朝此方向迈出了一步。

Abstract: Long-term memory promises LLM agents that grow more capable across sessions, maintaining an accurate, evolving understanding of the user that interaction forms. In practice, however, this memory is evaluated mostly through downstream behavior, such as later answers, personalization quality, or task success, which tests that understanding only indirectly and leaves the memory artifact itself largely unaudited. We argue that long-term memory should instead be evaluated as an auditable post-interaction artifact: after ordinary assistance, what structured user state can be reconstructed from the memory the agent leaves behind? We instantiate this view in MEMPROBE, a benchmark in which a memory-equipped agent assists simulated users, each carrying a hidden, taxonomy-anchored user-state bank, across a trajectory of leak-controlled tasks, after which that bank is reconstructed from the agent's resulting memory under both full-store and top-k access. Built on synthetic ground truth for efficient, scalable measurement, MEMPROBE spans 50 simulated users with 31 hidden dimensions each (1,550 recovery targets) and tests 5 representative memory systems. Testing state-of-the-art memory agents, we find that successful assistance and recoverable memory behave as distinct capabilities. Task completion nearly saturates, even for a memoryless baseline, while category-balanced recovery stays moderate (about 0.6) and drops further under top-k retrieval. MEMPROBE is the first benchmark to study memory recovery directly, reconstructing the user state a system retains and scoring it against ground truth. We see recovery as a concrete objective for future memory agents to optimize, and MEMPROBE as a step toward an environment where agents are trained to remember their users, growing more faithful the longer they know them.

</details>


### [169] [To Compare, or Not to Compare: On Methodological Practices in Evaluating Social Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24596)
*Federico Marcuzzi,Xuefei Ning,Roy Schwartz,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架来标准化异构的社会偏见评估基准，揭示了一个系统性的评估范式差距：孤立评估限制偏见激活，而比较设置会催化潜在歧视；CoT推理在比较设置下加剧偏见，且该现象随模型规模扩大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前社会偏见评估文献存在方法论碎片化问题，忽视基准测试的结构性框架，导致矛盾结论。需要统一框架来系统对比不同评估设置，并厘清CoT推理、中性选项等结构因素的混淆效应。

Method: 提出统一可控框架，标准化异构基准测试，系统对比孤立人口统计学评估与强制比较设置；通过控制实验分离CoT推理、中性回退选项等结构伪影的混淆效应，在多个模型系列上进行评估。

Result: 发现系统性范式差距：比较设置比孤立评估更能催化潜在歧视，主要由不明确的上下文驱动；CoT推理在比较设置下加剧社会偏见；即使提供中性选项或声称随机回答，系统偏见仍作为确定性偏见存在；该比较偏见现象随模型规模增大而增强。

Conclusion: 提出方法论指南：研究人员应利用比较设置来审计隐藏偏见，但实践者不能安全地依赖比较设置在模糊的真实世界任务中进行部署。

Abstract: As Large Language Models are increasingly deployed in critical applications, robustly evaluating their social biases is paramount. However, the current literature suffers from widespread methodological fragmentation, which yields contradictory conclusions. This stems largely from ignoring the structural framing of benchmark-level evaluations. To resolve this, we introduce a unified and controllable framework that standardizes heterogeneous benchmarks to systematically contrast isolated demographic assessments with forced-choice comparative settings. Crucially, this allows us to disentangle the confounding effects of Chain-of-Thought reasoning, neutral fallback options, and other structural artifacts in social bias evaluations. Our evaluation across multiple model families reveals a massive, systematic paradigm gap: while isolated assessments limit prejudice activation, comparative settings act as aggressive catalysts for latent discrimination, a shift primarily driven by underspecified contexts. Alarmingly, CoT reasoning exacerbates social biases under comparative settings, and this systemic bias persists as a deterministic prejudice even when models are provided neutral fallback options or claim to answer randomly. Finally, we demonstrate that this comparative prejudice is a generalized phenomenon that scales positively with model size. Ultimately, we offer a crucial methodological guideline: while researchers must leverage comparative settings to robustly audit hidden biases, practitioners cannot safely rely on comparative deployments in ambiguous real-world tasks.

</details>


### [170] [Qwen-AgentWorld: Language World Models for General Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24597)
*Yuxin Zuo,Zikai Xiao,Li Sheng,Fei Huang,Jianhong Tu,Yuxuan Liu,Tianyi Tang,Xiaomeng Hu,Yang Su,Qingfeng Lan,Yantao Liu,Qin Zhu,Yinger Zhang,Bowen Yu,Haiquan Zhao,Haiyang Xu,Jianxin Yang,Jiayang Cheng,Junyang Wang,Lianghao Deng,Mingfeng Xue,Tianyi Bai,Yang Fan,Yubo Ma,Yucheng Li,Zeyu Cui,Zhihai Wang,Zhihui Xie,Zhuorui Ye,An Yang,Dayiheng Liu,Jingren Zhou,Ning Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Qwen-AgentWorld系列语言世界模型，通过三阶段训练（CPT、SFT、RL）在7个领域实现环境模拟，并发布AgentWorldBench评测基准。实验表明该模型在环境仿真和下游智能体任务上显著优于现有模型，同时探索了世界模型作为解耦模拟器和统一基座模型的两种应用范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于语言模型的世界建模如何进一步推动通用智能体的边界，解决现有世界模型在复杂智能体环境仿真中的不足，并建立统一的评测标准。

Method: 三阶段训练流程：（1）CPT阶段注入通用世界建模能力，学习状态转移动力学并增强专业语料；（2）SFT阶段激活下一状态预测推理能力；（3）RL阶段通过混合规则与评分奖励的框架优化仿真保真度。同时构建AgentWorldBench评测基准。

Result: Qwen-AgentWorld在环境仿真质量上显著超越现有前沿模型；作为解耦模拟器可支撑数千环境的大规模智能体强化学习并获得超越真实环境训练的增益；作为统一基座模型，世界模型训练可作为高效预热身阶段提升7个智能体基准的下游性能。

Conclusion: 语言世界模型在智能体环境模拟和下游任务中具有巨大潜力，三阶段训练方案和两种应用范式为通用智能体研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: A world model predicts environment dynamics based on current observations and actions, serving as a core cognitive mechanism for reasoning and planning. In this work, we investigate how world modeling based on language models can further push the boundaries of general agents. (i) We first focus on building foundation models for agentic environment simulation. We introduce Qwen-AgentWorld-35B-A3B and Qwen-AgentWorld-397B-A17B, the first language world models capable of simulating agentic environments covering 7 domains via long chain-of-thought reasoning. Leveraging more than 10M environment interaction trajectories of 7 domains in real-world environments, we develop Qwen-AgentWorld through a three-stage training pipeline: CPT injects general-purpose world modeling capabilities from the state transition dynamics and augmented professional corpora, SFT activates next-state-prediction reasoning, and RL sharpens simulation fidelity through a tailored framework with hybrid rubric-and-rule rewards. To evaluate language world models, we present AgentWorldBench, a comprehensive benchmark constructed from real-world interactions of 5 frontier models on 9 established benchmarks. Empirical results demonstrate that Qwen-AgentWorld significantly outperforms existing frontier models. (ii) Beyond foundation models, we further investigate two complementary paradigms through which world modeling enhances general agents. First, as a decoupled environment simulator, Qwen-AgentWorld supports scalable and controllable simulation of thousands of real-world environments for agentic RL, yielding gains that surpass real-environment training alone. Second, as a unified agent foundation model, world-model training acts as a highly effective warm-up that improves downstream performance across 7 agentic benchmarks. Code: https://github.com/QwenLM/Qwen-AgentWorld

</details>


### [171] [Same Lesson, Different Story: Cross-Lingual Reconstruction of Cultural Narratives in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24610)
*Jory Alshaalan,Haya Albaker,Abeer Aldayel,Aljawharah Alabdullatif,Rehab Alahmadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过15种语言的414条谚语和4个LLM生成的13k叙事，发现跨语言提示虽保留谚语语义，但系统性改变了叙事中的主体性、社会定位和叙事结构，且不同模型间存在强收敛性，表明仅依赖语义相似性会高估文化保留程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多种文化传达相同道德教训时，对文化语境的评估变得复杂，而LLM能否在跨文化叙事中保留文化意义尚不确定。

Method: 构建多语言评估叙事框架，收集15种语言的414条语义等价谚语，使用4个LLM生成13k叙事，分析跨语言条件下模型是否保留语义意义、跨语言条件如何影响叙事实现、不同模型家族是否趋同于相似解释。

Result: 跨语言提示基本保留谚语语义，但系统性重新分配了主体性、社会定位和叙事结构；单语和跨语言设置中均观察到强模型间收敛性。

Conclusion: 多语言LLM依赖共享语义抽象，但仅依赖语义相似性评估会高估文化保留程度，需更全面的文化语境界定评估。

Abstract: The evaluation of cultural grounding context becomes complex when multiple cultures convey the same moral lesson. This challenge is particularly relevant to large language models (LLMs), which produce narratives across a wide range of languages and cultural contexts. However, it remains uncertain whether these models preserve culturally grounded meaning when equivalent moral lessons are conveyed through distinct cultural forms. This study introduces a multilingual evaluation narrative framework that integrates a cross-linguistic collection of 414 proverbs spanning 15 languages and uses four LLMs to generate 13k narratives. By employing semantically equivalent proverbs as culturally grounded prompts, the analysis assesses whether models preserve meaning across languages, how cross-lingual conditioning influences narrative realization, and whether different model families converge on similar interpretations. Results indicate that cross-lingual prompting largely preserves proverb-level semantic meaning while systematically redistributing agency, social positioning, and narrative structure. Additionally, strong inter-model convergence is observed in both monolingual and cross-lingual settings, suggesting that multilingual LLMs rely on shared semantic abstractions despite architectural and linguistic differences. These findings shed light on the need for more comprehensive evaluations of cultural grounding. Relying exclusively on semantic similarity in multilingual narrative assessments may overestimate cultural preservation by neglecting culturally meaningful variations in narrative expression.

</details>


### [172] [Privacy-Preserving RAG via Multi-Agent Semantic Rewriting: Achieving Confidentiality Without Compromising Contextual Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24623)
*Yuanhe Zhao,Tianyu Zhang,Huafei Xing,Derek F. Wong,Jianbin Li,Tao Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多智能体的隐私保护RAG框架，通过语义重写去除检索内容中的敏感标识符，在保持语义核心的同时显著降低隐私泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG在敏感场景中部署时，检索到的外部知识可能通过恶意提示导致隐私泄露，现有方法缺乏有效的隐私保护机制。

Method: 采用多智能体框架，包含三个专用智能体：隐私提取（识别敏感标识符）、语义分析（理解上下文语义）、重构（去除敏感信息并重建语义核心），通过协作方式实现检索内容的语义重写。

Result: 在ChatDoctor和Wiki-PII数据集上，LLaMA-3-8B模型下目标信息泄露从144例降至1例；BLEU-1得分为0.122，优于SAGE方法的0.117；且作为异步预处理模块，不增加在线推理延迟。

Conclusion: 该框架有效平衡了隐私保护与语义保真度，且作为离线预处理模块具备良好的部署实用性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation enhances large language models by incorporating external knowledge, but deploying it in sensitive scenarios risks privacy leakage via malicious prompts. To address this, we propose a multi-agent framework that sanitizes retrieved content through semantic rewriting. By employing three specialized agents for privacy extraction, semantic analysis, and reconstruction, our approach collaboratively removes sensitive identifiers while preserving the semantic core. We evaluate the framework on the ChatDoctor and Wiki-PII datasets across six large language models. Experimental results demonstrate a significant reduction in privacy leakage under targeted attacks. For instance, we reduced targeted information exposure in LLaMA-3-8B from 144 instances in the baseline to just 1. Furthermore, we maintain strong contextual fidelity with a BLEU-1 score of 0.122, outperforming the existing SAGE method's 0.117. Finally, the framework operates as an asynchronous preprocessing module, introducing no additional latency to online inference, as all rewriting is executed as a one-time offline preprocessing step. To promote reproducibility, the source code of this work is publicly available at https://github.com/foursoils/Privacy-Preserving-RAG.

</details>


### [173] [The Warrant Gap: Claim-Conditioned Re-scoring for Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24627)
*Arka Ujjal Dey,John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SIFT方法（基于完整claim的条件性证据重评分）和WSP指标（自动化NLI验证证据是否蕴含主张），在多个事实验证数据集上显著提升分解准确性并校准了人工黄金证据评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的事实验证系统在基准上准确率高，但常输出"支持"标签却引用无法真正支撑claim的证据；结构化分解是检验证据链的自然方式，但刚性的抽取协议会丢失完整claim上下文中所需的细粒度信息。

Method: 提出SIFT（claim-conditioned re-scoring of extracted evidence spans against the full claim），结合WSP（Warranted Supports Proportion），即自动NLI检查引用的证据是否蕴含claim。在FEVER、SciFact、5PILS、DP四个数据集上使用四个开源backbone进行评测。

Result: 在朴素分解方法损失高达27.6个百分点的单元格上，SIFT恢复准确性；WSP相比直接提示（direct prompting）更优；WSP在AUC 0.92和precision 0.98上对标人工黄金证据校准。

Conclusion: SIFT能有效恢复因刚性分解丢失的证据-claim对齐准确性，WSP可作为可靠的自动化证据充分性检查指标。

Abstract: Fact-checking systems built on LLMs achieve high verdict accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet routinely output Supports labels whose cited evidence does not license the claim. Structured decomposition is the natural way to inspect those warrants, but rigid extraction protocols strip the full-claim context that facets need. We introduce SIFT -- claim-conditioned re-scoring of extracted evidence spans against the full claim -- paired with WSP (Warranted Supports Proportion), an automatic NLI check that the cited warrant entails the claim. We evaluate on FEVER, SciFact, 5PILS, and DP across four open-source backbones. SIFT recovers accuracy on cells where naive decomposition costs up to 27.6 points, while raising WSP above direct prompting; WSP itself calibrates against human gold evidence at AUC 0.92 and precision 0.98.

</details>


### [174] [Harmonic: Hierarchical State Space Models for Efficient Long-Context Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24650)
*Petr Nyoma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Harmonic是一种层次化状态空间模型（SSM），通过堆叠三个不同时间尺度的循环层级（每层接收下层预测误差而非原始隐状态），在语言建模中显著优于Transformer和Mamba，尤其擅长长序列处理（最高64K tokens），并且消除了RoPE位置编码的长度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer模型受限于自注意力的二次复杂度（O(L²)），难以高效处理长序列；同时RoPE位置编码存在长度外推上限。Mamba等SSM虽线性复杂度，但在长序列上仍不够理想。作者旨在设计一种能更好处理长程依赖、同时消除位置编码长度限制的层次化SSM架构。

Method: 提出Harmonic架构，堆叠三个循环层级（recurrent levels），每个层级运行在逐渐减慢的时间尺度上。关键创新：每层不是接收下层的原始隐状态，而是接收下层的预测误差（prediction error）作为输入。这种层次化预测误差传递机制实现了O(L)的线性复杂度。

Result: 在enwiki8上，同等token预算下（28M参数），Harmonic在1K/8K/32K tokens上分别比Transformer好1.4%/6.7%/11.4%，比Mamba好0.7-1.8%。在64K tokens上，Transformer和Mamba在80GB H100上OOM，Harmonic成功训练并达到6.169 bpt。WikiText-103上结果一致（H-TF差距1.7%-7.2%）。在1B参数规模，用HarmonicBlock替换TinyLlama 1.1B的所有注意力层得到Hallamonic模型，消除了RoPE限制：在1K-8K序列长度上loss稳定，而TinyLlama在超过2K tokens后性能灾难性下降（在8K序列上Lambada指标差距+9.4 bpt）。

Conclusion: Harmonic通过层次化预测误差传递的SSM设计，在长序列语言建模上同时超越了Transformer和Mamba，具备线性复杂度、优越的长程依赖建模能力，并且天然不受位置编码长度限制，为大语言模型的长上下文处理提供了新的有效架构方案。

Abstract: We present Harmonic, a hierarchical state space model (SSM) for language modeling. The architecture stacks three recurrent levels at progressively slower timescales; each level receives the prediction error of the level below as input, rather than its raw hidden state. On enwiki8 with equal token budgets, Harmonic outperforms a comparable Transformer (28M params) by +1.4% at 1K tokens, +6.7% at 8K tokens, and +11.4% at 32K tokens (bpt, lower is better). It also outperforms Mamba at every tested length by 0.7--1.8%. At 64K tokens, both Mamba and Transformer run out of memory on an 80GB H100; Harmonic trains successfully, reaching 6.169 bpt. Results replicate on WikiText-103 (H-TF gap +1.7% to +7.2% across 1K--32K). At 1B parameter scale, replacing all attention layers in TinyLlama 1.1B with HarmonicBlock eliminates the RoPE positional encoding limit: the resulting Hallamonic model maintains stable loss across sequence lengths 1K--8K on two independent clean benchmarks (Lambada and fineweb-edu held-out), while TinyLlama degrades catastrophically past its 2K-token RoPE limit (gap: +9.4 bpt at seq=8K on Lambada). Compute is O(L) per forward pass vs. O(L^2) for attention.
  Logs: https://github.com/Omibranch/harmonic-logs.

</details>


### [175] [AI-PAVE-Br: Leveraging Large Language Models for Enhanced Product Attribute Value Extraction through a Golden Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24655)
*Murilo Gazzola,Hugo Gobato Souto,Samuel Silva,Júlia Schubert Peixoto,Felipe Siqueira,André Luis Pedroso de Morais,Caio Gomes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AI-PAVEBr系统，利用大语言模型（LLMs）和精心设计的提示工程，在巴西电商葡萄牙语产品描述中实现高精度属性值提取；同时发布了Golden Set——一个经人工标注的葡萄牙语PAVE基准数据集，实验证明该方法显著优于传统命名实体识别（NER）基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西电商领域产品数据爆炸式增长且复杂多样，传统PAVE方法难以处理葡萄牙语的 linguistic nuances 和产品描述多样性，亟需一种针对葡萄牙语的鲁棒且专业的结构化信息提取方法。

Method: 构建了AI-PAVEBr系统，基于大语言模型（LLMs）结合针对性的提示工程（prompt engineering）进行产品属性值提取；同时构建了Golden Set数据集，包含实体、类别、子类别的层次化人工标注结构。

Result: AI-PAVEBr在巴西电商葡萄牙语产品属性提取任务上显著优于传统NER基线方法，验证了LLM结合提示工程在非英语市场的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为非英语市场（巴西葡萄牙语）提供了可扩展的高精度PAVE解决方案，并向NLP社区贡献了首个公开可用的葡萄牙语PAVE基准数据集，促进了可复现研究。

Abstract: The explosive growth and complexity of product data within the dynamic Brazilian e-commerce landscape demand robust and specialized methods for structured information extraction. Traditional approaches to Product Attribute Value Extraction (PAVE) often struggle with the linguistic nuances and sheer diversity of product descriptions in Portuguese. To address this critical gap, this paper introduces two major contributions. First, we present AI-PAVEBr, a specialized system engineered with Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform high-accuracy PAVE specifically for Brazilian e-commerce catalogs. Second, to facilitate reproducible research and provide a definitive benchmark, we introduce and share the Golden Set, a new, meticulously curated, and manually annotated dataset for PAVE in Portuguese. We detail the creation process and structure (Entity, Category, Subcategories) of this high-quality reference set. Our experiments conclusively show that AI-PAVE-Br, leveraging targeted prompt engineering, dramatically outperforms conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) baselines. This work not only delivers a superior, scalable solution for a major non-English market but also enriches the NLP community with a valuable, publicly available resource for future PAVE research.

</details>


### [176] [DREAM: Dense Retrieval Embeddings via Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24667)
*Yixuan Tang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DREAM方法，通过将检索器的查询-文档相似度分数注入冻结LLM的注意力头中，利用自回归下一个令牌预测目标来训练稠密检索嵌入模型，在BEIR和RTEB基准上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稠密检索模型通常需要对比学习目标训练，需要昂贵且难以获取的正负文档对标签。研究者希望探索能否利用LLM的自回归下一个令牌预测目标来提供稠密检索的监督信号，从而降低对标注数据的依赖。

Method: 提出DREAM方法，将检索器生成的查询-文档相似度分数注入到冻结LLM的选定注意力头中，控制每个候选文档在LLM预测目标输出时获得的注意力权重，通过预测损失经由注意力机制为检索器提供梯度更新信号。

Result: 在BEIR和RTEB检索基准上，使用0.5B到3B参数的嵌入骨干网络，DREAM在不同模型规模下均一致优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DREAM证明通过自回归建模训练稠密检索器是一种有前景的方法，有效利用LLM的下一个令牌预测目标为检索器提供监督。

Abstract: Dense retrieval embedding models are a fundamental component of modern retrieval-based AI systems. Most dense retrievers are trained with contrastive objectives, which require labeled positive and negative document pairs that are often costly and difficult to obtain. In this work, we investigate whether the autoregressive next-token prediction objective of a large language model (LLM) can provide supervision for dense retrieval. The intuition is simple: if a document contains information relevant to a query, conditioning on that document should make the target output easier for the LLM to predict. A key challenge is that the next-token prediction loss is computed inside the LLM, while the retriever is a separate embedding model. To address this challenge, we propose DREAM (Dense Retrieval Embeddings via Autoregressive Modeling), which injects retriever-generated query-document similarity scores into selected attention heads of a frozen LLM. During training, these scores determine how much attention each candidate document receives while the LLM predicts the target output. The resulting prediction loss provides gradients for retriever training through the attention mechanism. We evaluate DREAM on retrieval benchmarks BEIR and RTEB using embedding backbones ranging from 0.5B to 3B parameters. DREAM consistently outperforms existing baselines across different model scales. These results demonstrate that DREAM provides a promising approach for training dense retrievers through autoregressive modeling.

</details>


### [177] [CN-NewsTTS Bench: a target-level automatic benchmark for raw-input Chinese news TTS pronunciation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24714)
*Shijun Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1，一个针对中文新闻TTS产品能否正确发音密集书面形式（如分数、连字符型号、范围、单位符号、百分比、英文缩写、中英拉混合名称等）的目标级基准测试，包含开发集、测试集、自动评分器及七款产品的初始评测结果，最优系统精确率为0.879，多款系统低于0.60。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文新闻文本中存在大量密集型书面形式（分数、连字符型号、范围、单位符号、百分比、英文缩写、中英拉混合名称等），这些形式在真实听力工作流中频繁出现，而TTS系统可能保留书面字符串但改变口语含义，现有评测缺乏针对这类目标的标准化基准。

Method: 构建CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1基准，包含200条开发集、800条公开测试集、992个可自动评估目标、基于三ASR集成的固定转录、自动目标评分器；对七款产品TTS系统进行评测，并报告ASR路线诊断、ASR子集消融、类别级结果、置信区间及提供商配置元数据。

Result: 最优系统严格精确率达到0.879，多款系统低于0.60。

Conclusion: 现有中文新闻TTS产品在密集书面形式的正确发音方面仍存在显著差距，CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1可作为评估和推动该领域进步的开放基准。

Abstract: Chinese news text contains dense written forms such as scores, hyphenated model names, ranges, unit symbols, percentages, English abbreviations, and mixed Chinese-Latin-digit names. These forms are frequent in real listening workflows, and a text-to-speech (TTS) system can preserve the written string while changing the spoken meaning. We introduce CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1, an open target-level benchmark for evaluating whether Chinese news TTS products pronounce such targets correctly from raw text, without user-side rules, LLM rewriting, SSML hints, or manual edits. The release contains a 200-record development set, an 800-record public test set, 992 public auto-evaluable targets, fixed transcripts from a three-ASR ensemble, an automatic target scorer, and initial results for seven product TTS systems. We additionally report ASR-route diagnostics, ASR-subset ablations, category-level results, confidence intervals, and provider configuration metadata. The best system reaches 0.879 strict accuracy, while several systems remain below 0.60.

</details>


### [178] [CANDLE: Character-level Arabic Noise Deduplication using Lightweight Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24758)
*Faris Alasmary,Taif Nono,Orjuwan Zaafarani,Kholood Al Tabash,Ahmad Ghannam,Anas Salamah,Shouq Sadah,Lahouari Ghouti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CANDLE是一个轻量级的阿拉伯语字符级噪声去重系统，采用CTC（连接主义时序分类）方法将字符规范化建模为序列对齐问题，在三个基准测试上达到5.37%的句子错误率，并通过知识蒸馏实现3倍深度缩减，同时为阿拉伯语LLM分词器带来高达12.8%的tokenizer fertility降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本中重复字符既可能是正确拼写，也可能是社交媒体中非正式字符延长的噪声，现有方法依赖手工规则、词典或形态分析器，缺乏轻量且通用的解决方案。

Method: 采用CTC（连接主义时序分类）将字符去重重新定义为序列对齐问题，基于字符级编码器进行规范化；进一步通过知识蒸馏将6层CTC模型压缩为2层学生模型。

Result: 在新闻文本、人工标注歧义案例和社交媒体三个基准上，CTC模型SER最低达5.37%，显著优于基于分类的基线；蒸馏后模型深度减少3倍且性能损失极小；下游任务中阿拉伯语LLM分词器的tokenizer fertility相对降低12.8%。

Conclusion: CTC方法在阿拉伯语字符去重任务上有效且高效，知识蒸馏可实现轻量化部署，去重规范化能直接降低LLM推理成本并改善上下文窗口利用率。

Abstract: Handling repeated characters in text can be tricky, since they can represent either the correct spelling of a word or informal character elongation often seen in social media posts. We present CANDLE, a lightweight system for character-level Arabic noise deduplication that addresses this challenge without relying on handcrafted rules, dictionaries, or morphological analyzers. At the heart of CANDLE is a novel application of Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC) to this task, a formulation not previously explored for character deduplication, which frames normalization as a sequence alignment problem over a character-based encoder. Evaluated on three benchmarks spanning clean newspaper, manually curated ambiguous cases, and real-world social media text, the CTC model achieves a Sentence Error Rate (SER) as low as $5.37\%$ and consistently outperforms a classification-based baseline by a large margin. To reduce inference overhead, we distill the 6-layer CTC model into a 2-layer student, achieving a $3\times$ depth reduction with minimal performance degradation.
