While platforms like OpenStreetMap (OSM) successfully capture where a path is, they frequently fail to convey how it physically feels to travel on it. This information barrier is problematic for wheelchair users. To solve this issue, we present OmniPath, a system that moves from passive mapping to proactive environmental auditing. Our framework fuses the network topology of OSM with the submeter precision of high-density aerial LiDAR (USGS 3DEP) to create a high-fidelity 3D model of the pedestrian environment. Rather than simply routing a user, our agent virtually traverses the network, analyzing the surface in 0.5 meter increments. It rigorously quantifies physical friction points specifically running slope, cross slope, and vertical discontinuities against ADA compliance standards, calculating a weighted severity score to categorize hazards from ``Mild'' to ``Critical.'' To ensure real world reliability, we validated the system against 200 physical ground truth field surveys across the National Mall using stratified random sampling. The framework demonstrated strong diagnostic reliability for high-severity hazards, achieving F1-scores of 0.60 for Severe and 0.58 for critical categories. By automating this micro-scale inspection, OmniPath identifies the ``invisible'' barriers that standard maps miss, effectively transforming a static dataset into accessibility data source that anticipates accessibility challenges before the user ever leaves home.

</details>


### [286] [T2D-Bench: Evidence-Gated Evaluation of LLM Outputs for Type 2 Diabetes Using a Multi-Layer Clinical-Lifestyle Knowledge Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24145)
*Saba A. Farahani,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: T2D-Bench是一个可复现的基准测试和证据门控评估框架，用于检验LLM输出的2型糖尿病建议是否满足显式、可图检查的证据要求。该框架基于多层级临床-生活方式知识图谱构建，在100个结构化临床案例上，GPT-4o-mini和GPT-4o分别有35%和33%的基线输出未通过证据路径检查。证据门控机制可检测无支持的遗漏并通过约束修订使输出达到可验证合规水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能够生成临床流畅的2型糖尿病建议，但常常未能满足指南约束或无法明确支持与生活方式相关的血糖声明，缺乏对证据要求的可验证性。

Method: 构建T2D-Bench基准测试框架，包含多层级临床-生活方式知识图谱（整合UMLS、DrugBank、SIDER、可计算的ADA标准护理规则及生活方式知识），设计证据路径检查机制和约束修订方法，在100个结构化临床场景（涵盖诊断、用药安全、对抗性生活方式冲突）上进行评估。

Result: 基线输出中GPT-4o-mini有35%、GPT-4o有33%未通过证据路径检查；证据门控机制能够检测无支持的遗漏并通过约束修订使输出符合基准证据要求。

Conclusion: 可计算的证据约束能够使LLM在糖尿病领域的临床输出中无支持的遗漏变得显式化、可度量和可修正。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can produce clinically fluent recommendations for type 2 diabetes while failing to satisfy guideline constraints or explicitly justify lifestyle-related glycemic claims. We present T2D-Bench, a reproducible benchmark and evidence-gated evaluation framework for testing whether LLM outputs satisfy explicit, graph-checkable evidence requirements. T2D-Bench is built on a multi-layer clinical-lifestyle knowledge graph that combines a biomedical spine (UMLS, DrugBank, SIDER), computable ADA Standards of Care rules, and lifestyle knowledge connected through a mechanistic bridge to glycemic laboratory effects. Across 100 structured vignettes spanning diagnosis, medication safety, and adversarial lifestyle conflicts, baseline outputs failed benchmark-defined evidence-path checks in 35% of cases for GPT-4o-mini and 33% for GPT-4o. The evidence gate detects unsupported omissions and uses constrained revision to bring outputs into verifier-level compliance with benchmark-defined evidence requirements. These results show that computable evidence constraints can make unsupported clinical omissions explicit, measurable, and correctable in diabetes-focused LLM outputs.

</details>


### [287] [The Geometry Behind Diffusion and Flow Matching: Gradient Flows and Geodesics in Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24157)
*Yian Yao,Weiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，扩散模型和最优传输流匹配模型均建立在相同的Wasserstein几何流形上，但遵循不同的变分原理：扩散模型是自由能KL散度的梯度流（初始值问题），对应JKO格式；流匹配是Benamou-Brenier公式的最小作用量测地线（边界值问题）。两者从不同路径到达相同端点，本质上是同一几何框架下的两种方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一扩散模型（DDPM、DDIM、NCSN/SMLD、Energy Matching）与最优传输流匹配模型（Flow Matching）的理论基础，揭示它们共享的Wasserstein几何流形，澄清两类方法之间的精确关系。

Method: 将生成模型置于具有二次Wasserstein度量W_2的概率测度空间P_2(R^d)中，分别分析两种变分原理：1) 自由能F(ρ)=KL(ρ||π)的梯度流对应Fokker-Planck方程，其隐式欧拉离散化即JKO格式，扩散模型的前向过程与去噪步骤均在此框架内；2) Benamou-Brenier公式的最小作用量测地线对应最优传输路径，流匹配沿此直线ODE进行确定性生成。

Result: 扩散模型遵循自由能梯度流（初始值问题），最优传输流匹配遵循Wasserstein测地线（边界值问题），两者在相同流形上从不同路径到达相同端点。扩散模型需要更多采样步数，而流匹配因沿直线测地线运行，所需采样步数更少。

Conclusion: 扩散模型与最优传输流匹配模型并非独立的理论体系，而是同一Wasserstein几何流形上两种不同的变分原理：前者是自由能梯度流的初始值问题离散化，后者是最优传输测地线的边界值问题。这一统一视角为理解生成模型的设计空间提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The space $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d$) of probability measures with finite second moment carries a natural geometry: the quadratic Wasserstein distance W_2 makes it a complete metric space and, following Otto, a (formal) Riemannian manifold whose geodesics are the optimal-transport interpolations. On this manifold, the gradient flow of the free energy F(rho) = KL(rho || π) is exactly the Fokker-Planck equation, and its implicit-Euler discretization is the JKO scheme. This is the geometry underlying diffusion models: the forward process descends the free energy, and each denoising step realizes one JKO step, which recovers DDPM, DDIM, NCSN/SMLD, and Energy Matching; this is one scheme, not separate theories. The same manifold supports a second variational principle. Its geodesics - the minimum-action curves of the Benamou-Brenier formula - are precisely the optimal-transport paths that Flow Matching learns. Fixing both endpoints and following the geodesic, generation becomes a deterministic ODE along a straight line, hence far fewer sampling steps. Placing both families of models on one manifold makes their relationship exact: diffusion follows a free-energy gradient flow, an initial-value problem; optimal-transport Flow Matching follows a Wasserstein geodesic, a boundary-value problem. The two reach the same endpoints along different paths.

</details>


### [288] [An Introduction to Causal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24160)
*Elias Bareinboim,Junzhe Zhang,Sanghack Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了因果强化学习（CRL）的概念，将因果推断与强化学习统一在一个框架下，指出两者都基于反事实关系这一共同基础，并引入广义策略学习、模仿学习和反事实学习等新学习范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断和强化学习两个领域长期独立发展，缺乏交互。然而两者都建立在反事实关系这一共同基础上，作者认为明确承认并形式化这一联系可以催生新的学习机会。

Method: 将RL环境分解为具有不同因果不变性的自主机制集合，用结构因果模型（SCM）进行建模，从而将在线学习、离策略学习和因果推断学习等不同学习模式统一在同一框架下。

Result: 识别并提出了几类新的学习设置：广义策略学习（含干预）、模仿学习和反事实学习，拓展了反事实学习的视野。

Conclusion: 因果推断与强化学习的结合（因果强化学习）具有巨大潜力，能够产生新的分析维度和学习范式，值得深入研究。

Abstract: Causal inference provides a set of principles and tools that allow one to combine data and knowledge about an environment to reason with questions of counterfactual nature, i.e., what would have happened had reality been different, even when no data of this unrealized reality is currently available. Reinforcement learning provides methods to learn a policy that optimizes a specific measure (e.g., reward, regret) when the agent is deployed in an environment and pursues an exploratory, trial-and-error approach. These two disciplines have evolved independently and with virtually no interaction between them. We note that they operate over different aspects of the same building block, counterfactual relations, which makes them umbilically connected. Based on these observations, novel learning opportunities arise when this connection is explicitly acknowledged and mathematized. To realize this potential, we note that any environment where the RL agent is deployed can be decomposed as a collection of autonomous mechanisms with different causal invariances, parsimoniously modeled as a structural causal model; any standard RL setting implicitly encodes such a model. This formalization allows us to put under a unifying treatment different modes of learning, including online, off-policy, and causal calculus learning, which appear unrelated in the literature. However, these modalities are not exhaustive: we introduce several natural and pervasive classes of learning settings that entail novel dimensions of analysis. Specifically, we introduce and discuss through causal lenses generalized policy learning, where to intervene, imitation learning, and counterfactual learning. These tasks lead to a broader view of counterfactual learning and suggest great potential for studying causal inference and reinforcement learning side by side, which we call causal reinforcement learning (CRL).

</details>


### [289] [Reinforcement Learning for Computer-Use Agents with Autonomous Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24515)
*Marta Sumyk,Oleksandr Kosovan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用视觉-语言模型自动评估作为强化学习奖励信号的框架，通过噪声校正的奖励估计器优化GUI智能体策略，在多个桌面环境基准上显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在计算机使用智能体(CUA)应用中面临奖励信号难以获取的问题：桌面环境任务成功与否通常需要视觉判断，难以通过手工设计的奖励函数或密集人工标注来指定可扩展的、机器可读的奖励信号。

Method: 提出RL微调框架，利用视觉-语言模型(VLM)根据最终截图和原始指令自动评估任务完成度并提供终端反馈；将自动评估器的反馈建模为含噪二元奖励通道，推导噪声校正的奖励估计器用于PPO优化。

Result: 在macOSWorld、Windows Agent Arena和OSWorld上的实验表明，校正后的评估器奖励在成功率上比零样本基线平均提升12.6个百分点，比原始评估器奖励微调提升5.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 自主视觉-语言评估可以作为GUI环境中RL的实用奖励信号，前提是显式建模并校正评估器噪声。

Abstract: Computer-Use Agents (CUAs) execute high-level user goals by perceiving and acting directly within graphical user interfaces. However, reinforcement learning for CUAs remains difficult because open-ended desktop environments rarely provide scalable, machine-readable reward signals: task success is often visually grounded and hard to specify with handcrafted reward functions or dense manual labels.
  We propose an RL fine-tuning framework that uses autonomous vision-language evaluation as a scalable supervision signal for GUI agents. Given a final screenshot and the original instruction, a Vision-Language Model judges task completion and provides terminal feedback without task-specific heuristics or manual labels during policy optimization.
  Because autonomous evaluators are imperfect, we model their feedback as a noisy binary reward channel and derive a noise-corrected reward estimator for Proximal Policy Optimization. Experiments across macOSWorld, Windows Agent Arena, and OSWorld show that corrected evaluator rewards outperform both zero-shot baselines and raw evaluator rewards, improving success rates by an average of 12.6 percentage points over zero-shot performance and 5.1 points over raw evaluator fine-tuning. These results suggest that autonomous evaluation can serve as a practical reward signal for RL in GUI environments when evaluator noise is explicitly modeled and corrected.

</details>


### [290] [Data Scale, Not Latency, Shapes Cross-Lingual Encoder Transfer in Streaming ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24169)
*Nenad Banfic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了流式语音识别模型在迁移到新语言时，使用多语言（ML）编码器初始化与仅英语（EN）编码器初始化的优劣。通过对0.6B参数的FastConformer模型在8种欧洲语言、5种数据规模（100h-2500h）、3种流式延迟等级及离线解码下的控制实验，发现多语言初始化的优势是数据受限而非延迟受限：在100h时平均WER降低4.21个百分点，但随数据量增加呈幂律衰减，2500h时优势仅剩0.20个百分点；延迟等级不影响该差距；4-bit量化引入约0.5pp退化。结论：低数据时使用多语言初始化，高数据时两者无差异，延迟和量化决策可独立进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决将流式语音识别模型适配到新语言时的冷启动选择问题：多语言编码器与英语编码器哪种初始化更优，以及该优势在不同数据量、延迟约束和量化条件下的持续性与稳定性。

Method: 在0.6B参数cache-aware FastConformer transducer上进行了受控对照实验，覆盖8种欧洲语言、5种目标语言数据量（100h-2500h）、3种流式延迟等级（含160ms和560ms）及离线解码，使用FLEURS等多个公开测试集评估WER。

Result: 多语言初始化的优势随数据量增加呈幂律衰减（数据翻倍优势减半）；流式延迟等级不影响EN-ML差距；4-bit量化引入约0.5pp平均WER退化，但初始化选择与量化决策正交。

Conclusion: 低数据场景推荐多语言初始化，大数据场景两者可忽略差异；延迟与量化决策可独立于初始化选择进行。

Abstract: Adapting a streaming speech recognition model to a new language requires choosing between two plausible warm starts: a multilingual (ML) encoder or an English-only (EN) encoder. The common intuition is that the multilingual encoder should help most at low data, but it is unclear how long that advantage persists, whether tight streaming latency amplifies it, and whether it survives deployment quantization. We answer these questions with a controlled sweep of a 0.6 B-parameter cache-aware FastConformer transducer across eight European languages, up to five target-language data scales (100 h to 2500 h), three streaming tiers plus offline decoding, and up to four public test sets. The main result is that multilingual initialization is a data-limited advantage, not a latency-limited one. On FLEURS at 160 ms, the mean EN-ML word error rate (WER) gap falls from +4.21 percentage points (pp) at 100 h to +0.20 pp at 2500 h; a power-law fit summarizes this decay, with each doubling of target-language data roughly halving the remaining advantage. Across the three streaming tiers, the across-language mean EN-ML gap is approximately stable at each scale from 100 to 1000 h, and is near zero by 2500 h. Finally, 4-bit weight-only encoder quantization at the matched 560 ms streaming tier reduces the encoder footprint by about 3x, with an average FLEURS WER increase of about 0.5 pp. The resulting guideline is simple: use multilingual initialization in low-data regimes, treat the choice as effectively irrelevant at large data, and make latency and quantization decisions independently.

</details>


### [291] [Themis: An explainable AI-enabled framework for Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24622)
*Andreas Chouliaras,Luke Connolly,Dimitris Chatzpoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Themis是一个结合可解释性人工智能（XAI）与人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）的测试与评估框架，支持200多种环境，能够训练与真实奖励信号匹配或更优的奖励模型，并提供云平台支持大规模用户实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RL系统缺乏行为安全保障，可解释性与人类反馈是两种有效防御手段，但尚无公开框架将二者结合，因此需要统一平台。

Method: 提出Themis框架，整合XAI与RLHF；支持200+环境；提供可配置的实验设置；构建基于云的人类反馈收集与管理平台，具备自动扩展能力。

Result: Themis训练的奖励模型能匹配甚至超越环境真实奖励信号；云平台可在普通商用机器上支持1000名用户同时进行连续实验。

Conclusion: Themis成功弥合了可解释性与人类反馈对齐之间的缺口，为安全RL研究提供了高效、可扩展的统一评估框架。

Abstract: Training safe Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems is inherently challenging, with no guarantee of avoiding unwanted behaviors. The most effective defenses against this are (i) transparency through explainability and (ii) alignment via human feedback. While both show promising results, no publicly available framework currently combines them. To address this, we introduce Themis, an XAI-enabled testing and evaluation framework for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback. Themis supports over 200 widely used environments and is easily configurable for experiments in RL, transparency, and alignment. Our results show that Themis can train reward models that match or outperform the environment's true reward signal using human preferences. We also provide a cloud-based platform for collecting human feedback and managing experiments. It is user-friendly, auto-scalable, and supports large participant groups across multiple experiments without extra development overhead. Tests show Themis can support one thousand users in back-to-back experiments on a modest commercial machine.

</details>


### [292] [Navigating User Behavior toward Personalized Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24196)
*Hengji Zhou,Yufeng Liu,Ye Liu,Yong Xu,Lianghao Xia,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NaviGen通过双重标识符（协同编码+文本编码）将用户行为转化为可执行指令，结合两阶段SFT+RL训练，实现个性化图像与视频生成，在多个领域提升生成质量与指令可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGC管线依赖用户提供完善指令，但终端用户很少能描述视觉细节，导致生成内容与用户需求不对齐。需要将用户交互历史转化为可执行的生成指令。

Method: 提出NaviGen框架：(1) 双重标识符——每项物品赋予协同编码（行为基座）和文本编码（语义桥梁）组成的统一token流；(2) 两阶段SFT+RL流程——先通过演化搜索的监督信号蒸馏偏好推理与指令写作能力，再通过层次化自一致奖励对齐用户意图。

Result: 在商品、游戏、短视频三个领域，NaviGen提升了个性化图像/视频生成质量、下一物品预测准确率，生成了更具体、相关且可直接用于视觉生成的指令。

Conclusion: 双重标识符+两阶段训练能有效将用户行为编码为语言可推理的形式，并赋予模型指令写作能力，实现行为到视觉生成的对齐。

Abstract: Modern AIGC pipelines deliver high-fidelity images and videos but presuppose a well-formed creation instruction, while end users rarely articulate visual details, leaving generators misaligned with user demand. We study personalized content generation, which turns a user's interaction history into an executable instruction for downstream synthesis, and identify two obstacles: behavior must be encoded in a form legible to language reasoning, and the model must acquire instruction-writing skill absent from both pretraining and behavior data. We propose NaviGen, which represents each item with a dual identifier coupling a collaborative code and a textual code as a behavioral substrate and a semantic bridge in one token stream. On this representation, a two-stage SFT+RL pipeline first distills preference reasoning and instruction writing from evolutionarily searched supervision, then aligns generation with user intent through hierarchical and self-consistent rewards. Experiments across product, game, and short-video domains show that NaviGen improves personalized image and video generation, strengthens next-item prediction, and yields more specific, relevant, and visually generatable instructions. Our code is anonymously released at: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/NaviGen.

</details>


### [293] [Exploring the relationship between human-centric AI and firm idiosyncratic risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24224)
*Zhen-Yuan Ralph Liu,Yu-Ting Wang,Jia-Jia Yan,Shivam Gupta,Mihalis Giannakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了工业5.0背景下以人为本的AI（HCAI）对企业特质风险（IR）的影响。基于情境化AI理论和社会技术系统理论，利用2015-2023年中国上市公司面板数据，发现HCAI能降低企业特质风险，且数字化和高管持股强化这一效应，而运营效率和具有IT背景的CEO则弱化该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管工业5.0中HCAI已被广泛讨论，但其对企业特质风险的影响尚不明确。特质风险反映投资者对企业AI战略差异的反应，对企业管理技术革命中的财务风险至关重要，因此亟需探究HCAI与IR之间的关系。

Method: 整合情境化AI理论与社会技术系统理论，将HCAI概念化为一种情境化AI策略。采用2015-2023年中国上市公司的多源面板数据，通过实证分析检验HCAI对IR的影响，并考察数字化、运营效率、高管持股和具有IT背景的CEO等社会技术因素的调节作用。

Result: HCAI与企业更低的特质风险相关；数字化和高管持股强化了HCAI的风险降低效应；而运营效率和具有IT背景的CEO意外地削弱了这一效应。

Conclusion: 研究为AI时代的伦理AI治理和企业财务风险管理提供了理论贡献和实践启示。

Abstract: Despite the extensive discussions of human-centric AI (HCAI) in Industry 5.0, its effects on firms' idiosyncratic risks (IR) remains underexplored. This is an imperative issue for firms navigate financial risks during the current technological revolution, as IR reflects investor reactions to corporate heterogeneous AI strategies and implementations by isolating firm-level stock volatility from systematic factors. Integrating situated AI theory with social-technical systems theory, we conceptualise HCAI as a situated AI strategy that reduces AI-related ethical risks and fosters AI-Human synergies in firms' business operations, ultimately reducing IR by aligning with stakeholders' diverse expectations. Moreover, socio-technical factors, namely digitalisation, operational efficiency, executive shareholding, and CEOs with IT background, may moderate the HCAI-IR relationship. Using a multi-source panel dataset of Chinese listed firms from 2015 to 2023, we find that HCAI is associated with lower firm IR. Furthermore, digitalisation and executive shareholding strengthen this risk-reducing effect, whereas operational efficiency and CEOs with IT background surprisingly attenuate it. Our findings offer theoretical contributions and practical insights for both ethical AI governance and firm financial risk management in the AI era.

</details>


### [294] [FlowR2A: Learning Reward-to-Action Distribution for Multimodal Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24231)
*Xirui Li,Zhe Liu,Xiaoqing Ye,Wenhua Han,Yifeng Pan,Junyu Han,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowR2A通过将模拟奖励从判别目标重构为生成条件，利用流匹配解码器学习奖励条件下的动作分布，统一了基于评分方法的密集监督和基于锚点方法的提议生成，在NAVSIM基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多模态驾驶规划中评分方法（稠密监督但动作词汇固定）与锚点方法（动态生成提议但监督稀疏且局限于单一真实轨迹）之间的长期矛盾。

Method: 提出FlowR2A，将模拟奖励从判别性目标重构为生成性条件，通过流匹配解码器从稠密的轨迹-奖励对中学习奖励条件下的动作分布；引入细粒度每时间步奖励条件化和奖励噪声增强，以平衡硬安全约束与软进度目标；支持通过奖励引导和锚定采样实现可控测试时采样。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2基准上取得最先进结果，多模态提议质量显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: FlowR2A成功统一了评分方法和锚点方法的优势，使模型内化了动作与其在安全性、进度、舒适性和规则合规性方面结果之间的相关性。

Abstract: Multimodal driving planning faces a long-standing tension between two paradigms: scoring-based methods benefit from dense reward supervision but are confined to a fixed action vocabulary, while anchor-based methods generate proposals dynamically yet suffer from sparse supervision constrained to a single ground-truth trajectory. In this work, we propose FlowR2A, which resolves this tension by reframing simulation-based rewards from discriminative targets into generative conditions. By learning the reward-conditioned action distribution from dense trajectory-reward pairs with a flow-matching decoder, FlowR2A unifies the dense supervision of scoring-based methods with the proposal generation of anchor-based methods in a single generative model, forcing the model to internalize the correlation between an action and its outcomes in safety, progress, comfort, and rule compliance. To balance hard safety constraints against soft progress objectives, we introduce fine-grained per-timestep reward conditioning and reward noise augmentation. The generative formulation naturally supports controllable test-time sampling via reward guidance and anchored sampling, producing high-quality proposals. FlowR2A achieves state-of-the-art results on the NAVSIM v1 and v2 benchmarks, with multimodal proposals of substantially higher quality than prior methods.

</details>


### [295] [Assessing Distribution Shift in Human Activity Recognition for Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24781)
*Rebecca Adaimi,Edison Thomaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了传感器人体活动识别（HAR）中4种分布偏移（设备类型、传感器位置、采样率、用户行为）的影响，发现多样性偏移主导了所有偏移类型，并评估了28种域泛化方法，结果表明现有方法仅略优于经验风险最小化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAR领域虽然进展显著，但设备异构性和上下文变化导致的数据多样性问题仍未解决，现有文献对分布偏移的具体影响及其导致的域泛化问题理解不足。

Method: 系统评估4种分布偏移（设备类型、传感器位置、采样率、用户行为）的影响；引入统一的HAR分布偏移基准；全面评估28种域泛化方法的性能。

Result: 多样性偏移主导所有偏移类型，表明存在跨域不共享的独特特征；28种域泛化方法仅略微优于经验风险最小化基线，说明现有方法在HAR域泛化上效果有限。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统探索传感器HAR中针对特定分布偏移的域泛化和域适应问题，并开源了基准平台和数据集以推动后续研究。

Abstract: While the field of Human Activity Recognition (HAR) continues to draw interest from researchers and advance in important ways, some key challenges remain. One of the most difficult aspects of building HAR models that show good performance in real-world settings is dealing with data diversity from device and sensor heterogeneity, and contextual changes that are intrinsic to real-world applications. While data diversity in HAR has been well-acknowledged in the literature, there remains a gap in understanding the effect of various types of distribution shifts on HAR models and the domain generalization problem that arises. Towards that end, this paper systematically evaluates 4 different types of distribution shifts, including variations in device type, sensor placement, sampling rate, and user behavior. Quantifying their effects, we illustrate that diversity shifts predominantly define all types of shifts, indicating the existence of unique features that are not shared across different domains. We then introduce a uniform HAR-based distribution shift benchmarks and conduct a comprehensive evaluation of up to 28 domain generalization methods. Our analysis exposes the limitations of current domain generalization algorithms in achieving model generalizability, marginally outperforming the empirical risk minimization baseline. This work represents the first systematic exploration of domain generalization and adaptation concerning specific distribution shifts in sensor-based HAR, offering an open-source benchmark platform and datasets to spur further research.

</details>


### [296] [SP-Mind: An Autonomous Reasoning Agent for Spatial Proteomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24235)
*Yucheng Yuan,Yuanfeng Ji,Zhongxiao Li,Ruijiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个自主AI智能体SP-Mind，统一空间蛋白质组学分析流程，从原始多组织成像到表型发现；同时构建包含102项任务、涵盖18个类别的综合基准SP-Bench，在多项评估中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前空间蛋白质组学分析工作流碎片化，依赖专家手动编排异构工具，限制了研究的可扩展性和可重复性。

Method: SP-Mind是一个自主AI智能体，配备专家策划的生物分析技能和专用计算工具，能将自然语言查询转化为端到端分析工作流，无需任务特定微调。同时构建了SP-Bench基准进行全面评估。

Result: 在SP-Bench基准和既定下游任务上的广泛评估中，SP-Mind相比现有开源生物医学智能体基线取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SP-Mind有效统一了空间蛋白质组学分析全流程，提升了研究的可扩展性和可重复性，为精准医学中的空间蛋白质组学分析提供了自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Spatial proteomics enables single-cell-resolution characterization of protein expression within tissue architecture, playing a critical role in understanding tumor microenvironments and guiding precision medicine. However, current analysis workflows remain fragmented, requiring expert manual orchestration of heterogeneous tools and limiting research scalability and reproducibility. We present SP-Mind, the first autonomous AI agent designed to unify the spatial proteomics analysis pipeline, from raw multiplexed tissue imaging to downstream phenotype discovery. Equipped with expert-curated biological analysis skills and specialized computational tools, SP-Mind converts natural-language queries into end-to-end analytical workflows without task-specific fine-tuning. To rigorously evaluate its capabilities, we introduce SP-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark spanning diverse tissue types, comprising 102 tasks across 18 distinct categories. Through extensive evaluation on SP-Bench and established downstream tasks, SP-Mind achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to existing open-source biomedical agent baselines.

</details>


### [297] [Towards Federated Long-Tailed Graph Learning: An Energy-Guided Dual Decoupling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24237)
*Lianshuai Guo,Zhongzheng Yuan,Xunkai Li,Meixia Qu,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FedEPD框架，通过拓扑净化与语义重校准的双重解耦范式，解决联邦图学习中长尾分布导致的数据稀缺和模型偏置问题，在多个基准上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据类别常呈现长尾分布，导致全局模型偏向多数类，少数类节点因结构性隔离而被异质性主导邻域淹没。现有拓扑无关的统计补偿方法在数据稀缺下易过拟合邻域结构噪声，导致表征退化。

Method: 提出FedEPD框架：(1) 利用分布感知的Dirichlet能量剪枝过滤空间异质性边；(2) 从拓扑中心节点提取鲁棒全局原型，通过空间低通原型注入增强局部表征；(3) 两阶段交替优化策略在保护多数类决策边界的同时提升少数类精度。

Result: 在多种长尾基准上达到SOTA，Accuracy提升4.97%，Macro-F1提升5.48%。

Conclusion: FedEPD通过拓扑净化与语义重校准的解耦设计，有效缓解了联邦图学习中长尾分布导致的表示退化问题，验证了双重解耦范式在非独立同分布场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Federated Graph Learning facilitates collaborative graph modeling across distributed clients while preserving data privacy. However, real-world data categories frequently exhibit long-tailed distributions. Such statistical scarcity severely degrades performance in two ways: it biases the global model toward majority classes, and it structurally isolates minority nodes by submerging them in heterophilic, head-dominated neighborhoods. While existing methods attempt topology-agnostic statistical compensations, they often fail under data scarcity. Instead of recovering tail nodes, they overfit the structural noise from adjacent dominant classes, leading to representation degradation. To address these limitations, we propose FedEPD, a framework built on a dual decoupling paradigm that separates topological purification from semantic recalibration. Specifically, FedEPD utilizes distribution-aware Dirichlet energy pruning to filter spatial heterophilic edges. It then overcomes Non-IID distribution shifts by extracting robust global prototypes from topologically central nodes, which are incorporated into local representations via a spatial low-pass prototype injection. Furthermore, a two stage alternating optimization strategy strictly protects majority decision boundaries while improving minority accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedEPD achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse long-tailed benchmarks, yielding absolute improvements of up to 4.97% in Accuracy and 5.48% in Macro-F1.

</details>


### [298] [Probing the Misaligned Thinking Process of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24251)
*Kaiwen Zhou,Constantin Venhoff,Jonathan Michala,Xin Eric Wang,William Saunders*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过将错位行为分解为细粒度认知过程（错位指标），利用线性探针从模型内部激活中检测这些指标，以监控大语言模型的错位行为。构建了18个指标的分类体系和自动化多轮对话生成流程，在5种错位行为上实现了与强LLM评判器相当的检测性能（AUROC 0.935），并保持低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型表现出日益增长的错位行为（如策略性欺骗、懈怠、自我保存），且被部署在高风险场景中，因此亟需可靠的方法来检测此类行为以确保安全负责的使用。

Method: 1) 将错位行为分解为18个细粒度认知过程（错位指标）；2) 构建自动化、元计划引导的多轮对话生成流程来训练探针；3) 利用线性探针从模型内部激活中检测错位指标；4) 构建包含自动化行为诱发、已有错位基准和自然对话的OOD测试套件进行泛化评估。

Result: 在5种错位行为上，线性探针在OOD基准上达到0.935 AUROC，与强LLM评判器性能相当，同时在良性对话中保持低假阳性率。

Conclusion: 通过将错位分解为可检测的认知指标并使用线性探针进行内部激活分析，可以有效监控大语言模型的错位行为，为模型安全提供了一种可解释、低成本的检测方案。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit a growing range of misaligned behaviors such as strategic deception, sandbagging, and self-preservation. As they are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, it is critical to reliably detect such behaviors to ensure safe and responsible use. In this work, we propose to monitor misalignment by decomposing it into fine-grained cognitive processes -- misalignment indicators -- and detecting their presence in a model's internal activations via linear probes. We develop a taxonomy of 18 indicators spanning different misaligned behaviors, paired with an automated, meta-plan-guided pipeline that generates multi-turn training conversations. To rigorously evaluate generalization, we construct an out-of-distribution suite combining automated behavioral elicitation, established misalignment benchmarks, and natural benign conversations. Across 5 misaligned behaviors, our probes match a strong LLM judge with 0.935 AUROC on out-of-distribution benchmarks while keeping a low false positive rate on benign traffic. We further perform in-depth analysis to understand the probes and the model's internal representations of misalignment indicators.

</details>


### [299] [Tractable Reasoning and Conjunctive Query Answering for Defeasible DL-Lite under Rational Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24279)
*Giovanni Casini,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Rational Closure在DL-Lite轻量级描述逻辑核心和Horn变体中的应用，提出了一种基于现有经典推理器的插件架构，实现了在RC下高效进行实例检测和合取查询回答，且仅需极小的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rational Closure是处理可废止知识的经典非单调推理形式，但此前尚未在DL-Lite轻量级描述逻辑家族的核心及Horn变体中得到充分研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，并探索高效的实现方案。

Method: 提出了一种插件架构，该架构建立在现有标准经典推理器之上，通过最小化额外计算开销，将Rational Closure机制集成到DL-Lite的推理和合取查询回答中。

Result: 证明了在Rational Closure下，针对DL-Lite的推理（实例检测）和合取查询回答可以通过所提出的插件架构高效完成，计算开销极小。

Conclusion: Rational Closure可有效应用于DL-Lite家族，且借助插件架构能兼容现有经典推理器，实现高效的非单调推理和查询回答。

Abstract: In Description Logics (DLs), reasoning under Rational Closure (RC) is a well-known and widely accepted non-monotonic formalism to handle defeasible knowledge. In this paper, we study the application of RC to the core and horn variants of the DL-Lite family of lightweight description logics. We analyze both entitlement (instance checking) and Conjunctive Query (CQ) answering under RC. Our main contribution is providing a plug-in architecture that builds upon existing standard classical reasoners, establishing that reasoning and CQ answering under RC for DL-Lite can be done efficiently with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [300] [LemonHarness Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24311)
*Kailong Ren,Fubo Sun,Jiachen Liu,Liu Yang,Zimo Yin,Jiaying Li,Congli Yin,Ming He,Yu Huo,Jiawei Liu,Zeping Chen,Yubin Huangfu,Ronghua Li,Yixuan Wu,Xing Su,Yanzhi Xu,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao,Lei Zhang,Xiaohui Geng,Jianping Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LemonHarness，一个用于长周期Agent的集成执行框架，通过建立显式执行边界、结构化工具接口、可复用规则知识库和时序感知执行机制，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到84.49%-86.52%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent在长周期任务中缺少显式的工作空间边界，状态变更操作（文件写入、临时工件生成等）分散在文件系统中难以追踪，导致Agent难以感知实际状态变化。

Method: 1) 建立显式执行边界，将模型调用、工具执行和规则知识置于统一受控边界内；2) 通过结构化工具接口执行状态变更操作，并将执行反馈记录为观察供后续模型决策；3) 引入可复用规则知识库，将重复执行规则和验收标准转化为运行时知识；4) 时序感知执行机制，向模型暴露已用和剩余预算，优化探索、实现和验证之间的平衡。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0上：LemonHarness_GPT-5.3-CodeX在445次试验中达到84.49%准确率；搭配更强GPT-5.5骨干网后，平均准确率提升至86.52%。

Conclusion: 统一的运行时边界、可调用的规则知识和时序感知执行能够提升长周期Agent执行的稳定性。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents are applied to longer tasks, they increasingly modify workspace state across multiple rounds of iteration. However, agents typically observe only tool outputs and log fragments, while the actual state changes occur in the file system. Without explicit workspace boundaries, state-changing operations such as file writes and temporary artifact generation may scatter changes across paths. Over time, these weakly constrained changes accumulate, making states such as modified files difficult to track. This paper presents LemonHarness, an integrated execution framework for long-horizon agents. LemonHarness establishes an explicit execution boundary by constraining state-changing operations within a clearly defined workspace and bringing model invocation, tool execution, and rule knowledge within a single controlled boundary. State-changing operations, including file writes, dependency installation, and temporary artifact creation, are executed through structured tool interfaces, with execution feedback recorded as observations available to subsequent model decisions. The system also introduces a reusable rule knowledge base, which turns recurring execution rules and acceptance criteria into runtime knowledge. LemonHarness further adds a time-aware execution mechanism that exposes elapsed and remaining budget to the model, so it can rebalance exploration, implementation, and validation effort as time pressure shifts and avoid timeouts from long waits or excessive verification. On Terminal-Bench 2.0, LemonHarness_GPT-5.3-CodeX reached 84.49% accuracy over 445 trials; pairing the same framework with the stronger GPT-5.5 backbone raised the average accuracy to 86.52% across five jobs. The results suggest that a unified runtime boundary, callable rule knowledge, and time-aware execution can improve the stability of long-horizon agent execution.

</details>


### [301] [Prob-BBDM: a Probabilistic Brownian Bridge Diffusion Model for MRI sequence image-to-image translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24313)
*Martin Valls,Pascal Bourdon,Christine Fernandez-Maloigne,Guillaume Herpe,David Helbert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于布朗桥扩散模型（BBDM）的概率性图像翻译模型（Prob-BBDM），用于从2D轴向切片合成MRI序列，仅需4步扩散即可实现高质量、高效率、可泛化的医学图像合成，在BraTS 2021数据集上达到88.46% SSIM和26.09 dB PSNR，且合成切片在肿瘤分割任务中取得88.71% Dice分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态医学影像分析对优化检查质量至关重要，但获取多种成像模态在临床中资源消耗大且耗时，尤其是3D成像。现有方法难以在效率和合成质量之间取得平衡，亟需一种高效且高质量的图像翻译方法。

Method: 提出Prob-BBDM模型，基于布朗桥扩散模型（BBDM）实现图像到图像的翻译；集成变分编码器引导的扩散机制，利用概率图像分布增强合成质量；从2D轴向切片合成MRI序列，扩散过程仅需4步。

Result: 在BraTS 2021数据集上，多任务翻译中达到最高88.46% SSIM和26.09 dB PSNR，优于基线方法；仅需4步扩散；在外部第三方数据集上验证了泛化能力；合成切片用于肿瘤分割取得88.71% Dice分数和3.49 mm HD95。

Conclusion: Prob-BBDM实现了高质量、高效且可泛化的MRI合成，合成切片保留了关键诊断信息，为医学图像翻译提供了有前景的方案。

Abstract: AI-driven image-to-image synthesis is rapidly advancing, with growing applications in medical imaging. Multi-modal image analysis plays a crucial role in optimizing examination quality, yet acquiring multiple imaging modalities in clinical settings remains resource-intensive and time-consuming, especially for 3D imaging. To address this challenge, we propose a novel image-to-image translation model based on Brownian Bridge Diffusion Models (BBDM), which synthesizes magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) sequences from 2D axial slices. Our approach integrates a variational encoder-guided diffusion mechanism, leveraging probabilistic image distributions to enhance synthesis quality. Evaluated on the BraTS 2021 dataset, our Probabilistic-BBDM (Prob-BBDM) achieves superior performance across multiple translation tasks, reaching up to 88.46% SSIM and 26.09 dB PSNR, with consistent improvements over baselines. Notably, our diffusion process requires only 4 steps, making it computationally efficient while maintaining high-quality synthesis. To further validate generalizability, we test Prob-BBDM on an external third-party dataset, demonstrating consistent performance across domains. Additionally, we assess the clinical utility of the synthesized slices by using them as input to a pre-trained segmentation model. Tumor segmentation yields a Dice score of 88.71% and an HD95 of 3.49 mm, confirming that the synthesized slices preserve critical diagnostic information. These results highlight the potential of Prob-BBDM for high-quality, efficient, and generalizable MRI synthesis, offering a promising step toward improved medical image translation.

</details>


### [302] [MVG-KAN: Multi-View Geo-Wind Guided KAN for PM$_{2.5}$ Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24347)
*Cheng Huang,Muyao Guan,Jairus Yougui Railey,Ning Xu,Honghui Xu,Changjiang Zhang,Zhen Zhang,Shiqing Zhang,Cong Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MVG-KAN模型，通过多视角（局部周期性、站点残差动态、气象引导的空间扩散）进行PM2.5短期预测，结合地理-风向图与时间KAN网络，提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空预测方法（仅基于距离、相关性或纯自适应图）难以充分表征PM2.5演变中多因素耦合的异质性，尤其是风向依赖的污染物传输。

Method: 1) 周期-残差预测骨干分离稳定日/周模式与非周期残差；2) 构建Geo-Wind图，融合地理距离衰减与风向风速感知的污染物传输先验；3) 引入时间KAN残差头（TKAN），对去周期化PM2.5残差与历史多污染物序列进行非线性自回归校正。

Result: 通过多视角建模（局部周期性、站点残差动态、气象引导空间扩散），有效提升PM2.5短期预测性能。

Conclusion: MVG-KAN通过物理启发的有向图先验与KAN时序建模的协同，更好地表征PM2.5演变中的异构因素，优于传统方法。

Abstract: Accurate short-term PM$_{2.5}$ forecasting is important for public health protection, air-quality early warning, and urban environmental management. However, PM$_{2.5}$ variation is driven by multiple coupled factors, including stable periodic changes induced by human activities and meteorological regularity, station-specific short-term concentration evolution, and meteorology-driven pollutant dispersion among monitoring stations. Existing spatio-temporal forecasting methods may capture station relationships to some extent, but distance-only, correlation-based, or purely adaptive graphs are often insufficient to comprehensively represent these heterogeneous factors, especially wind-direction-dependent pollutant transport. To address this problem, we propose a Multi-View Geo-Wind Guided KAN model for PM$_{2.5}$ forecasting, named \textbf{MVG-KAN}, which models station-level PM$_{2.5}$ evolution from three complementary views: local periodic regularity, station-wise residual temporal dynamics, and meteorological-environment-guided spatial dispersion. Specifically, the periodic-residual forecasting backbone first separates stable daily and weekly patterns from non-periodic residual variations. A Geo-Wind Graph is constructed by combining geographic distance decay with wind-direction- and wind-speed-aware transport, providing a lightweight physically motivated directed spatial prior for residual propagation among stations. In addition, a temporal Kolmogorov-Arnold network (TKAN) residual head is then introduced to learn station-wise nonlinear autoregressive correction from de-periodized PM$_{2.5}$ residuals and historical multi-pollutant sequences, thereby enhancing the modeling of local residual inertia and pollutant co-variation.

</details>


### [303] [Accelerating Disaggregated RL for Visual Generative LLMs with Diffusion-Based Parallelism and Trainer-Assisted Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24369)
*Sijie Wang,Zhengyu Qing,Zhiqiang Tan,Yiming Yin,Yeqing Zhang,Yaoyuan Wang,Qiang Wang,Xiaowen Chu,Shaohuai Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DigenRL 提出了一种针对扩散生成式大语言模型的解耦强化学习框架，通过生成轴流水线、时间步并行、弹性训练器辅助生成和紧一步约束异步策略，在 16-32 GPU 上实现 1.56-2.10 倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习系统（如 veRL-Omni）依赖共置执行，导致扩散生成式 LLM 的异构部署受限、资源分配不灵活且无法独立扩展，缺乏高效的解耦 RL 系统。

Method: 提出 DigenRL 解耦框架，包含：(1) 生成轴流水线 (GAP) 和时间步并行 (TSP) 实现细粒度管线化；(2) 弹性训练器辅助生成 (TAG) 让训练 GPU 动态辅助执行推演生成；(3) 紧一步约束异步策略利用尾气泡。

Result: 在 16-32 GPU 上使用 HunyuanVideo-13B、Wan2.1-14B、FLUX.1-12B 和 QwenImage-20B 模型实验，相比 veRL-Omni 和 GenRL 实现 1.56-2.10 倍吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: DigenRL 证明了解耦架构在扩散生成式 LLM 强化学习中的有效性，实现了灵活的资源分配、异构 GPU 支持和高效任务调度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant post-training paradigm, driving the emergence of high-performance RL systems such as veRL for autoregressive large language models (LLMs). In parallel, diffusion-oriented RL algorithms, e.g., DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, have rapidly expanded the scope of RL from language reasoning to diffusion-based visual and flow-based generation. However, efficient RL systems for diffusion generative LLMs remain underexplored. Existing implementations, e.g., veRL-Omni, still rely on colocated execution, which simplifies synchronization but couples rollout and training resources, limits heterogeneous deployment, and constrains independent scaling.
  To this end, we introduce DigenRL, a disaggregated RL framework for diffusion-based generative LLMs that supports flexible resource allocation, accommodates heterogeneous GPUs, and facilitates efficient task scheduling. To maximally reduce the execution bubbles in the disaggregated architecture, we propose: 1) a generation-axis pipeline (GAP) and time-step parallelism (TSP) in the diffusion architecture to enable finer-grained pipelining between rollout and training; 2) an elastic trainer-assisted generation (TAG) approach to enable the trainer GPU resources to dynamically assist in executing rollout generations; and 3) a tightly one-step constrained asynchronous strategy to further utilize the tail bubble in the pipeline. Extensive experiments are conducted on three hardware testbeds with 16-32 GPUs using HunyuanVideo-13B, Wan2.1-14B, FLUX.1-12B, and QwenImage-20B generative models. Experimental results show that DigenRL achieves 1.56-2.10x throughput improvements over state-of-the-art diffusion RL systems, veRL-Omni and GenRL.

</details>


### [304] [When Helpfulness Overrides Causal Caution: Context-Dependent Suppression and Recovery in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24370)
*Hiroshi Okumura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了LLMs在从学术语境转向实践咨询语境时会系统性抑制"因果谨慎"（Causal Caution）——即在缺乏充分经验证据时克制不做因果判断的倾向。在480次试验中，四款高性能LLM在学术语境下的因果谨慎维持率达91.7-100%，但在实践咨询语境下骤降至6.7-18.3%。一个简单的自我纠正提示即可显著恢复该能力（71.4-100%），表明该抑制是语境驱动的表达变化而非能力缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准研究主要评估LLMs的因果推理能力，却忽视了更基础的认知维度：因果谨慎——即当经验证据不足时主动避免做出因果判断的倾向。随着LLMs越来越多地应用于商业和政策决策支持场景，这种"不判断"的能力与"判断"的能力同样重要，但尚未被系统研究。

Method: 采用基于Pearl因果层次（PCH）的评估量表，在480次试验中对Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Opus 4.7、GPT 5.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro四种高性能LLM进行系统测试。实验设计包括学术语境和实践咨询语境对比、不同提示类型（推荐型vs.解释型）分析，以及自我纠正提示干预。统计方法采用Fisher精确检验和McNemar检验。

Result: 学术语境下因果谨慎维持率91.7-100%，实践咨询语境下降至6.7-18.3%（p < .001）。在要求具体建议或解释性理由的实践提示中，仅0.5%的响应维持了因果谨慎。自我纠正提示可将其恢复至71.4-100%（p < .001）。

Conclusion: 因果谨慎的抑制反映的是语境依赖的表达变化而非能力限制，源于"有帮助性"导向的响应模式。这提示组织治理中应设计多智能体架构，将提案生成与因果审计分离，作为有前景的治理方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into decision-support roles in business and policy contexts. While prior benchmark studies have primarily evaluated LLMs' causal reasoning capabilities, a more fundamental epistemic dimension has been overlooked: Causal Caution, defined as the propensity to refrain from causal judgment when empirical evidence is insufficient. This study examines the systematic suppression of Causal Caution that occurs when LLMs shift from academic to practical advisory contexts. Using an evaluation rubric inspired by Pearl's Causal Hierarchy (the PCH score), we conducted experiments on four high-performance LLMs -- Claude Sonnet 4.6, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT 5.5, and Gemini 3.1 Pro -- across 480 trials. Causal Caution maintenance rates were 91.7--100.0% in academic contexts but dropped to 6.7--18.3% in practical advisory contexts (Fisher's exact test, p < .001 across all models). Furthermore, when restricted to practical prompts requesting concrete recommendations or explanatory rationales, only 1 of 200 responses (0.5%) maintained Causal Caution. A brief self-correction prompt -- "Please reconsider this judgment from the perspective of causal relationships" -- restored the expression of Causal Caution to maintenance rates of 71.4--100.0% (McNemar's test, p < .001 across all models). These results suggest that helpfulness-oriented response patterns may suppress the expression of Causal Caution in practical advisory contexts, with important implications for organizational governance. The findings indicate that this suppression reflects context-dependent variation in expression rather than an underlying capability limitation, suggesting that multi-agent architectures that separate proposal generation from causal auditing may offer a promising governance design.

</details>


### [305] [PHANTOM: A Large-Scale Dataset of Multimodal Adversarial Attacks for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24388)
*Simone Gallivanone,Hossein Khodadadi,Mauro Dore,Mauro Medda,Nicola Franco*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个大规模、开源的视觉-语言模型对抗攻击数据集，包含47,524个对抗样本，覆盖10个高级类别和55个子类别的有害意图，旨在降低对抗性研究门槛，促进VLM安全性的可复现和全面评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成大量对抗攻击的计算成本高、复杂度大，现有基准覆盖不足，研究社区难以获取多样化的对抗数据，需要提供一个大规模、开放、标准化的数据集来系统性评估VLM的鲁棒性和安全性。

Method: 从多个现有基准整合并扩展，覆盖10个高级类别和55个子类别，共7,826个意图；使用最新的SOTA攻击策略生成47,524个对抗样本；并引入额外的类别以扩大覆盖范围。

Result: 构建了一个包含47,524个对抗样本的大规模数据集，涵盖10个高级类别和55个子类别，共计7,826个意图，并增加了新的类别以拓展覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 该数据集能够帮助研究者和从业者系统评估VLM的鲁棒性和安全性，微调攻击生成模型，开发或压力测试防御机制，推动对抗性研究的可复现性和可比性。

Abstract: We introduce a large-scale, open-source dataset of pre-generated adversarial attacks for vision-language models (VLMs). The dataset is designed to be diverse, representative, and practical, extending existing benchmarks by covering 10 high-level categories and 55 subcategories of harmful intents. Our primary goal is to make adversarial data accessible to the research community, given the computational cost and complexity of generating large numbers of attacks. The dataset comprises 47 524 adversarial samples, generated using state-of-the-art attack strategies from recent literature. Our work complements existing efforts by consolidating and extending prior benchmarks from multiple established sources, resulting in 7 826 intents, and introduce an additional category to broaden coverage. This provides realistic evaluation resources for studying model robustness and alignment. Our dataset intends to enable researchers and practitioners to systematically evaluate the robustness and safety of VLMs, fine-tune attack-generation models, and develop or stress-test defensive guardrails under diverse adversarial conditions. By releasing this resource, we aim to lower the barrier to adversarial research and foster more reproducible, comprehensive, and comparable evaluations of VLM safety.

</details>


### [306] [Age of LLM: A Strategic 1v1 Benchmark for Reasoning, Diplomacy and Reliability of Large Language Models under Fog of War](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24391)
*Arnaud Ricci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Age of LLM基准，在13x7网格中两个LLM进行回合制1v1对战，引入战争迷雾、完整外交和严格JSON可靠性维度，评测15个推理模型后发现核 rush 主导、外交几乎不达成、非法动作反映信念追踪，以及可靠性可能与获胜存在弱关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开基准存在数据污染问题，缺乏对LLM在对抗性不确定性下推理能力的系统性评测，需要一种能同时评估LLM战略决策、信念追踪、欺骗和可靠性的动态评估框架。

Method: 构建13x7网格的回合制1v1对战基准，包含战争迷雾、完整外交（消息、停火、最后通牒、秘密铀）和严格JSON格式可靠性维度；采用私有引擎、随机地图种子和对手匹配以缓解数据污染；仅提供规则提示（无建造顺序建议）；对15个推理模型进行54场比赛、5,258个动作的评测。

Result: (1) 核 rush 主导（规则一致子语料库78%，全语料库85%），原因在于秘密同步发射机制下的机械性执行而非认知威慑失败；(2) 军事征服罕见但更快（12.3 vs 18.9回合）；(3) 外交频繁但几乎从不达成；(4) ~58%非法动作为迷雾/状态错误，非法动作率可作为信念追踪的度量；(5) 可靠性可能与获胜存在弱关联（探索性发现）。

Conclusion: 语料库规模小、不平衡且未交换双方，排名仅为初步描述性视图；但回合级动作和消息轨迹为研究LLM在对抗性不确定性下的推理提供了新视角，包括信念追踪、自发欺骗和每模型认知"人格"，可作为未来研究方向。发布回放格式、等距查看器和所有回放，引擎源码可申请获取。

Abstract: We introduce Age of LLM, a turn-based 1v1 benchmark in which two LLMs face off on a 13x7 grid to destroy the enemy base. Three stressors are deliberate: fog of war, full diplomacy (messages, ceasefires, ultimatums; uranium kept secret), and a reliability dimension where every turn must follow a strict JSON schema and an illegal action is silently discarded. The engine is private and each match uses a fresh random map seed and opponent, mitigating the data contamination that affects public benchmarks. Models receive a (near) rule-only prompt with no build-order advice (two tactical seed phrases were present during data collection; see Section 2.7). We benchmark 15 reasoning models across 54 matches and 5,258 actions. Findings: (1) the nuclear rush dominates (78% on the rules-coherent v0.11+ sub-corpus; 85% corpus-wide) with a sole-launcher signature that is largely mechanical under secret-simultaneous launch rules, not a cognitive deterrence failure; (2) military conquest is rare but faster (12.3 vs 18.9 turns); (3) diplomacy is prolific yet almost never consummated; (4) ~58% of illegal actions are fog/state errors, making the illegal-action rate a measure of belief-tracking; (5) -- the least established, and the only one we label exploratory -- a weak link associates reliability with winning. The corpus is small, unbalanced and not side-swapped, so the ranking is a preliminary descriptive view, not a contribution. Beyond ranking, the turn-by-turn traces of actions and messages make the corpus a lens on how LLMs reason under adversarial uncertainty -- their belief-tracking, spontaneous deception, and per-model cognitive "personas" -- which we frame as a future research direction. We release the replay format, an isometric viewer and all replays; engine source on request.

</details>


### [307] [ATRIA: Adaptive Traceable ECG Reporting with Iterative Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24392)
*Donggyun Hong,Kyuhwan Lee,Junmyung Kwon,Yong-Yeon Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATRIA是一个多智能体ECG报告系统，模拟临床医生的迭代工作流程，实现报告声明与证据的绑定、未支持语句的标记、会话中上下文整合以及逐项验证与修订，克服了端到端生成和单次传递系统的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG报告生成方法存在两个问题：端到端融合方法错误传播且无法分阶段干预，而基于智能体的系统虽然解耦了任务但仍是单次传递，无法回溯之前输出。临床ECG报告实际上需要迭代式、逐步整合上下文和双向编辑的工作流程。

Method: 提出ATRIA多智能体系统，核心设计包括：(1) 将每个报告声明与其支持证据绑定；(2) 标记缺乏证据支持的语句；(3) 在会话中整合额外上下文；(4) 允许临床医生逐项验证和修订单个发现。系统使用已在临床使用的ECG分析模型以保证临床可信度，并作为云Web服务部署。

Result: 通过四个交互案例展示了ATRIA的功能，并提供了在线演示和视频。

Conclusion: ATRIA通过模拟临床医生迭代式工作流程，实现了可验证、可修订的ECG报告生成，且基于已投入临床使用的模型和云部署架构，具备即时部署能力。

Abstract: Existing ECG report generation is tightly coupled -- interpretation and reporting fused end-to-end, so errors propagate without stage-level recourse -- while agent-based systems decouple tasks but remain single-pass, never revisiting earlier outputs. Clinical ECG reporting instead unfolds iteratively, requiring progressive context integration and bidirectional editing. We present \textsc{ATRIA}, a multi-agent ECG reporting system that mirrors the clinician's iterative workflow: it binds every report claim to its supporting evidence, flags statements unsupported by that evidence, incorporates additional context mid-session, and lets clinicians verify and revise individual findings rather than accept one opaque output. Because its agents use ECG analysis models already in clinical use, the underlying findings are clinically trustworthy; and as a cloud-based web service, \textsc{ATRIA} is ready for immediate deployment. We demonstrate \textsc{ATRIA} through four interaction cases, with a live demo and video available.

</details>


### [308] [Cycle-Consistent Neural Explanation of Formal Verification Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24414)
*Andoni Rodriguez,Alberto Pozanco,Daniel Borrajo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种循环一致性神经架构，用于将形式化验证证书自动翻译成忠实、可理解的自然语言解释，在金融合规领域的420个测试证书上达到90.0%的循环验证可靠性，比多LLM少样本基线高出13.9个百分点，同时推理速度快860倍且成本为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证产生的机器可检查证书对于非专业利益相关者来说难以理解，缺乏透明性和可解释性。

Method: 提出循环一致性神经架构：前向网络NN1将证书映射为解释，逆向网络NN2从解释重建证书，符号验证器闭环提供可微的忠实度代理；指针生成机制确保从证书中直接复制状态名称实现词汇接地。

Result: 在420个测试证书（覆盖6种验证方法、YES/NO两种裁决）上，结合混合推理时路由策略达到90.0%循环验证可靠性，超越16种LLM组合的最佳基线76.1%达13.9个百分点；12个裁决/种类类别中胜出10个，3个类别达100%可靠性；推理速度185ms vs 160s（860倍加速）。

Conclusion: 训练专用化方法在结构化证书解释任务上优于通用大语言模型提示方法，同时消除了云端推理的部署约束，实现离线、确定性和零推理成本。

Abstract: Formal verification produces machine-checkable certificates that attest to the satisfaction or violation of temporal properties, yet these certificates remain opaque to non-specialist stakeholders. We propose a cycle-consistent neural architecture that generates faithful natural language explanations of verification certificates. A forward network NN1 maps certificates to explanations, and an inverse network NN2 reconstructs certificates from explanations; a symbolic verifier closes the loop, providing a differentiable faithfulness proxy. A pointer-generator mechanism ensures lexical grounding by copying state names directly from the certificate. We evaluate on 420 test certificates spanning six verification methods (bounded proof, k-induction, inductive invariant, lasso, reachability, witness pair) in both YES and NO verdict variants, drawn from a financial compliance domain with 207 named states. Our trained architecture, combined with a hybrid inference-time routing strategy, achieves 90.0% cycle-verified soundness, surpassing a multi- LLM few-shot baseline (76.1% for the best of 16 LLM combinations across four frontier models) by 13.9 percentage points. The neural model wins on 10 of 12 verdict/kind categories, with three categories reaching 100% soundness. The architecture offers 860x faster inference (185 ms vs. 160 s per certificate for the full multi-LLM baseline), offline operation, deterministic outputs, and zero per-inference cost. These results demonstrate that trained specialization outperforms general-purpose LLM prompting for structured certificate explanation, while eliminating the deployment constraints of cloud-based inference.

</details>


### [309] [Agentic AI for Bilevel Long-Term Optimization of Policy-Driven Physical Layer Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24416)
*Bingnan Xiao,Chenhao Yang,Wei Ni,Xin Wang,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic-LTPO，一个基于智能体AI的嵌套双层优化框架，用于自适应物理层问题配置，在无蜂窝MIMO波束成形场景下实现长期性能优化，相比传统方法提升57.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络运营策略的动态变化、服务需求的多样性以及严格的实时约束，使传统基于固定目标和约束的方法失效，亟需一种能自适应长期性能优化的框架。

Method: 提出Agentic-LTPO双层优化框架：上层采用智能体AI，结合检索增强的经验验证机制，将运营策略、环境摘要和历史经验转化为结构化的下层优化问题配置；下层求解更新后的问题，做出实时物理层决策。在无蜂窝MIMO波束成形场景中，上层设计多智能体决策过程，下层采用闭式波束成形器。

Result: 实验表明，Agentic-LTPO对动态运营策略具有强适应性，相比传统方法，系统长期性能提升57.2%。

Conclusion: 智能体驱动的双层优化框架能有效应对动态网络环境，为自适应物理层优化提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Network operators' changing policies, service requirements, and stringent real-time constraints render existing methods designed with fixed objectives and constraints ineffective. This paper presents Agentic long-term performance optimization (Agentic-LTPO), a nested bilevel optimization framework that can be applied to adaptive physical layer problem configuration. The key idea is to employ agentic AI to generate upper-level configurations in a bilevel optimization structure, where evolving operator policies, environment summaries, and historical experiences are translated into structured lower-level optimization problem configurations. The lower level solves the problems with updated configurations for real-time physical-layer decisions. Considering cell-free MIMO beamforming as a use case, we embody Agentic-LTPO by designing a new multi-agent decision process with retrieval-augmented experience-based verification in the upper level, together with a closed-form beamformer in the lower level. Experiments demonstrate that Agentic-LTPO exhibits strong adaptability to dynamic operator policies and effectively enhances the system's long-term performance by 57.2% compared to traditional methods.

</details>


### [310] [Can Aggregate Invariants Accelerate Continuous Subgraph Matching? Limits, Laws, and a Dynamic Spectral Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24421)
*Minghao Chen,Jiale Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了谱过滤技术能否加速动态图上的连续子图匹配（CSM），通过严格的理论分析和实验验证，提出了轻量级局部谱索引维护方法，在候选剪枝和更新枚举跳过方面取得显著效果，同时揭示了聚合测试仅加速候选集相关操作而非邻接引导探索的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索静态子图匹配中有效的谱过滤技术能否迁移到动态图上的连续子图匹配场景，以加速这一更具挑战性的问题。

Method: 首先理论分析了懒惰维护谱边界的不可行性并刻画了最紧的安全规则；然后提出选择性精确维护策略，利用剪枝效用与重计算成本的反相关关系，仅对触摸顶点的小邻域谱进行微秒级重计算；最后集成到解耦的CSM基准中，与无谱的对照组进行比较。

Result: 谱测试可移除高达51%的候选集或安全跳过47%的更新枚举，但枚举中间结果保持不变；在两个引擎、四个真实图、两种流类型和77个求解查询上验证；构建的半径分层工作负载确认了异常检测能力（-99.9%中间结果，748倍加速）。

Conclusion: 聚合测试仅加速与候选集规模相关的操作（如构建、列表扫描），而不加速邻接引导的探索；提出中间结果不变性方法论用于评估CSM过滤器，并发布了可复用的动态局部谱索引。

Abstract: Spectral filtering recently delivered substantial pruning for \emph{static} subgraph matching: Laplacian interlacing rejects candidates whose neighborhoods cannot host the query. We study whether such aggregate structural tests can accelerate \emph{continuous} subgraph matching (CSM) over dynamic graphs, and answer in three parts. First, lazily maintained spectral bounds are infeasible exactly where spectral pruning has value: we characterize the tightest safe rule over a formalized perturbation relaxation and show that even it loses essentially all pruning power within four touching updates. Second, exact maintenance is affordable when selective: pruning utility and recomputation cost are anti-correlated across vertices -- hubs provably never prune -- so recomputing small-neighborhood spectra on touch sustains exact local spectra at microseconds per update, complete by construction. Third, integrated into a decoupled CSM benchmark against an identical-minus-spectra control, the tests remove up to $51\%$ of candidates or safely skip up to $47\%$ of update enumerations, yet enumeration intermediates remain unchanged -- beyond the gates' skipped first-level bindings, typically zero -- across two engines, four real graphs, two stream types, and $77$ solved queries; a constructed radius-stratified workload confirms the instrument detects the exception when one exists ($-99.9\%$ intermediates, $748\times$ faster). Aggregate tests accelerate what scales with candidate sets -- construction, list scans -- never adjacency-guided exploration. We distill an intermediate-invariance methodology for evaluating CSM filters and release a reusable dynamic local-spectra index.

</details>


### [311] [ReM-MoA: Reasoning Memory Sustains Mixture-of-Agents Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24437)
*Heng Ping,Arijit Bhattacharjee,Peiyu Zhang,Shixuan Li,Wei Yang,Ali Jannesari,Nesreen Ahmed,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReM-MoA通过引入排名推理记忆和策展多样化记忆路由机制，解决了现有MoA架构随深度增加性能退化的问题，实现了跨层推理记忆的可扩展多智能体推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mixture-of-Agents架构在增加深度时会出现性能退化、早期平台期或饱和现象，无法持续获得增益，亟需一种能够随深度扩展而持续提升性能的机制。

Method: (1) 排名推理记忆：持久存储并排名所有层的推理轨迹，通过比较性评审智能体进行排序；(2) 策展多样化记忆路由：让不同智能体接触成功与失败轨迹的不同组合，保持探索多样性同时传播高质量推理；(3) 可选的跨域评审模型蒸馏流程，通过前沿模型监督提升排名质量。

Result: 在数学、形式逻辑、代码、知识和常识五个推理基准上，ReM-MoA在深度和宽度扩展上均一致优于先前MoA变体，且优势随深度增加而扩大。

Conclusion: 结构化跨层推理记忆是实现可扩展多智能体推理的关键缺失机制。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) architectures improve inference-time scaling by organizing multiple LLM agents into layered reasoning pipelines. However, existing MoA variants fail to sustain gains as depth increases, exhibiting degradation, early plateauing, or saturation. We propose ReM-MoA, a memory-augmented MoA framework that sustains scaling through two mechanisms: (1) a Ranked Reasoning Memory that persistently stores and ranks reasoning traces from all layers using a comparative Reviewer Agent, and (2) a Curated Diversified Memory Routing scheme that exposes different agents to distinct combinations of successful and failed traces, preserving exploration diversity while propagating high-quality reasoning. We further introduce an optional multi-domain Reviewer distillation pipeline that improves ranking quality through frontier-model supervision. Across five reasoning benchmarks spanning math, formal logic, code, knowledge, and commonsense, ReM-MoA consistently outperforms prior MoA variants across both depth and width scaling, and its advantage widens with depth, establishing structured cross-layer reasoning memory as a key missing mechanism for scalable multi-agent inference.

</details>


### [312] [Bayesian control for coding agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24453)
*Theodore Papamarkou,Vladislav Smirnov,Viktor Mazanov,Artem Vazhentsev,Preslav Nakov,Timothy Baldwin,Artem Shelmanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将代码代理的编排问题形式化为成本敏感的序列假设检验，通过贝叶斯控制器动态决定证据收集、候选优化、验证或停止，在六个生成器和九个基准上验证了其在昂贵验证场景下的有效性，并证明了信念状态可提供更优的不确定性量化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码代理的编排器通常使用固定规则且忽略不确定性，缺乏在成本约束下动态决策的能力。

Method: 将编排问题建模为成本敏感的序列假设检验，使用贝叶斯控制器维护对候选正确性的信念，动态决策是否收集更多证据、优化候选、验证或停止。

Result: 在六个生成器和九个编码基准上，贝叶斯控制在验证成本高且批评者信息丰富但不完美时最为有效；信念状态提供的不确定性量化优于令牌概率和原始工具成功基线。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯控制方法在代码代理编排中具有显著优势，特别是在昂贵验证场景下，同时信念状态可用于提升不确定性量化性能。

Abstract: Modern coding agents pair LLM generators with various tools, including cheap diagnostics and expensive verifiers. The tool-use decisions are typically governed by orchestrators that often use fixed rules and ignore uncertainty. We formulate orchestration as cost-sensitive sequential hypothesis testing: a Bayesian controller maintains a belief over candidate correctness and dynamically decides whether to gather more evidence, refine the candidate, verify it, or stop. Across six generators and nine coding benchmarks, Bayesian control proves to be most valuable when verification is costly and critics are informative but imperfect. Beyond control, the belief state yields an interpretable correctness score that outperforms token-probability and raw tool-success baselines for uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [313] [CompressKV: Semantic-Retrieval-Guided KV-Cache Compression for Resource-Efficient Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24467)
*Xiaolin Lin,Jingcun Wang,Olga Kondrateva,Yiyu Shi,Bing Li,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CompressKV是一种面向GQA-based LLMs的资源高效KV缓存压缩框架，通过识别语义检索头（SRHs）来选择性保留关键token的KV对，并跨层分配缓存预算，在仅使用3% KV缓存时保留超97%的全缓存性能，在Needle-in-a-Haystack任务中用0.7% KV存储达到90%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存驱逐方法对所有注意力头使用启发式token评分，忽略了不同注意力头的功能差异，导致关键token被错误驱逐，降低了长上下文LLM推理性能。

Method: CompressKV首先识别语义检索头（SRHs），这些头能够捕捉提示词的初始和末尾token以及语义上重要的中间上下文证据；然后利用SRHs选择需保留KV对的token；最后根据层间驱逐误差的离线估计，跨层分配缓存预算。

Result: 在LongBench上仅用3% KV缓存保留超97%全缓存性能；在Needle-in-a-Haystack上用0.7% KV存储达到90%准确率；一致优于现有KV缓存驱逐方法。

Conclusion: CompressKV在长上下文LLM推理中实现了更优的资源-性能权衡，为资源受限硬件上的可持续部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Long-context large language model (LLM) inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint and decoding cost of key-value (KV) caches, limiting sustainable deployment on resource-constrained hardware. Existing KV cache eviction methods typically apply heuristic token scoring over all heads in GQA-based LLMs. These methods ignore the different functionalities of attention heads, leading to the eviction of critical tokens and thus degrading the performance of LLMs. To address this issue, we propose CompressKV, a resource-efficient KV-cache compression framework for GQA-based LLMs. Instead of aggregating attention scores from all heads, CompressKV identifies Semantic Retrieval Heads (SRHs) that capture both the initial and final tokens of a prompt and semantically important mid-context evidence, and uses them to select tokens whose KV pairs should be retained. Furthermore, CompressKV allocates cache budgets across layers according to offline estimates of layer-wise eviction error. Experiments on LongBench and Needle-in-a-Haystack show that CompressKV consistently outperforms existing KV-cache eviction methods across memory budgets. Notably, it preserves over 97\% of full-cache performance using only 3\% of the KV cache on LongBench question-answering tasks and achieves 90\% accuracy with just 0.7\% KV storage on Needle-in-a-Haystack. These results demonstrate an improved resource--performance trade-off for long-context LLM inference. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/TUDa-HWAI/CompressKV

</details>


### [314] [The Latent Bridge: A Continuous Slow-Fast Channel for Real-Time Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24470)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种Latent Bridge方法，将慢速推理VLM（Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking，~1.5s/步）的连续残差投影到快速反应VLM（MiniCPM-o 4.5，毫秒级）的输入嵌入空间，实现无需文本中转的实时协作。在7个Atari游戏和MetaDrive驾驶任务中，Latent Bridge在规划密集型任务上显著优于纯文本桥接（MsPacman +57%，RoadRunner +28%），且增益与慢模型自身优势高度相关（r=0.93）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时通用计算机代理（尤以游戏为最苛刻场景）需在数十毫秒内作出反应，同时具备跨秒级别的规划能力。单一模型无法兼顾延迟-质量权衡：慢速推理VLM（~1.5s/响应）无法满足15Hz控制环路需求，快速反应VLM虽毫秒级响应但在规划密集型任务上表现不佳。因此需要将两种模型的优势耦合。

Method: 冻结两个规模匹配的模型（9B快速反应模型、8B慢速推理模型），仅训练通信通道。对比三种耦合方式：(1) Fast-Only (F)：纯快速模型基线；(2) Text Bridge (T)：慢模型生成文本后缀供快模型读取；(3) Latent Bridge (L)：借鉴LLaVA范式，将慢模型的残差投影到快模型的输入嵌入空间，避免文本往返延迟。在每个领域上，根据预留种子数据微调解码器。

Result: 在7个Atari游戏和MetaDrive上，Latent Bridge在所有领域匹配或超越Text Bridge：显著提升MsPacman（+57%）和RoadRunner（+28%），在其他任务上无害替代。双通道组合（Text+Latent）在RoadRunner上性能崩溃（-96%），因此仅应使用单一通道。桥接增益高度可预测：当且仅当慢推理优于快反应（T > F）时桥接有效，Latent与Text增益呈强相关（r=0.93）。MetaDrive作为受控负例，Latent Bridge确实惰性（因为Text Bridge也无增益）。

Conclusion: Latent Bridge是一种安全、高效的快慢模型耦合方式，在规划密集型任务上优于文本桥接且可作为通用替代方案。其增益可预测性（仅当慢模型本身优于快模型时有效）使得该方法部署风险可控。发布可复现的流水线和回放记录。

Abstract: A real-time agent for general computer use - with games as the most demanding case - must act within tens of milliseconds while still planning over seconds. These two regimes sit at opposite ends of the latency-quality tradeoff. A reasoning VLM (Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking) deliberates effectively but requires ~1.5 s per response - far too slow for a 15 Hz control loop. In contrast, a reactive VLM (MiniCPM-o 4.5) acts in milliseconds but underperforms on planning-heavy tasks. We couple two frozen models of matched scale (9B reactive, 8B reasoning), leaving the communication channel as the sole trainable component. The standard coupling is a Text Bridge (T): the slow model writes a suffix the fast model reads. We introduce a learned continuous Latent Bridge (L) that projects the slow model's residuals into the fast model's input-embedding space in a LLaVA-style manner, avoiding any text round-trip; both are compared against Fast-Only (F). On 7 Atari games and a driving domain (MetaDrive), tuning the action decoder per channel on held-out seeds, the Latent Bridge matches or beats the Text Bridge in every domain: it significantly improves two games (MsPacman +57%, RoadRunner +28%) and is a safe drop-in elsewhere. Combining both channels interferes destructively (RoadRunner -96%), so only one should be used. The benefit is highly predictable: the bridge helps if and only if slow reasoning already beats fast reaction (T > F) - the Latent and Text gains over Fast-Only move together at r=0.93. MetaDrive is the controlled negative, where the Latent Bridge is demonstrably inert because the Text Bridge adds no value. We release replay recordings and reproducible pipelines.

</details>


### [315] [On the Smallness of the Large Language Models Scaling Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24504)
*Sauro Succi,Peter V. Coveney,Alex Hansen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文讨论了当前大语言模型（LLMs）在能源资源方面的不可持续性问题，指出其缩放指数正显示出一个不可持续的趋势。作者证明，将这种指数的微小性归因于由于忽略无限数据极限下损失函数的非零值（“基座效应”）而产生的数值偏差，并不能消除不可持续性问题。最后，基于与流体湍流现象学模型的类比，评论了数据的平滑度（粗糙度）对缩放指数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前大语言模型应用在能源消耗方面的不可持续性，并批判性地分析了一种常见的数值修正观点（基座效应）是否能够解决该问题。

Method: 通过理论分析讨论缩放指数的意义和局限性，并引入流体湍流现象学模型进行类比分析。

Result: 指出“基座效应”并不能真正解决LLMs的能源不可持续性问题，并且数据的平滑度（粗糙度）对缩放指数有重要影响。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的缩放指数所揭示的能源不可持续性问题无法通过基座效应等数值修正来消除，而数据粗糙度的影响值得进一步关注。

Abstract: We discuss reasons why the scaling exponents of current Large Language Models (LLMs) applications are indicating an unsustainable regime in terms of energy resources. We further show that attributing the smallness of such exponents to a numerical bias due to the neglect of a non-zero value of the loss function in the limit of infinite data (``pedestal effect") does not remove the unsustainability issue. Finally, the effects of the smoothness (roughness) of the data on the scaling exponents is commented upon based on an analogy with phenomenological models of fluid turbulence.

</details>


### [316] [A specialized reasoning large language model for accelerating rare disease diagnosis: a randomized AI physician assistance trial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24510)
*Haichao Chen,Songchi Zhou,Zhengyun Zhao,Shikai Hu,Xianghong Jin,Hongwei Ji,Li He,Shuli Li,Yiming Qin,Xin Tan,Runfeng Shi,Yih Chung Tham,Jiaye Zhu,Ye Li,Ye Jin,Longhao Cao,Dawei Li,Honghan Wu,Hongqiu Gu,Guanqiao Li,Tudor Groza,Chunying Li,Dian Zeng,Weihong Yu,Gareth Baynam,Saumya Shekhar Jamuar,Min Shen,Shuyang Zhang,Bin Sheng,Sheng Yu,Tien Yin Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RaDaR是一个开源的小型推理大语言模型（32B参数），专门用于罕见病诊断。该模型使用49,170个公开的免费文本病例和104,666个合成病例进行推理增强训练。在公开基准测试和四个外部验证中心中，RaDaR在所有评估的开源模型中表现最强，包括671B参数的DeepSeek-R1。在回顾性队列中，RaDaR在61.06%的病例中先于临床怀疑识别出最终诊断，提前时间中位数为1.87个月。在随机医生辅助试验中，RaDaR辅助将医生的罕见病诊断准确率提高了21.44个百分点。合成数据消融实验表明，表型锚定的叙述为长尾罕见病提供了有用的训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病影响全球数百万患者，但由于专业临床知识的稀缺，及时诊断仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战。现有大语言模型在罕见病诊断方面存在临床可部署性不足、缺乏临床证据以及训练数据稀缺等问题。

Method: RaDaR是一个32B参数的开源推理大语言模型，采用49,170个公开免费文本病例和104,666个合成病例进行推理增强训练。开发过程中使用了表型锚定的叙述生成合成数据。

Result: RaDaR在公开基准和四个外部验证中心中表现优于包括671B DeepSeek-R1在内的所有开源模型。回顾性队列中，61.06%的病例提前于临床怀疑做出诊断（提前1.87个月）。医生辅助试验中，诊断准确率提升21.44个百分点。

Conclusion: RaDaR及其开发验证框架提供了一种可部署的罕见病推理模型和一个在数据稀缺条件下可复现的诊断AI开发框架。表型锚定的合成叙述为长尾罕见病提供了有效的训练信号，并在测试数据范围内呈现单调扩展趋势。

Abstract: Rare diseases affect millions of individuals worldwide, yet timely diagnosis remains a major public health challenge due to scarcity of specialized clinical expertise. While large language models (LLMs) show promise to support rare disease diagnosis, current models are constrained by insufficient clinical deployability, limited clinically grounded evidence, and scarcity of training data. Here we present RaDaR (Rare Disease navigatoR), an open-source, compact reasoning LLM (32B parameters) for rare disease diagnosis. RaDaR was trained with 49,170 publicly available free-text cases and 104,666 synthetic cases with reasoning-enhanced training. RaDaR showed the strongest performance among evaluated open-source models, including the 671B DeepSeek-R1, across public benchmarks and four external validation centers. In a retrospective cohort, RaDaR prioritized the final diagnosis before documented clinical suspicion in 61.06 percent of cases, corresponding to a potential lead time of 1.87 months and 50.18 percent of the within-center interval. In a randomized physician-assistance trial, RaDaR assistance improved physicians' rare-disease diagnostic accuracy by 21.44 percentage points compared with internet search alone. Synthetic-data ablations suggested that phenotype-anchored narratives provide useful training signal for long-tail rare diseases, with a monotonic scaling trend within the tested data range. Together, RaDaR and its development and validation framework provide a deployable rare-disease reasoning model and a reproducible development framework for diagnostic AI under data scarcity.

</details>


### [317] [Governed Shared Memory for Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24535)
*Yanki Margalit,Nurit Cohen-Inger,Erni Avram,Ran Taig,Oded Margalit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了多智能体LLM环境中的fleet-memory问题，识别了四种故障模式，提出了四种系统级原语并在生产服务MemClaw中实现，通过ArgusFleet测试框架评估，发现长上下文检索不足以支撑生产级多智能体记忆，需要明确的系统级抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM环境缺乏稳健的共享知识管理机制，现有方法未能系统性地识别和解决记忆共享中的故障模式。

Method: 形式化定义fleet-memory问题及四种故障模式，提出四种系统级原语（scoped retrieval, temporal supersession, provenance tracking, policy-governed memory propagation），在生产级多租户记忆服务MemClaw中实现，并通过ArgusFleet可复现测试框架评估四个治理维度。

Result: 1) 溯源：100%重构深度为四的推导链，子秒每跳延迟；2) 传播：高内部可见性，零跨fleet泄漏，强写模式下写可见延迟优化至单次搜索往返；3) 发现两个生产架构问题：非对称作用域执行漏洞和流水线排序冲突。

Conclusion: 长上下文检索不足以支撑生产级多智能体记忆，受控共享记忆需要明确的系统级抽象，真实环境评估对暴露执行漏洞和流水线排序失败至关重要。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM environments require robust mechanisms for shared knowledge management. This paper formalizes the fleet-memory problem and identifies four foundational failure modes: unauthorized leakage, stale propagation, contradiction persistence, and provenance collapse. To address these, we define explicit systems-level primitives: scoped retrieval, temporal supersession, provenance tracking, and policy-governed memory propagation. These primitives are implemented in MemClaw, a production multi-tenant memory service, and evaluated via ArgusFleet, a reproducible harness testing four governance dimensions. Rather than a baseline comparison, this study measures a live production service, emphasizing real-world architectural insights and negative results. Key Evaluation Results Provenance: Successfully reconstructed 100% of depth-four derivation chains with correct writer identity at sub-second per-hop latency. Propagation: Demonstrated high intra-fleet visibility with zero cross-fleet leakage. Under strong write mode, write-to-visible latency was optimized to a single search round-trip. Production Architectural Issues Discovered Asymmetric Scope Enforcement: Tenant isolation held, but sub-tenant scope was initially bypassed on direct GET-by-id requests for agent-scoped credentials (disclosed and remediated during the study). Pipeline Ordering Conflict: While contradiction supersession works for admitted writes, a synchronous near-duplicate gate can prematurely reject contradictory writes before the asynchronous contradiction detector can evaluate them. Conclusion: Long-context retrieval alone is insufficient for production multi-agent memory. Governed shared memory demands explicit systems-level abstractions, and live evaluation is vital to expose enforcement and pipeline-ordering failures missed by design-only treatments.

</details>


### [318] [GUI vs. CLI: Execution Bottlenecks in Screen-Only and Skill-Mediated Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24551)
*Xiao Zhou,Siyue Zhang,Yilun Zhao,Jinbiao Wei,Tingyu Song,Arman Cohan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个匹配的执行层基准测试（440个桌面任务，涵盖18个应用和12个工作流类别），在相同目标、初始状态和验证器条件下比较GUI代理和CLI代理的性能。最强GUI代理通过率59.1%，最强原始技能CLI代理通过率48.2%，但通过验证器引导的技能增强，CLI成功率提升至69.3%。结果表明GUI代理受限于长程工作流中的可靠交互，CLI代理受限于技能接口的覆盖和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对计算机使用代理的评估混淆了交互模态与任务、初始状态、验证器和允许操作的差异，无法公平比较GUI代理和CLI代理的真实能力差异。

Method: 构建了一个匹配的执行层基准测试，包含440个桌面任务、18个应用和12个工作流类别。GUI代理只能使用屏幕界面操作，CLI代理只能使用程序化命令接口，但两者获得相同的目标、初始状态和最终状态验证器。此外，还测试了验证器引导的技能增强方法对CLI代理的影响。

Result: 最强GUI代理完全通过率59.1%，最强原始技能CLI代理完全通过率48.2%；通过验证器引导的技能增强，CLI代理成功率提升至69.3%。

Conclusion: GUI和CLI代理面临不同的执行瓶颈：GUI代理受限于长程工作流中的可靠接地交互，CLI代理受限于技能接口的覆盖范围和可扩展性。CLI代理的缺陷更多来自技能覆盖不完全而非模型能力本身。

Abstract: Computer-use agents can execute software tasks through either graphical interfaces or programmatic command interfaces, but existing evaluations confound interaction modality with differences in tasks, initial states, verifiers, and permitted actions. We introduce a matched execution-layer benchmark of 440 desktop tasks across 18 applications and 12 workflow categories, where screen-only GUI agents and skill-mediated CLI agents receive identical goals, states, and final-state verifiers while being restricted to modality-native actions. In this controlled setting, the strongest GUI agent reaches a 59.1% full pass rate, outperforming the strongest original-skill CLI agent at 48.2%; however, verifier-guided skill augmentation raises CLI success to 69.3%, showing that much of the CLI deficit comes from incomplete skill coverage rather than model capability alone. These results suggest that GUI and CLI expose different execution bottlenecks: GUI agents are limited by reliable grounded interaction over long-horizon workflows, whereas CLI agents are limited by the coverage and scalability of their skill interfaces.

</details>


### [319] [Quant Convergence: Bridging Classical Value Investing and Modern Factor Models for Systematic Equity Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24575)
*Augusto Eiji Yamazaki,Hugo Garrido-Lestache Belinchon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将本杰明·格雷厄姆的价值投资规则作为数学"低通滤波器"，约束复杂机器学习模型（XGBoost和AutoGluon）在美股市场的投资行为。基于20年S&P 500数据的回测显示，纯格雷厄姆随机森林模型实现了232.13%的最高回报率（Calmar比率1.38），而AutoGluon虽获222.68%回报但回撤高达39.78%。混合格雷厄姆规则与动量的组合随机森林实现了202.91%回报和最低最大回撤34.53%。结论：格雷厄姆的"安全边际"能有效防止现代AI过度承担风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代金融中复杂的机器学习模型容易记忆市场短期噪声，而非发现具有长期价值的公司，导致模型过度承担风险。

Method: 构建三组特征（纯格雷厄姆规则、现代市场因子、两者混合），使用XGBoost和AutoGluon等复杂模型，基于20年S&P 500数据，采用严格买入持有策略在2022年3月至2026年3月进行回测。

Result: 纯格雷厄姆随机森林模型最高回报232.13%（Calmar比率1.38）；AutoGluon回报222.68%但回撤39.78%；组合随机森林回报202.91%且最大回撤最低仅34.53%。

Conclusion: 格雷厄姆的"安全边际"并未过时，而是有效约束现代AI过度承担风险的手段。

Abstract: Modern finance relies heavily on complex machine learning models to find patterns in the stock market. However, as these AI models get more complicated, they often memorize short-term market noise instead of finding companies with real, lasting value. We designed this research to test if Benjamin Graham's classic value investing rules could act as a mathematical "low-pass filter" to keep these modern models in check. We built three different sets of features - pure Graham rules, modern market factors, and a mix of both - and tested them against highly complex models (XGBoost and AutoGluon) using 20 years of S&P 500 data. By applying a strict buy-and-hold strategy over a four-year test period (March 2022 to March 2026), the results showed that more complex algorithms do not always win. While the AutoGluon model captured high returns (222.68%), it suffered a substantial 39.78% drop because it bought volatile tech stocks right before the market crashed. On the other hand, the pure Graham Random Forest achieved the highest overall return (232.13%) with much less risk (1.38 Calmar Ratio). Furthermore, the Combined Random Forest successfully mixed momentum with Graham's rules, making a 202.91% return while keeping the lowest maximum drop (34.53%) of any model tested. Ultimately, this research proves that Graham's "margin of safety" isn't outdated; it is actually a highly effective way to prevent modern AI from taking on too much risk.

</details>


### [320] [LLMs Prompted for Legal Context Object More: Overrefusal from Small On-Premises LLMs in Criminal Legal Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24585)
*Anastasiia Kucherenko,François Brouchoud,Dimitri Percia David,Andrei Kucharavy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现在法律场景下，权威风格的前缀（如“你正在担任国家最高法院的助手”）会使小型LLM的拒绝率比无前缀基线提高2-20倍，而角色扮演越狱前缀的效果则因模型而异。这表明可本地部署的小型LLM在真实机构用户可能自然引入的上下文框架下表现不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律专业人士已开始使用个人LLM进行翻译和改写等任务，但即使是此类看似无害的使用，也可能因LLM助手选择性地拒绝某些主题而通过案件处理速度引入偏见。为了提前预测此类偏见，需要研究最可能被用作设备端助手的小型LLM在合法提示下的过度拒绝问题。

Method: 研究选取了多个现代小型LLM（最可能用作设备端助手），通过比较无前缀基线、权威风格前缀（如“国家最高法院助手”、“辩护律师”）以及已知的角色扮演越狱前缀对提示拒绝率的影响，评估过度拒绝的程度。

Result: 权威风格前缀使拒绝率系统性提高2-20倍；角色扮演越狱前缀效果复杂，在某些模型中大幅提高拒绝率，在其他模型中几乎没有影响。

Conclusion: 小型可本地部署的LLM在真实机构用户可能自然引入的上下文框架下表现不稳定，需要进一步研究以最小化偏见机会。

Abstract: While the validity of LLMs' use in the legal context remains subject to ethical and legal debate, legal professionals are already experimenting with personal LLMs, if only for translation and reformulation. However, even such a seemingly innocuous use can introduce biases through case processing speed if LLM assistants selectively refuse assistance on certain topics. To better anticipate such biases, we investigate several modern small LLMs that are most likely to be used as on-device assistants, to assess the impact of overrefusal on legal prompts.
  Surprisingly, we find that authority-style prefixes (``you are acting as an assistant of the national supreme court'', ``[...] defense lawyer'') systematically increase refusal rates by 2--20x over the no-prefix baseline, while a known role-play jailbreak prefix shows mixed effects, sharply increasing refusals in some models and barely shifting them in others. The finding suggests that small on-prem deployable LLMs are unstable under contextual framings that a real institutional user might naturally introduce, and further investigation is essential to minimize opportunities for bias.

</details>


### [321] [AdversaBench: Automated LLM Red-Teaming with Multi-Judge Confirmation and Cross-Model Transferability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24589)
*Khanak Khandelwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdversaBench是一个端到端红队测试流水线，通过5种结构化的提示变异算子生成对抗样本，并采用三裁判加一仲裁的确认机制来评估大语言模型。在45个种子上所有种子均产生确认失败。关键发现包括：算子有效性因类别而异；二值失败率掩盖了难度差异；裁判间一致性高但Cohen's kappa接近零；对抗提示可零样本迁移到更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估LLM的对抗性鲁棒性需要一种既能生成困难输入，又能可靠确认失败是否真实的方法。现有方法在这两方面都存在不足。
