Method: 提出AdversaBench端到端红队测试流水线：(1) 5个结构化算子（如inject_distractor）对种子提示进行变异；(2) 查询目标模型；(3) 三裁判加一仲裁的失败确认机制。在45个种子（推理、指令遵循、工具使用三个类别）上实验。

Result: 所有种子均产生确认失败。四个主要发现：算子效果差异大（inject_distractor在指令遵循上奖励均值0.00，其他类别0.80-0.83）；指令遵循种子平均需2.4次攻击迭代（其他1.1次）；裁判间一致率80-87%但因标签偏斜导致Cohen's kappa接近零；对抗提示可在不同模型间零样本迁移。

Conclusion: AdversaBench展示了结构化变异结合多重确认机制的有效性。对抗提示的跨模型迁移性表明变异利用了通用行为模式而非模型特定漏洞，这对红队测试实践有重要启示。

Abstract: Scaling adversarial evaluation of large language models requires both a method for generating hard inputs and a reliable way to confirm that resulting failures are real. We present AdversaBench, an end-to-end red-teaming pipeline that mutates seed prompts with five structured operators, queries a target model, and confirms failures through a three-judge panel with a meta-judge tiebreaker. We report experiments on 45 seeds across three categories: reasoning, instruction-following, and tool use. Every seed produced a confirmed failure. Four findings stand out. First, operator effectiveness varies sharply by category: inject_distractor scores 0.00 mean reward on instruction-following seeds but 0.80-0.83 on reasoning and tool-use. Second, binary failure rate hides difficulty: instruction-following seeds required 2.4 attacker iterations on average versus 1.1 for other categories, a gap visible in survival curves. Third, pairwise judge agreement of 80-87% coexists with near-zero Cohen's kappa due to label skew; category-level disagreement rates are more informative. Fourth, adversarial prompts generated against Llama 3.1 8B transfer zero-shot to Llama 3.3 70B, suggesting the mutations exploit general behavioral patterns rather than model-specific weaknesses. Code, dataset, and analysis scripts are available at https://github.com/khanak0509/AdversaBench .

</details>


### [322] [ASALT: Adaptive State Alignment for Lateral Transfer in Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24601)
*Anurag Akula,Satheesh K. Perepu,Abhishek Sarkar,Kaushik Dey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASALT方法，通过观测级和状态级适配器将不同维度空间映射到共享嵌入空间，实现跨异构域的多智能体强化学习知识迁移，在样本效率和全局回报上优于现有基线，并能缓解负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL迁移学习方法要求源域和目标域的观测空间与全局状态空间维度必须相同，限制了跨异构域的知识迁移能力。

Method: 提出ASALT方法，引入观测级和状态级适配器，将目标域的观测和全局状态映射到共享嵌入空间，使演员和评论家网络都能实现有效的跨域知识迁移。

Result: 在多个标准基准环境配置下，ASALT在合作场景中在样本效率和全局回报方面超越现有基线，但其有效性取决于源域和目标域之间的不匹配程度；同时能缓解负迁移问题。

Conclusion: ASALT通过适配器机制有效解决了跨异构状态空间维度的知识迁移问题，为MARL中更灵活的迁移学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) addresses the problem of training multiple agents that pursue collaborative, competitive, or mixed objectives. Prior work has investigated transfer learning between source and target domains in MARL; however, the majority of existing approaches impose the constraint that the dimensionalities of the observation space and the global state space must be identical across domains. In this paper, we introduce a method that explicitly accommodates mismatched state-space dimensionalities between source and target domains. The proposed approach, ASALT, incorporates both observation-level and state-level adapters that map the target-domain observations and global states into a shared embedding space, thereby enabling more effective transfer of knowledge across both actors and critics. These adapters can generate embeddings that support efficient strategy transfer across heterogeneous domains. Experimental results on multiple configurations in standard benchmark environments demonstrate that ASALT surpasses existing baselines in terms of sample efficiency and global return in cooperative settings, but its effectiveness depends on the degree of mismatch between source and target domains. Furthermore, our findings indicate that ASALT mitigates negative transfer, which frequently constitutes a major obstacle when transferring policies between domains with differing observation and action spaces.

</details>


### [323] [Uncertainty-Aware Longitudinal Forecasting of Alzheimer's Disease Progression Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24604)
*Arya Hariharan,Shreyank N Gowda,Anala M R*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合序数诊断预测、多水平轨迹生成和分解不确定性估计的概率框架，用于阿尔茨海默病进展建模。该框架在ADNI和OASIS-3数据集上优于多种基线模型，生成了具有合理可信区间覆盖率的五年概率轨迹，并能有效分离偶然不确定性和认知不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法将阿尔茨海默病进展建模简化为单步分类，将认知正常、轻度认知障碍和痴呆视为扁平类别，无法描述患者随时间演化的过程及预测的不确定性累积情况。

Method: 采用Temporal Fusion Transformer编码器配合CORAL序数输出层、不对称损失加权和转换器过采样；结合自回归混合密度网络生成五年概率轨迹；通过解析混合方差和五成员bootstrap集成分离偶然不确定性和认知不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI上优于线性、循环和Transformer基线模型，尤其在MCI与痴呆区分上提升最大；生成的轨迹达到接近标称的90%可信区间覆盖率，不确定性随预测水平扩展；认知不确定性在罕见进展类型、MCI和痴呆患者及OASIS-3外部评估中更高。

Conclusion: 该概率框架能有效建模疾病进展的时序依赖性和不确定性，为临床提供更可靠的长期预测和不确定性量化。

Abstract: Longitudinal modelling of Alzheimer's disease progression is clinically useful only if it can describe not just the most likely next diagnosis, but how a patient may evolve over time and how reliable that forecast is. Most deep learning approaches reduce this problem to single-step classification, treating cognitively normal, mild cognitive impairment, and dementia as flat categories while providing limited insight into how uncertainty accumulates across future visits. We propose a probabilistic framework that combines ordinal diagnosis prediction, multi-horizon trajectory generation, and decomposed uncertainty estimation. A Temporal Fusion Transformer encoder is adapted with a CORAL ordinal output layer, asymmetric loss weighting, and converter oversampling to respect disease-stage ordering and improve sensitivity to MCI-to-dementia transitions. Conditioned on the learned patient-context representation, an autoregressive Mixture Density Network generates five-year probabilistic trajectories for diagnosis state, CDR Sum of Boxes, MMSE orientation, and hippocampal volume. On ADNI, the model outperforms linear, recurrent, and transformer baselines for next-visit diagnosis prediction, with the strongest gains on MCI-versus-dementia discrimination. Generated trajectories achieve near-nominal 90% credible interval coverage, widening uncertainty across the forecast horizon, and biomarker dynamics consistent with expected Alzheimer's disease progression. We further separate aleatoric from epistemic uncertainty using analytic mixture variance and a five-member bootstrap ensemble, which provides the strongest encoder diversity and output-level epistemic signal. Epistemic uncertainty is higher for rare progression archetypes, MCI and dementia patients, and under external evaluation on OASIS-3, where it increases alongside prediction error.

</details>


### [324] [ScaleToT: Generalizing Structured LLM Reasoning for Billion-Scale Low-Activity User Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24605)
*Tianbao Ma,Chang Xi,Yichuan Zou,Chengen Li,Linxun Chen,Zilong Lu,Yanan Niu,Zhaojie Liu,Han Li,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ScaleToT框架，通过LLM处理子集构建结构化推理链，再迁移到低活跃用户群体，在十亿级广告部署中LTV预测提升6.738%，仅需覆盖7.32%人口的推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低活跃用户缺乏丰富交互历史，LLM直接推理不可靠且成本过高，需要一种可控成本的结构化推理方法覆盖大规模低活跃用户群体。

Method: 1) 利用有界熵引导的思维树(ToT)构建类型化的用户状态链进行结构化推理；2) 通过SFT和OSIPO训练学生模型从稀疏静态配置中学习推理；3) 将学生推理表示迁移到轻量级配置编码器，为剩余用户提供共享推理信号，无需LLM推理。

Result: 在十亿级广告部署的LTV预测线上A/B测试中，LT30提升6.738%；离线推理仅覆盖7.32%的人口，大幅降低计算成本。

Conclusion: ScaleToT通过知识蒸馏和结构化推理链迁移，实现了用低成本覆盖大规模低活跃用户群体的有效用户建模。

Abstract: Accurate user modeling often depends on rich interaction histories, which are unavailable for billions of low-activity users. Large Language Models (LLMs) can infer latent user states from static profiles, but this reasoning becomes unreliable when profiles are sparse, and applying an LLM to billions of users is prohibitively expensive. We present ScaleToT, which learns structured reasoning from a small LLM-processed subset and extends it to the broader low-activity user population. To improve reasoning reliability, ScaleToT constructs typed user-state chains with a bounded entropy-guided Tree-of-Thought (ToT) refinement procedure. To make this structured reasoning usable from sparse profiles, the teacher-curated chains are used to train a student model on static profiles through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Outcome-Driven Segment-Aware Implicit Reward Policy Optimization (OSIPO). ScaleToT then transfers the student's reasoning representations to a lightweight profile encoder, providing shared reasoning signals for the remaining users without LLM inference. We evaluate ScaleToT on lifetime value (LTV) prediction in a billion-scale advertising deployment. A randomized online A/B test increased LT30 by 6.738\%, while offline reasoning covered only 7.32\% of the potential population, greatly reducing compute cost compared with full-population reasoning.

</details>


### [325] [AI Tokenomics: The Economics of Tokens, Computation, and Pricing in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24616)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI代币经济学框架，将代币作为连接技术成本与经济价值的核算单位，系统研究代币的生成、消耗、定价、分配与优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代币已成为现代基础模型服务的实际核算单位，但缺乏系统性框架来理解代币如何在不同层面（技术成本、工作流、企业资源配置）与经济效益产生关联。

Method: 构建代币层面的技术成本与工作流层面的生产函数之间的连接，纳入企业资源配置、测量方法及市场设计问题，形成跨层次分析框架。

Result: 揭示了代币支出与经济价值的区别：价值取决于边际生产力、工作流位置、隐藏推理活动、风险及下游传播效应。

Conclusion: 识别出隐藏代币测量、实证校准、代币生产力、动态分配及代币市场等开放研究方向。

Abstract: Tokens have become the practical accounting unit for modern foundation model services, linking information processing, computation, memory use, energy expenditure, pricing, and economic value. This paper develops a framework for AI tokenomics: the study of how tokens are generated, consumed, priced, allocated, and optimized across AI systems. We connect token-level technical costs to workflow-level production functions, enterprise resource allocation, measurement and instrumentation methods, and emerging market-design questions. The framework shows that token expenditure and economic value are distinct: value depends on marginal productivity, workflow position, hidden reasoning activity, risk, and downstream propagation effects. The paper concludes by identifying open research directions in hidden-token measurement, empirical calibration, token productivity, dynamic allocation, and token-based markets.

</details>


### [326] [Abstractions of Queries in Ontology-Based Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24618)
*Michel Leclère,Marie-Laure Mugnier,Guillaume Pérution-Kihli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于存在规则的OBDA设置中的查询抽象问题，提出在扩展的UCQ（包含有限不等式和标记数据库常量的特殊谓词）框架下，能够表达最小完全抽象（及完美抽象），并通过与数据交换中最大恢复概念的关联，刻画了最大声音抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于存在规则和确定答案语义的OBDA中，如何将数据查询抽象到本体层是一个关键问题。由于完美抽象可能不存在，已有研究引入了最小完全抽象和最大声音抽象的概念，但需要更合适的查询语言来表达这些抽象。

Method: 将UCQ扩展为包含有限形式的不等式和标记数据库常量的特殊谓词，研究该扩展语言的表达能力和复杂性；通过建立与数据交换中最大恢复概念的新联系来刻画最大声音抽象。

Result: 该扩展不会增加感兴趣问题的计算复杂度，但能够表达最小完全抽象（从而表达完美抽象）；通过最大恢复概念成功刻画了最大声音抽象。

Conclusion: 提出的UCQ扩展在保持计算可行性的同时，增强了表达查询抽象的能力，并且与数据交换理论建立了有意义的联系。

Abstract: In ontology-based data access (OBDA), multiple data sources are integrated via mappings to an ontology. We consider an OBDA setting based on existential rules and the certain answer semantics. We address the recent issue of query abstraction, which consists of abstracting data queries by translating them to the ontology layer. Since a perfect abstraction may not exist, the notions of minimally complete and maximally sound abstractions have been introduced.
  We study abstractions within an extension of UCQs with a limited form of inequality and a special predicate marking database constants. While this extension does not lead to an increased complexity of the problems of interest, it is able to express minimally complete abstractions, hence perfect abstractions when they exist. We also characterize maximally sound abstractions by making a new connection with the notion of maximum recovery stemming from data exchange.

</details>


### [327] [When CQs Go Wrong: Challenges in CQ Verification with OE-Assist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24619)
*Anna Sofia Lippolis,Mohammad Javad Saeedizade,Robin Keskisärkkä,Aldo Gangemi,Eva Blomqvist,Andrea Giovanni Nuzzolese*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了本体评估中能力问题（CQ）验证过程的挑战，通过19名参与者使用LLM助手进行20项任务的实验，发现需要工具在发布前细化CQ以避免歧义和复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CQ验证是本体评估的核心环节，但现有过程耗时且易出错，因为需要精确解释语言细微差别并与本体形式化构造对齐，歧义和复杂性进一步导致建模决策和验证结果不一致。

Method: 组织19名参与者使用LLM助手进行20项CQ验证任务，分析CQ的挑战性因素并探索提升用户表现的方法。

Result: 实验表明有必要开发工具在发布前细化CQ，以避免后续本体工程阶段出现歧义或过度复杂性。

Conclusion: CQ验证过程中存在显著挑战，使用LLM助手能提供支持，但更根本的解决方案是需要前置工具来优化CQ质量。

Abstract: Competency Questions (CQs) are the central component of CQ-verification, an established process in which an ontology is evaluated against a set of natural language questions to determine whether the intended purpose of the ontology has been properly modelled. However, CQ-verification is often time-consuming and error-prone, as it requires careful interpretation of linguistic nuances and precise alignment with formal ontology constructs. Ambiguities and complexity in CQs can further complicate this process, leading to inconsistent modelling decisions and verification outcomes. In this paper, we investigate what makes a CQ challenging and possible solutions to enhance the users' performance in the CQ-verification process. We experimented with the data of 19 participants who performed CQ-verification on 20 tasks using an LLM assistant to support ontology evaluation. The results show the necessity of a tool to refine CQs before publishing them to avoid ambiguity or excessive complexity in later phases of the ontology engineering process.

</details>


### [328] [SAFARI: Scaling Long Horizon Agentic Fault Attribution via Active Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24626)
*Chenyang Zhu,Jiayu Yao,Kushal Chawla,Youbing Yin,Nathan Wolfe,Pengshan Cai,Jingyu Wu,Spencer Hong,Sangwoo Cho,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Daben Liu,Sambit Sahu,Erin Babinsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAFARI是一种通过工具增强的诊断循环框架，将线性上下文加载替换为主动调查机制，有效解耦了智能体故障归因精度与架构上下文窗口限制，在超长轨迹故障诊断任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将完整轨迹加载到LLM上下文窗口中进行故障诊断，存在注意力稀释问题，且当轨迹超出上下文长度限制时完全失效，无法满足日益复杂的多步、多智能体任务的诊断需求。

Method: SAFARI框架包含专用工具箱（读取和搜索轨迹片段）和持久短期记忆（STM），通过主动调查的诊断循环替代线性上下文加载，实现跨回合推理，将诊断精度与架构上下文限制解耦。

Result: 在Who&When数据集上（1M token预算）比SOTA提升20%，在TRAIL GAIA子集上（25K token预算）提升19%。当目标故障位于模型原生上下文窗口5倍之外时，仍保持0.58的精确率，而传统评估器完全失败。

Conclusion: SAFARI通过主动调查机制有效解决了超长智能体轨迹的故障归因问题，证明了将诊断过程与上下文长度解耦的有效性，为处理超出上下文窗口限制的复杂智能体系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: As autonomous agents tackle increasingly complex multi-step, multi-agent tasks, their execution trajectories have scaled beyond the constraints of even the largest context windows. Current methods for effectively diagnosing agent failures load the full trajectory into an LLM's context window, which suffers from attention dilution and fails when agentic traces inevitably exceed context limits. To address this, we introduce SAFARI (Scaling long-horizon Agentic Fault AttRibution via active Investigation), a framework that replaces linear context loading with a tool-augmented diagnostic loop. By equipping LLMs with a specialized toolbox to read and search trajectory segments alongside a persistent Short-Term Memory (STM) for cross-turn reasoning, SAFARI effectively decouples diagnostic accuracy from architectural context limits. Our experiments demonstrate that SAFARI outperforms state-of-the-art results by 20% on the Who&When dataset within a 1M token budget, and by 19% on TRAIL GAIA subset on a 25K token budget. Most significantly, SAFARI maintains a 0.58 precision even when the target fault resides 5x beyond the model's native context window, a scenario where traditional evaluators fail entirely.

</details>


### [329] [CineCap: Structured Reasoning with Spatio-Temporal Anchors for Cinematographic Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24636)
*Xinyu Mao,Yuhui Zeng,Xiaokun Liu,Wenyu Qin,Meng Wang,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Xiaohan Xing,Max Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CineCap是一个结合结构化推理（时空锚点）与强化学习（全面性、准确性和门控覆盖率奖励）的电影级字幕生成框架，在自建基准CineCap Bench上超越了现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影级字幕生成对于细粒度视频理解和可控视频生成至关重要，但现有多模态大语言模型在这一任务上探索不足，且存在专业电影概念推理困难与字幕全面性-准确性平衡两大挑战。

Method: 提出CineCap框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 结构化推理模块，利用时空锚点将专业电影级描述定位到显式视觉证据，并组织为紧凑原子推理用于监督微调；(2) 强化学习模块，设计全面性、准确性和门控覆盖率奖励来优化描述完整性与事实正确性之间的平衡。

Result: 在自建CineCap Bench基准（472个手动标注视频-字幕对）上，CineCap显著优于强闭源和开源基线模型，达到了最新的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: CineCap通过结构化推理与强化学习的结合，有效解决了电影级字幕生成中专业概念推理和描述质量平衡的难题，为电影级视频理解与生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cinematographic captioning aims to describe how a video is filmed using professional film-language concepts such as camera movement, shot size, depth of field, composition, and shooting angle. This capability is important for fine-grained video understanding and controllable movie-quality video generation, yet remains underexplored in existing multimodal large language models. Unlike question-answering-based evaluation of cinematic understanding, cinematographic captioning requires a unified open-form description over multiple cinematographic dimensions. This task is challenging for two main reasons: the model must infer professional cinematographic concepts from subtle visual evidence, and it must generate captions that are both comprehensive and accurate. Accordingly, we propose CineCap, a framework that combines structured reasoning with spatio-temporal anchors and reinforcement learning with comprehensiveness, accuracy, and gated coverage rewards. The former grounds professional cinematographic descriptions in explicit visual evidence and organizes them into compact atomic reasoning for supervised fine-tuning, while the latter improves the balance between descriptive completeness and factual correctness. In addition, we construct CineCap Bench, a benchmark of 472 manually annotated video-caption pairs for systematic evaluation. Extensive experiments show that CineCap consistently outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, establishing a new state of the art for cinematographic captioning. The code, model checkpoint, and benchmark are publicly available in https://github.com/Hectormxy/CineCap.git.

</details>


### [330] [LaGO: Latent Action Guidance for Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24669)
*Kuan-Yen Liu,Ren-Jyun Huang,Ti-Rong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LaGO提出利用预训练LLM作为潜在动作先验（而非直接控制器）来软引导在线策略优化，在CLEVR-Robot和Meta-World基准上显著提升了PPO的成功率和奖励，且更强的LLM提供更有效的引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将LLM直接用作控制器进行精确动作生成，但在实践中不可靠；作者希望利用LLM的知识来引导而非替代在线强化学习策略，提升决策质量。

Method: LaGO使用预训练LLM作为潜在动作先验（latent action prior），在在线策略优化（PPO）过程中以软约束方式引导策略更新，而非让LLM直接输出环境动作。

Result: 在CLEVR-Robot上平均成功率从15.1%提升至27.2%；在Meta-World上从2.7%提升至15.2%；更强的LLM提供更优引导效果。

Conclusion: LLM的知识可以通过潜在动作先验形式有效提升在线强化学习的规划与决策能力，且该方法可应用于离散和连续控制两类任务。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for planning and sequential decision-making, but prior work often relies on using them as direct controllers, which requires precise action generation and can be unreliable in practice. This paper proposes Latent Action Guidance for Online Reinforcement Learning (LaGO), a framework that uses a pretrained LLM as a latent action prior to softly guide online policy optimization, rather than treating the LLM as an explicit planner or controller. Experiments on both a discrete-control benchmark, CLEVR-Robot, and a continuous-control benchmark, Meta-World, demonstrate that LaGO consistently improves both reward and success rate over Vanilla PPO. In particular, LaGO increases the average success rate from 15.1% to 27.2% on CLEVR-Robot and from 2.7% to 15.2% on Meta-World. Our analysis further shows that stronger pretrained LLMs provide more effective guidance, suggesting that LLM knowledge can improve planning and online decision-making.

</details>


### [331] [Cost-Optimal Decision Diagrams for Stochastic Boolean Function Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24672)
*Xia Zong,Tuomo Lehtonen,Jussi Rintanen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带变量选择启发式、剪枝和缓存的精确分支定界算法，用于在可变成本和概率分布下最小化命题公式的期望评估成本。实验表明算法具有可扩展性，并证明了问题的#P-hard性和PSPACE包含性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在信息获取成本不同的决策场景中，如何构造确定性评估策略以最小化命题公式的期望评估成本是一个实际问题，但此前缺乏通用的精确算法。

Method: 提出分支定界算法，包含变量选择启发式、剪枝和缓存技术；同时设计了贪心波束搜索变体用于效率-质量权衡分析。

Result: 随机实例实验验证了算法的可扩展性；结构化心脏病诊断实例验证了实际应用效果；理论分析确定了问题的计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 该算法是首个面向此通用问题的实用精确算法，证明了问题属于#P-hard且包含于PSPACE中。

Abstract: In many decision-making scenarios, acquiring information incurs different costs. We consider the problem of constructing a deterministic evaluation strategy that minimizes the expected cost of evaluating a propositional formula under variable costs and a probability distribution over truth assignments. We present a branch-and-bound algorithm with variable-selection heuristics, pruning, and caching. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first practical exact algorithm for this level of generality. Experiments on random instances demonstrate scalability and quantify the efficiency-quality trade-off of a greedy beam-search variant. We additionally evaluate a structured heart-disease diagnosis instance. Finally, we prove that the problem is $\#P$-hard and contained in $\mathrm{PSPACE}$.

</details>


### [332] [Decentralised AI Training and Inference with BlockTrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24722)
*Peter Toth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spheroid BlockTrain，一种去中心化训练协议，将模型分割为独立可训练的块，每个块在局部目标上优化后组合为完整模型。在 WikiText 上达到 CE 1.359（PPL 3.89），与端到端 Transformer 基线相当（差距约 0.04 CE），并支持跨公共网络的多机训练与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI训练越来越依赖密集、集中控制的加速器集群，这为超大规模云服务商和大型集中实验室创造了结构性优势，使开放或独立的AI研究依赖于稀缺资本、特权基础设施和数据中心地理位置的限制。为了打破这种集中化垄断，需要一种去中心化的训练方案。

Method: Spheroid BlockTrain 将模型划分为多个独立可训练的块（blocks），每个块在局部目标上独立优化（局部目标源自相同的全局目标），推理时将各块组合为一个完整模型。每个工作节点仅训练一个块，无需维护完整模型的优化器状态。支持通过 HTTP/TCP 传输序列化检查点和更新，实现多机协作训练。

Result: 在字节级 WikiText 上，BlockTrain 达到交叉熵 1.359（困惑度 3.89），与同设置端到端 Transformer 参考模型相差约 0.04 CE；六工作节点联合训练达到 CE 1.385；三主机公共网络实验将 CE 从 5.580 降至 1.811，传输 15.22 GB 数据；推理方面，在三个公共网络 GPU 主机上支持高达 75.80B 参数的逻辑 fp16 形状，优于匹配的纯自回归 TCP 管道基线。

Conclusion: BlockTrain 证明了去中心化训练在保持模型质量接近集中式训练的同时，降低了对集中式基础设施的依赖，为开放和独立的AI研究提供了一种可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Frontier AI training is increasingly shaped by access to dense, centrally controlled accelerator clusters. This creates a structural advantage for hyperscalers and large centralized laboratories, and makes open or independent AI efforts depend on scarce capital, privileged infrastructure, and data-center geography. We present Spheroid BlockTrain, a decentralized training protocol in which a model is partitioned into independently trainable blocks, each optimized on a local objective derived from the same global target and composed at inference into one model. On byte-level WikiText, BlockTrain reaches cross entropy 1.359 (perplexity 3.89), within about 0.04 CE of a same-setup end-to-end Transformer reference, while each active worker trains only one block and avoids full-model optimizer state. A shared six-worker block training run reaches CE 1.385 by averaging same-block updates into one assembled model. HTTP/TCP transport experiments move real serialized checkpoints and updates, including a public-IP three-host run that improves CE from 5.580 to 1.811 while moving 15.22 GB. For inference, the current BlockTrain path uses one block-stack traversal per full output and serves over direct TCP across three public-network GPU hosts up to a 75.80B-parameter logical fp16 shape, outperforming a matched plain-autoregressive TCP pipeline baseline because it emits a full sequence per WAN pipeline traversal rather than one token per traversal.

</details>


### [333] [Scaling Laws for Task-Specific LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24747)
*Lavinia Ghita,Dhruv Desai,Ioana Boier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了领域特定的大语言模型压缩经验缩放定律，以量化金融为应用领域，提出了一种融合链式思维监督损失的混合蒸馏方法，发现监督格式是领域知识与通用知识权衡的关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型规模日益增大，在延迟和成本受限的实际部署场景中面临挑战，亟需理解领域特定压缩过程中知识与性能的缩放规律。

Method: 在量化金融领域，比较了基于logit和基于LoRA的蒸馏方法在迭代结构剪枝下的表现，引入了一种融合链式思维监督损失的混合蒸馏损失函数，以稳定推理轨迹上的KL散度蒸馏过程。

Result: 领域内任务质量在压缩下可预测地下降，而通用知识基准在更早的压缩点即发生崩溃；监督格式是权衡领域知识与通用知识的关键因素，链式思维监督可以有效恢复被剪枝擦除的通用知识。

Conclusion: 发布了FinHeadlineMix数据集、缩放定律结果和实践建议，为领域特定压缩决策提供了可复用的框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a growing range of domains, yet their scale poses deployment challenges in applications where latency and cost constraints are critical. This paper derives empirical scaling laws for domain-specific LLM compression, quantifying how in-domain and general knowledge performance scale with dataset size, compression ratio, supervision format, and iterative pruning schedule. Using quantitative finance as our application domain, we compare logit-based and LoRA-based distillation under iterative structural pruning, introducing a blended chain-of-thought supervision loss that stabilizes KL-divergence distillation over reasoning traces. In-domain task quality degrades predictably under compression while general-knowledge benchmarks collapse well before the same point; supervision format is the key driver of this tradeoff, with chain-of-thought supervision actively recovering general knowledge that pruning erases. We release the headline dataset FinHeadlineMix, scaling law results, and practical recommendations to provide a reusable framework for domain-specific compression decisions.

</details>


### [334] [Can Scale Save Us From Plasticity Loss in Large Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24752)
*J. Fernando Hernandez-Garcia,Tomás Figliolia,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了GPT风格Transformer模型（5M到314M参数）在多语言持续学习中的可塑性损失问题。实验发现：可塑性损失随模型大小呈现亚线性增长标度律；更大模型虽能延缓但无法完全避免可塑性损失；即使在平稳多语言训练下也会出现可塑性损失，表明该现象并非仅存在于突变的持续学习场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可塑性损失（loss of plasticity）是持续学习领域的根本性挑战，但以往研究多聚焦于小型旧架构且很少涉及自然语言领域。本文旨在验证在现代基于Transformer的大语言模型范式中，可塑性损失是否仍然是一个问题。

Method: 使用GPT风格Transformer模型（5M到314M非嵌入参数）在多语言持续学习问题上进行训练，通过留出的越南语探测任务（held-out Vietnamese probing task）的退化程度来量化可塑性损失。同时研究了可塑性损失与模型大小的标度律关系，并在平稳多语言训练条件下进行对照实验。

Result: 1）所有规模的模型均出现可塑性损失；2）可塑性损失随模型大小呈亚线性增长标度律；3）增大参数数量可延缓但无法彻底防止可塑性损失；4）在平稳多语言训练下同样观察到可塑性损失现象。

Conclusion: 即使是现代大规模Transformer语言模型，在经过足够长时间的训练后，无论是在持续学习还是平稳学习设置下，最终都会丧失高效适应新数据的能力。单纯增加参数量不足以完全解决可塑性损失问题。

Abstract: The loss of plasticity - the ability of a network to learn new information after having already learned older information - is a fundamental challenge in creating artificial neural networks capable of continual learning. Although this phenomenon has been known for decades, it has mostly been studied in older, relatively small architectures and rarely in natural-language domains. To determine whether loss of plasticity remains a problem in the modern transformer-based LLM paradigm, we study plasticity loss in GPT-style Transformer models trained on a multilingual continual learning problem. Consistent with prior work, we find evidence of plasticity loss across models ranging from 5M to 314M non-embedding parameters, as measured by deterioration on a held-out Vietnamese probing task. We further find that the onset of plasticity loss follows a predictable scaling law, growing sublinearly with model size. These results suggest that larger models may delay the measurable effects of plasticity loss, but that increasing parameter count alone is likely to be insufficient to completely prevent it. We also find evidence of plasticity loss under stationary multilingual training, challenging the view that the phenomenon is exclusive to continual learning with abrupt task changes. Overall, our results suggest that even large Transformer language models trained on natural-language will eventually lose the ability to efficiently adapt to new data after sufficiently long training, in both continual and stationary settings.

</details>


### [335] [BluTrain: A C++/CUDA Framework for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24780)
*Adhitya Charan,Adwaid Suresh,Anuj Kumar,Aparna A,Dhanakumar K,Dharun M S,Dinesh G,Goutham Kumar Reddy K,Harshini V M,Jenifa D,Jona Delcy C A,Kathirvel S,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Kiruthik Kanna M,Kurra Vishnu Sai,Madhumithaa G K,Navin Kumar,Ram Charan Golla,Revathi T,Rishikkanth R,Sanjay Krishna M,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BluTrain是一个从头构建的轻量级、架构通用的C++/CUDA训练框架，在8-GPU系统上训练124M参数GPT-2时，相比PyTorch实现了更高吞吐量（407K vs 395K tokens/s）和更低内存占用（降幅达22%），同时保持数值保真度和收敛质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在大规模场景下的瓶颈已从模型架构转向系统工程，现有框架在硬件表达控制、系统复杂性抽象和编排逻辑重复性方面存在不足，需要一种能从底层控制硬件表达同时抽象系统复杂度的训练框架。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，使用标准C++和核心CUDA编程模型构建BluTrain框架，原生实现每一层：类型化张量模块（含反向模式自动微分）、线性代数库、缓存分配器、多模式分布式执行模块以及基于MLIR的深度学习编译器。

Result: 在8-GPU 6000 Ada系统上训练124M参数GPT-2（FP32），BluTrain平均吞吐量达407K tokens/s（PyTorch为395K），内存占用降低22%，严格保持数值保真度，且收敛到略低的最终验证损失。

Conclusion: 通过原生实现和开放底层调优能力，BluTrain能够突破框架级性能上限，证明在深度学习系统工程层面仍有显著优化空间。

Abstract: Progress in deep learning is, at scale, more a matter of systems engineering than of modelling: the behaviour of a model in training (its throughput, its memory footprint, and the numerical fidelity of the result) is determined less by the architecture itself than by how that architecture is expressed on the hardware. To achieve absolute control over this hardware expression while abstracting away systems complexity to make modelling seamless and eliminating the need for repetitive orchestration logic, BluTrain was architected from first principles as a robust, lightweight, and architecture-general training framework in standard C++ and the core CUDA programming model. Every layer is implemented natively: a typed tensor module with reverse-mode autograd, a linear-algebra library, a caching allocator, a multi-mode distributed-execution module, and an MLIR-based deep-learning compiler. In formal evaluations training a 124M-parameter GPT-2 baseline in FP32 on an 8-GPU 6000 Ada system, BluTrain outperforms industry-standard baselines in both throughput (sustaining an average of 407K tokens/s versus PyTorch's 395K tokens/s) and memory efficiency (achieving up to a 22% footprint reduction), while strictly preserving numerical fidelity and converging to a marginally lower final validation loss. With every layer explicitly open to native tuning, the performance ceiling is the framework's own to raise.

</details>


### [336] [Solving Inverse Problems of Chaotic Systems with Bidirectional Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24824)
*Peiyan Hu,Jian Zhang,Jiashu Pan,Ruiqi Feng,Tao Zhang,Zhi-Ming Ma,Yuan-Sen Ting,Gongjie Li,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出双向条件流匹配（Bi-CFM）方法及其守恒约束变体（CBi-CFM），用于解决混沌动力系统中的逆问题（从终态推断初态），在多个基准系统上实现了两个数量级以上的加速和更优的分布级指标，并在三体问题与真实球状星团观测中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌系统的逆问题（从终态反推初态）由于不适定性、解的非唯一性、不稳定性以及时间反向动力学的混沌特性而长期未得到解决，亟需一种能够处理混沌演化随机性并抑制指数级误差累积的方法。

Method: 提出双向条件流匹配（Bi-CFM），学习初态与终态分布之间的双向映射以捕获混沌演化的随机性并减轻误差指数累积；对于具有守恒律的系统，进一步扩展为守恒约束Bi-CFM（CBi-CFM）。

Result: 在Lorenz、Circuit及高维Lorenz 96系统上，Bi-CFM在五项分布级指标上优于基线方法，并实现两个数量级以上的加速；在三体行星散射问题中，CBi-CFM的守恒律误差与真实值相当；在真实球状星团观测上实现了精度提升。

Conclusion: 该方法为求解长时标、真实世界混沌动力系统的逆问题提供了一条可扩展的路径，标志着在该方向上的重要进展。

Abstract: Modeling chaotic systems is crucial yet challenging. Inverse problems in chaotic dynamics, namely inferring initial conditions from final states, remain largely unsolved because of ill-posedness, non-uniqueness, instability, and potentially chaotic time-reverse dynamics. We address this open problem with Bidirectional Conditional Flow Matching (Bi-CFM), which learns bidirectional mappings between distributions of initial and final states to capture the stochasticity of chaotic evolution and mitigate exponential error accumulation over time. Furthermore, for systems with conservation laws, we extend it to Conservation-constrained Bi-CFM (CBi-CFM). Across the classic Lorenz, Circuit, and high-dimensional Lorenz 96 systems, Bi-CFM improves five distribution-level metrics over baselines while achieving a speedup of more than two orders of magnitude. In the three-body planet-planet scattering problem in planetary dynamics, CBi-CFM better respects conservation laws, with conservation errors comparable to those of the ground truth. Finally, on real observations of globular clusters, collisional million-body systems shaped by $\sim 10^{10}$ years (10 Gyr) of evolution, our method represents an advance in accuracy, establishing a scalable route to solving inverse problems of long-timescale real-world chaotic dynamics.

</details>


### [337] [Difference-Making without Making a Difference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24832)
*Sander Beckers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出Andreas & Günther提出的七种实际因果关系定义存在本质上的同一性，其最新的事实差异制造定义实际上同时包含三类竞争性解释，导致其整个理论框架内部不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Andreas & Günther在因果关系定义分类中的根本性错误——他们所声称的不同类型定义实际上并不存在本质区别。

Method: 通过对Andreas & Günther最新定义（事实差异制造）的逻辑分析，证明其同时体现了事实差异制造、反事实差异制造和基于规则三类因果解释；并通过关键案例对比检验所有七种定义的一致性。

Result: 发现Andreas & Günther的事实差异制造定义本质上混合了三种不同类型的因果机制，导致其分类体系失效；关键案例测试进一步表明所有七种定义均存在内部矛盾。

Conclusion: Andreas & Günther所提出的七种实际因果关系定义及其分类体系存在根本性缺陷，无法自洽。

Abstract: Over a series of seven papers, Andreas & Günther have introduced seven definitions of actual causation and have classified them as belonging to three different, competing, types of accounts: factual difference-making, counterfactual difference-making, and regularity-based. I show that their most recent - factual difference-making - definition instantiates all three types, thereby proving that these are distinctions without a difference. I further compare their novel account to the other six accounts on several crucial examples, revealing that this undermines all seven of their accounts.

</details>


### [338] [Accuracy and Satisfaction in Multi-Turn LLM Dialogues for NFR Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24834)
*Ali Pourghasemi Fatideh,Wilder Baldwin,Maria Dhakal,Collin McMillan,Sepideh Ghanavati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了开发者与LLM对话助手（如GitHub Copilot）在非功能需求（特别是HIPAA合规性）评估中的多轮对话准确性和质量。49名程序员对148个HIPAA衍生NFR进行评估，发现开发者倾向于认同LLM的评估，但准确性较低。较长回复和信息提供轮次会降低用户满意度，而主动交互则提升满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅关注功能正确性，缺乏对非功能需求（NFR）对话质量与准确性的评估方法，而NFR具有模糊性、上下文依赖性和跨程序多部件特性。

Method: 雇佣49名程序员与GitHub Copilot进行多轮对话，评估148个HIPAA衍生NFR在iTrust代码库上的三个维度：需求满足程度、推理过程和代码定位。

Result: 开发者倾向于同意LLM的评估结果，但相对于专家标注的ground truth准确性较低。更长的系统回复和更多信息提供轮次对用户满意度有负面影响，而主动交互有正面影响。

Conclusion: 为设计支持NFR评估的LLM对话系统提供了重要见解，强调需要超越单轮准确性的评估方法。

Abstract: LLM-based dialogue assistants have become mainstream tools for software developers, yet current evaluation benchmarks focus exclusively on functional correctness. This leaves a critical gap in assessing the quality and accuracy of these conversations when handling Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs), which are inherently vague, context-dependent, and involve many parts of a program. Evaluating how well these systems support collaborative reasoning about NFRs requires methods that go beyond single-turn accuracy to capture both the correctness of the system's outputs and the quality of the multi-turn interaction. In this paper, we investigate the accuracy and quality of multi-turn conversations between developers and an LLM-based agent in the domain of Health Insurance Portability and Accountability Act (HIPAA) regulatory compliance. We hired 49 programmers to interact with GitHub Copilot to assess 148 HIPAA-derived NFRs against the iTrust codebase, a system designed to comply with HIPAA regulations, across three dimensions: requirement satisfaction level, reasoning, and code localization. We find that developers tend to agree with LLM assessments, but accuracy against expert ground truth is low. We model user satisfaction and find that longer system responses and more information-providing turns negatively affect user satisfaction, whereas proactive interactions positively affect it. Our findings provide insights for designing LLM-based dialogue systems that support NFR assessment.

</details>


### [339] [Grading the Grader: Lessons from Evaluating an Agentic Data Analysis System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24839)
*Tian Zheng,Kai-Tai Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何可靠评估智能数据分析系统的输出，提出了三层人机协作的评分级联机制（严格正则匹配、宽松LLM评分、片段人工审查），在153个数值任务上实现了100%精确率和97%召回率，并发现变量类型是最影响评分动态的元数据字段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能数据分析系统生成的输出包含代码、数值结果和文字诊断，比单轮LLM响应更难以评估。需要区分智能体输出与真实答案之间的真正分歧与评分伪影，因此研究如何可靠地自动化评估这类系统以及提升评分质量的策略。

Method: 基于LAMBDA多智能体数据分析系统，在DSGym的153个数值QRData任务上，开发并评估了三层人机评分级联：1) 严格正则匹配（关键词锚定提取管道提升60个百分点召回率）；2) 基于LLM的宽松评分（迭代提示机制将评分成功率从36%提升至97%）；3) 基于片段的详细人工审查。对比了有无原始问题重新注入的提示策略效果。

Result: 两种自动评分器均达到100%观察精确率（0/70假阳性）。宽松评分器召回率为97%。关键字锚定提取管道将严格评分器召回率比末尾数字启发式提升60个百分点。迭代提示机制将评分成功率从36%提升至97%，宽松通过率从16%提升至46%。原始问题重新注入未带来额外收益，确认提示实质是答案模板线索。变量类型是与评分管道动态和结果最相关联的元数据字段。

Conclusion: 该三层人机评分级联能可靠评估智能数据分析系统，自动评分器在保证高精确率的同时达到较高召回率。非生成式AI与生成式AI策略具有不同失败模式，可互补使用。提示机制中答案模板线索比原始问题重新注入更有效。变量类型是影响评分质量的关键元数据特征。

Abstract: Agentic data analysis systems produce rich outputs, including code, numerical results, and verbal diagnostics. This makes them more challenging to evaluate than single-turn LLM responses. It is therefore necessary to distinguish genuine disagreement between an agent's output and a ground-truth answer from grading artifacts. We investigate how reliably automated graders assess such a system and what strategies improve grading quality by applying LAMBDA, a multi-agent data-analysis system, on 153 numerical QRData tasks from DSGym. We develop and evaluate a three-layer human-AI grading cascade: strict regex matching, LLM-based lenient grading, and snippet-based human inspection, which combines non-GenAI and GenAI strategies with different failure profiles. Both automated graders achieve 100% observed precision (0/70 false positives). The lenient grader's recall is 97% against human labels. A keyword-anchored extraction pipeline raises the strict grader's recall by 60 percentage points over a last-number heuristic; the lenient grader is architecturally parser-independent. An iterative nudge mechanism raises grading run success from 36% to 97% and lenient-pass rates from 16% to 46%; comparing nudging with and without original-question re-injection shows that re-injection offers no benefit, confirming the nudge as an answer template cue. We further observe in this case study that variable type is the task metadata field most consistently associated with grading pipeline dynamics and observed outcome grades.

</details>


### [340] [Matching Tasks to Objectives: Fine-Tuning and Prompt-Tuning Strategies for Encoder-Decoder Pre-trained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24841)
*Ahmad Pouramini,Hesham Faili*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Match Task to Objective (MTO)框架，通过将预训练目标与任务对齐，在生成和问答任务中实现超过120%的性能提升，尤其是在少样本场景下。该框架涵盖多目标预训练、自适应数据准备和模板设计三个阶段，并扩展至提示调优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不同预训练目标对编码器-解码器预训练语言模型在生成和问答任务中的影响，特别是常识知识检索与补全任务，并解决如何为特定任务选择合适目标的问题。

Method: 提出MTO框架：1) 在预训练和微调阶段引入多目标训练；2) 设计自动化方法根据识别目标准备无监督任务数据；3) 设计对齐预训练和适应阶段目标的模板；4) 扩展至提示调优方法。

Result: 在少样本设置中相比传统方法提升超过120%的性能；显著超越相关工作；在全数据集场景中也超过基线；有效提升提示调优性能。

Conclusion: MTO框架能精确指导特定任务的模型选择与优化，具有重要实用价值，代码已开源。

Abstract: Prompt-based learning has emerged as a dominant paradigm in natural language processing. This study explores the impact of diverse pre-training objectives on the performance of encoder-decoder pre-trained language models across generation and question answering tasks, with a focus on commonsense knowledge retrieval and completion. We highlight the benefits of incorporating multiple objectives during both pre-training and fine-tuning stages. We introduce the Match Task to Objective (MTO) framework and methods for determining the appropriate objective for a given task. This framework offers automated methods to prepare task-related data for adaptation through unsupervised training, based on the identified objective. In the fine-tuning stage, we design novel templates that align with the objectives of the pre-training and adaptation stages. When aligned with task requirements, these strategies can achieve a performance gain of over 120\% compared to conventional methods in few-shot settings. They significantly outperform related works in few-shot settings and exceed the baseline even in full-dataset scenarios. Furthermore, we extend this approach to include prompt-tuning methodologies, providing guidance for more effective soft prompt engineering and optimization. Our strategies significantly enhance prompt-tuning performance as well. These insights hold substantial value, precisely guiding the selection and optimization of models customized for specific tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/puraminy/MTO/

</details>


### [341] [World Models in Pieces: Structural Certification for General Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24842)
*Yikai Lu,Yifei Wu,Xinyu Lu,Tongxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出结构性认证框架，证明通用智能体在大世界场景下无法普遍胜任，并通过分解组合目标为局部转换提供世界模型的逐项保证，实现可认证部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大世界场景下，智能体能力必然分散化，标准均匀保证无法区分关键瓶颈与无关失败，需要新的形式化工具来提供有意义的性能保证。

Method: 首先通过形式化证明通用智能体并非普适的，否定标准最坏情况分析的有效性；然后提出结构性认证（structural certification）框架，这是一种转换局部（transition-local）框架，将带界的目标条件性能映射为智能体内部世界模型的逐项保证；最后提供算法，利用深度组合目标过滤特定转换，并证明在这些目标上通用智能体具有O(1/n)+O(δ)误差界的结构性世界模型。

Result: 证明了结构性世界模型的O(1/n)+O(δ)误差界，且该界在小δ场景下是紧的，而该场景的存在性由认证本身明确保证。

Conclusion: 该框架通过定位长程规划可靠的具体转换，实现了通用智能体的可认证部署。

Abstract: In the big-world regime, agents cannot be universally capable and their ability is inevitably specialized across a world model in pieces. Consequently, standard uniform guarantees fail to distinguish between the understanding of critical bottlenecks and irrelevant failures. We first formalize this limitation by proving that general agents are not universal, rendering standard worst-case analysis uninformative. To overcome this, we introduce structural certification, a transition-local framework that maps bounded goal-conditioned performance to entry-wise guarantees on the agent's internal world model. Our main contribution is constructive. We provide algorithms that filter specific transitions using deep compositional goals and prove that a general agent on these goals has a structural world model with a $\mathcal{O}(1/n) + \mathcal{O}(δ)$ error bound. Conversely, this bound is tight in the small-$δ$ regime, whose existence is explicitly guaranteed by our certification. These results enable the certifiable deployment of general agents by localizing the specific transitions where long-horizon planning is reliable.

</details>


### [342] [OpenThoughts-Agent: Data Recipes for Agentic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24855)
*Negin Raoof,Richard Zhuang,Marianna Nezhurina,Etash Guha,Atula Tejaswi,Ryan Marten,Charlie F. Ruan,Tyler Griggs,Alexander Glenn Shaw,Hritik Bansal,E. Kelly Buchanan,Artem Gazizov,Reinhard Heckel,Chinmay Hegde,Sankalp Jajee,Daanish Khazi,Emmanouil Koukoumidis,Xiangyi Li,Hange Liu,Shlok Natarajan,Harsh Raj,Nicholas Roberts,Ethan Shen,Nishad Singhi,Michael Siu,Ashima Suvarna,Hanwen Xing,Patrick Yubeaton,Robert Zhang,Leon Liangyu Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Steven Dillmann,Saadia Gabriel,Xunyi Jiang,Anurag Kashyap,Boxuan Li,Yein Park,Minh Pham,Sujay Sanghavi,Lin Shi,Ke Sun,Yixin Wang,Zhiwei Xu,Erica Zhang,Siyan Zhao,Wanjia Zhao,Jenia Jitsev,Alex Dimakis,Benjamin Feuer,Ludwig Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OT-Agent是一个完全开源的数据筛选管线，用于训练具有泛化能力的智能体语言模型。通过100多项对照实验，揭示了任务来源和多样性的重要性。基于该管线构建的10万条训练集微调Qwen3-32B，在七个智能体基准上取得44.8%的平均准确率，比最强开源模型高3.9个百分点，且训练数据具有良好扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源智能体模型（如SWE-Smith、SERA、Nemotron-Terminal）通常针对单一基准进行训练，缺乏跨多样化智能体任务泛化的训练数据筛选方法。因此，作者希望构建一个完全开源的数据筛选管线，系统地研究如何为通用智能体模型筛选训练数据。

Method: (1) 设计并开源了一个完整的数据筛选管线；(2) 进行了100多项受控消融实验，系统研究管线各阶段的影响；(3) 基于管线构建了10万条训练样本；(4) 使用该数据集微调Qwen3-32B模型。

Result: 微调后的Qwen3-32B在七个智能体基准上平均准确率为44.8%，比最强开源智能体模型Nemotron-Terminal-32B（40.9%）高出3.9个百分点。训练数据在计算量受控的比较中，在每个训练集规模下均优于其他开源数据集，表现出良好的扩展性。

Conclusion: 任务来源和多样性是智能体训练数据的关键因素；OT-Agent的全开源管线可有效训练泛化能力强的智能体模型，数据与模型均已公开。

Abstract: Agentic language models dramatically expand the applications of AI yet little is publicly known about how to curate training data for broadly capable agents. Existing open efforts such as SWE-Smith, SERA, and Nemotron-Terminal typically target a single benchmark, leaving open the question of how to train models that generalize across diverse agentic tasks. The OpenThoughts-Agent (OT-Agent) project addresses this gap with a fully open data curation pipeline for training agentic models. We conduct more than 100 controlled ablation experiments to systematically investigate each stage of the pipeline, yielding insights on the importance of task sources and diversity. We then assemble a training set of 100K examples from our pipeline and fine-tune Qwen3-32B on this dataset, which yields an average accuracy of 44.8% across seven agentic benchmarks and a 3.9 percentage point improvement over the strongest existing open data agentic model (Nemotron-Terminal-32B, 40.9%). Moreover, our training data exhibits strong scaling properties, outperforming alternative open datasets at every training set size in compute-controlled comparisons. We publicly release our training sets, data pipeline, experimental data, and models at openthoughts.ai to support future open research on agentic model training.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [343] [Embodied Explainability and Ontological Obstacles: Why We Struggle to Explain the Answers of Large Language Models (LLMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23840)
*Marvin Pafla,Jesse Hoey,Kate Larson,Mark Hancock*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文论证可解释性不应被视为AI模型的内在属性，而应基于具身认知理论，将其理解为人在共享实践中通过行动创造的理解过程。通过概念分析和演示，揭示了大模型"内部探查"的本体论障碍，并指出当前XAI实践中许多解释名不副实，可能导致过度依赖。最后主张可解释性应归于那些在情境实践中提供探索、协调和修复行为可供性的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前将可解释性视为AI模型内在属性的主流观点，基于Dourish和生成认知理论提出具身化的可解释性理解，揭示大语言模型可解释性研究中的本体论困境。

Method: 论证性论文，采用概念分析和演示的方法，结合Dourish和生成认知理论框架进行理论建构。

Result: 发现"探查内部"时存在本体论障碍：代理者引入的外部抽象可能被误认为模型自身的抽象；关注内部推理忽视了解释者参与自身理解的过程。当前XAI实践中许多解释名不副实，扭曲了目的并可能增加过度依赖。

Conclusion: 可解释性主张应保留给那些在情境实践中提供可供性（探索、协调、修复行为）的设计；具身化解释通过使重要的东西公开可供行动来重组意义建构。

Abstract: Explainability is often framed as a property of an AI model, with explanations extracted from its internals and shown to users. In this argument paper, we instead provide an embodied account of explainability based on Dourish and enactivist cognition: understanding is created in use as people act on affordances in shared practice. Using demonstrations and conceptual analysis, we reveal ontological obstacles when "looking inside" large language models: surrogates import external abstractions that can be mistaken for the model's, and focusing on internal reasoning misses that explainers participate in their own understanding. We discuss these obstacles in XAI practice, arguing that many explanations are misnamed, which skews their purpose and can increase overreliance. Finally, we highlight how embodied explanations reorganize sense-making by making what matters publicly available for action, and argue that explainability claims should be reserved for designs that provide affordances to probe, coordinate, and repair behaviour in situated practice.

</details>


### [344] [Do Language Models Pass the Bechdel Test? Auditing Gender Biases in LLM-Generated Screenplays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24022)
*Megha N. Govindu,Stephanie T. Wang,Sorelle A. Friedler,Danaé Metaxa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过自动化Bechdel测试和社会网络分析方法，审计了LLM生成电影剧本中的性别表征偏差，发现人类编写的剧本比LLM生成的剧本更可能通过Bechdel测试，但在其他网络分析指标（如中心性、同质性和三元关系）上，LLM生成的剧本在某些情况下偏差更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在媒体制作中的广泛应用，需要系统评估其对故事内容中性别表征的影响，补充现有关于LLM输出中性别偏见的研究。

Method: 通过自动化Bechdel测试和社会网络分析（中心性、同质性、三元关系）对GPT-5、Gemini 3 Pro和Claude Sonnet 4.5生成的剧本与768个人类编写剧本进行对比审计。

Result: 人类编写的剧本更可能通过Bechdel测试；但在中心性、同质性和三元关系等网络分析指标上，LLM生成的剧本在某些情况下偏差更小；不过所有剧本类型在大多数指标下都表现出一定的表征偏差。

Conclusion: 需要进一步对媒体表征和AI生成内容进行定量评估。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in media production from journalistm to filmmaking, what impact do they have on the stories being told? Prior work has shown LLMs to perpetuate social biases, including those related to gender. We complement existing literature on gender bias in LLM outputs by auditing the network structure of LLM-generated movie screenplays through automating the Bechdel test, a popular measure of women's representation in literary and film works. We also introduce the use of social network analysis measures to further analyze representational bias in LLM-generated scripts. We evaluate screenplays generated by three state-of-the-art LLMs (GPT-5, Gemini 3 Pro, and Claude Sonnet 4.5) against 768 corresponding human-written screenplays, finding that human-written scripts are more likely to pass the Bechdel test. However, other network analyses, like centrality, homophily, and triadic relationships demonstrate that in some cases LLM-scripts have less bias, although all script types demonstrate some representational bias under most measures. We conclude by discussing the continued need for further quantitative assessments of media representations and AI-generated content.

</details>


### [345] [The impact of generative artificial intelligence on academic development of Chinese students in humanities and social sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24104)
*Lei Fan,Fangxue Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 基于对中国人文社科学生的大规模调查，研究生成式AI在学习动机、独立思考、创造力及学业表现方面的影响。多数学生认为有积极提升，但也存在过度依赖、准确性问题、隐私顾虑等挑战；学生倾向于部分/可选的课程整合方式，并普遍认可GenAI对未来职业发展的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在重塑高等教育学习方式，对人文社科领域影响尤为显著（因该领域学习成果常以写作和解释性形式呈现，与GenAI能力高度契合），但关于GenAI对人文社科学生教育影响的系统性证据仍然有限，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用大规模问卷调查方法，对中国人文社科学生进行调查。基于相关学习理论，聚焦四个维度：使用模式、对学习过程和学业表现的影响、使用GenAI面临的挑战、以及课程整合的偏好方式。

Result: 超过半数学生认为学习动机、独立思考和创造力得到提升，但相当比例学生报告变化不大或有所下降；更多学生报告学业表现提升，但这可能部分反映了传统评估方式的局限性。不同GenAI使用时长的学生在感知学习和表现提升方面存在差异，同时存在可观察的学科差异和适度的性别差异。绝大多数学生重视伦理考量，但仅略超半数对隐私保护满意。准确性问题与过度依赖是最突出的担忧。学生倾向于部分或可选的课程整合方式，并普遍认可GenAI对未来职业发展的重要性。

Conclusion: 基于学生视角，本研究为负责任且具有教学意义的GenAI整合提供了循证建议。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence(GenAI) is reshaping learning in higher education, with particularly pronounced implications for the humanities and social sciences(HSS), where learning outcomes are commonly expressed through written and interpretive forms that align closely with GenAI's capabilities. Yet, systematic evidence on the educational impacts of GenAI on HSS students remains limited. Addressing this gap, this study draws on a large-scale survey of HSS students in China to examine its role in academic development. Guided by relevant learning theories, this study focuses on four dimensions: patterns of use, effects on learning processes and academic performance, challenges associated with GenAI use, and preferred approaches to curricular integration. We found that more than half perceived enhanced learning motivation, independent thinking and creativity, although a substantial minority reported little change or even decline. Comparatively, a notably larger majority reported academic performance gains, although these gains may partly reflect limitations in conventional assessment practices. The study identifies variations in perceived learning and performance improvements among students with differing durations of GenAI experience, along with observable disciplinary differences and modest gender differences. While an overwhelming majority valued the importance of ethical considerations, only slightly more than half were satisfied with privacy protection. Limited accuracy and overreliance emerged as the most pressing concerns reported by students. Students favored partial or optional curricular integration supported by practice-oriented training, and widely recognized GenAI's significance for their future professional development. Grounded in student perspectives, this study offers evidence-based recommendations for the responsible and pedagogically meaningful integration of GenAI

</details>


### [346] [Human-Centered Design: The Disclosure of Generative Artificial Intelligence for Emerging Professionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24136)
*Sydney Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在以人为本的设计（HCD）课程中，通过结构化任务、明确边界和强制披露AI使用的方式，使生成式AI成为增强而非削弱人类设计师能力的工具，避免技能退化和伦理风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI在HCD领域的应用增长，AI可能带来伦理偏见、复杂性增加以及对人文价值的忽视。尤其是新兴从业者若缺乏HCD基础，AI可能成为"拐杖"而非工具，导致深度工作能力下降、自主性降低和关键基础技能退化。现有AI使用披露方法缺乏对合理实施的指导，且可能鼓励隐瞒。

Method: 基于ITIS8300 HCD课程的一学期项目实践，采用里程碑式结构、团队角色分配（包括生成式AI倡导者），设置明确边界要求融入人工反馈，最终生成高水平的AI使用披露报告，详细说明设计过程、方法、发现及AI使用理由。

Result: 该课程在赋予执行自由的同时设定明确界限，强化了HCI工作流程的合理性，并鼓励透明的AI使用。这种模式模拟了低监管行业环境，证明当AI使用是安全、合理且透明时，能够通过AI增强工作流程显著推进该领域发展，支持协同创造并提高生产力。

Conclusion: 在HCD教育中，通过结构化课程设计、明确的AI使用边界和强制披露机制，可以确保AI作为增强工具而非替代品，在提升效率的同时保护人类设计师的核心能力和自主性。

Abstract: As the Human centered design continues to grow, generative AI has the potential to streamline the research process by iterating tasks within established workflows to increase efficiency. However, integrating AI raises concerns surrounding ethical bias, complexity, and the lack of prioritization of humanistic values. Emerging professionals represent a cohort with the opportunity to learn Human Centered Design principles, yet without this foundation AI becomes more of a crutch than a tool, leading to reduced experience with deep work, decreased autonomy, and deskilling of key foundations. Disclosures are a common method to self report AI usage, but they provide little clarification on appropriate implementation and may encourage omission to avoid consequences. This paper reflects on experiences in the Human Centered Design course ITIS8300, which emphasized optimizing user experience, enhancing innovation and collaboration, and improving efficiency through iterative user feedback. A semester long project, structured through milestones and team roles including a generative AI advocate, resulted in a high level disclosure report detailing design processes, methodology, findings, and rationale for AI usage. The course offered freedom in execution while setting clear boundaries for incorporating human feedback, reinforcing justification for HCI workflows and encouraging transparent AI use. This approach mirrors an industry with minimal regulation, demonstrating that when AI usage is safe, justified, and transparent, it can significantly advance the field through AI augmented workflows and support co creation an increase productivity.

</details>


### [347] [A Dynamic Coupling Theory of Expertise Through Thinking Flow and Workflow Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24197)
*Annie Yuan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"工作流认知"（Workflow Cognition）作为解释专业能力（expertise）的理论框架，将其定义为思维流（Thinking Flow）与工作流演化（Workflow Evolution）递归耦合所涌现的认知架构，并重新定义专业能力为工作流认知在纵向职业经验中运作的涌现表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专业知识理论（如内隐知识、刻意练习、技能习得、专家表现）多描述专业能力的条件或结果，而非解释其持续涌现和演化的认知架构。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过理论建构提出"工作流认知"框架，定义"思维流"与"工作流演化"的递归耦合机制，并辅以手工艺、创意生产、教育和领导力等领域的比较分析，建立动态耦合模型。

Result: 提出专业能力的新本体论定义：专业能力是工作流认知在纵向职业经验中的涌现表现；知识、技能、决策、审美偏好和行为模式是可观察的表达而非专业能力本身；建立了动态耦合模型，为纵向内隐认知、纵向审美认知和专业能力工作流语法奠定基础。

Conclusion: 该框架贡献了一种专业能力的认知本体论，支持未来AI+专家系统中人类专业能力的计算表征，将专业能力从静态知识积累重新概念化为实践中的认知生成过程。

Abstract: Expertise has long been explained through tacit knowledge, deliberate practice, skill acquisition, and expert performance. While these perspectives have advanced understanding of expertise, they often describe its conditions or outcomes rather than the cognitive architecture through which expertise continuously emerges and evolves. This paper proposes Workflow Cognition as a theoretical framework for explaining expertise as a dynamic cognitive phenomenon. Workflow Cognition is defined as the cognitive architecture emerging from the recursive coupling of Thinking Flow and Workflow Evolution. Thinking Flow refers to ongoing processes of perception, interpretation, judgement, decision-making, and reflection; Workflow Evolution refers to the continuous adaptation of actions, task structures, and operational strategies within situated practice. Through their coupling, expertise is not treated as a static accumulation of knowledge or skill, but as an evolving process generated through cognition-in-practice.
  Building on this framework, the paper advances a new ontological definition of expertise: expertise is an emergent manifestation of Workflow Cognition operating across longitudinal professional experience. Knowledge, skills, decisions, aesthetic preferences, and behavioural patterns are therefore interpreted as observable expressions of expertise rather than expertise itself. Drawing on illustrative comparisons across craft, creative production, education, and leadership, the paper introduces a Dynamic Coupling Model of Expertise and establishes a foundation for future work on Longitudinal Tacit Cognition, Longitudinal Aesthetic Cognition, and Expertise Workflow Grammar. The framework contributes a cognitive ontology of expertise and supports future computational representations of human expertise within AI+Expert systems.

</details>


### [348] [Average Rankings Mask Per-Subject Optimality: A Friedman-Nemenyi Benchmark of EEG Motor-Imagery BCI Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24394)
*Xavier Vasques,Paul Barbaste,Olivier Oullier*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模基准测试（1,056种配置、34万+模型训练）证明，即使在对解码最有利的单次会话条件下，基于运动想象的脑机接口中没有任何单一解码流水线能普遍优于其他方法；协方差切空间投影和共空间模式是最强的特征族，但排序依赖数据集且在大规模异质人群中无显著差异；个体层面最佳流水线仅对35%参与者最优，提示需个性化模型选择而非通用解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电图运动想象解码因个体间和个体内变异性而面临挑战，文献中常声称某种解码流水线（如空间方法或黎曼方法）具有广泛优越性。本文旨在严格检验这一声称的最弱版本——即在最有利条件下是否真的存在一个普遍最优的解码流水线。

Method: 使用MOABB框架，在三个公开数据集（PhysionetMI 109人、Cho2017 52人、Zhou2016 4人）和两个频带（8-15Hz、8-30Hz）上，评估1,056种解码配置（特征提取器×缩放器×分类器），进行超过34万次个体级模型拟合。所有模型在单次会话的单个参与者内拟合和测试，采用Friedman检验、Nemenyi临界差分析和Wilcoxon符号秩检验等统计方法进行多分类器比较。

Result: 协方差切空间投影和共空间模式是最强的特征族，但其排序依赖于数据集；在最大且最异质的队列（PhysionetMI）上两者统计上不可区分（Nemenyi p=0.27；Kendall's W=0.11）。个体层面单一最优流水线仅对35%的PhysionetMI参与者最优，非线性描述符对约三分之一参与者最佳。为参与者匹配流水线相比固定最佳选择可增加约7个准确率百分点。分类器和缩放器的选择相对于特征表示是次要的。

Conclusion: 即使在最有利的解码条件下，不存在任何单一解码流水线能普遍胜出；这为个性化问题设定了下界，并定量论证了需要基于参与者意识的模型选择而非通用解码器。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is the dominant non-invasive modality for brain-computer interfaces (BCIs), yet reliable decoding of motor imagery is hampered by inter- and intra-individual variability. A recurring claim is that one decoding pipeline, most often a spatial or Riemannian method, is broadly preferable. We test the weakest version of that claim under the most favourable conditions. Using the Mother of All BCI Benchmarks (MOABB) framework, we evaluated 1,056 decoding configurations (feature extractor x scaler x classifier), >340,000 subject-level model fits, across three public left-versus-right motor-imagery datasets (PhysionetMI, 109 participants; Cho2017, 52; Zhou2016, 4) and two frequency bands (8-15 Hz, 8-30 Hz). Every model is fit and tested within a single session of a single participant, the easiest regime, giving every pipeline its best chance. We apply the statistics standard for multi-classifier comparison: Friedman omnibus tests, Nemenyi critical-difference analysis and Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with effect sizes. Covariance tangent-space projection (cov-tgsp) and Common Spatial Patterns (CSP) are the strongest families, but their ordering is dataset-dependent and, on the largest and most heterogeneous cohort (PhysionetMI), statistically indistinguishable (Nemenyi p = 0.27; Kendall's W = 0.11). At the individual level the single best pipeline is optimal for only 35% of PhysionetMI participants, and nonlinear descriptors are best for roughly one third; matching pipeline to participant adds about seven accuracy points over the best fixed choice. The ranking is not an artefact of dimensionality, and classifier and scaler choices are secondary to the feature representation. Even in the easiest regime, no single pipeline dominates: a lower bound on the personalization problem and a quantitative case for participant-aware model selection rather than a universal decoder.

</details>


### [349] [Optimizing Visual Analytics Workflows: From Theory to Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24454)
*Philip Beaucamp,Alfie Abdul-Rahman,Rita Borgo,Wolfgang Jentner,Saiful Khan,Yiwen Xing,David Ebert,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何将基于信息论的可视分析（VA）工作流优化方法论从理论转化为实践。通过行动研究方法，多个领域的研究者对方法论进行了案例验证，分析了其在实践中的优势、可行性和障碍，并提出了消除障碍的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VA的核心原则是人机协同，但在实践中需要权衡不同过程（人/机）的优劣并优化工作流。现有理论方法论虽已提出，但缺乏从理论到实践的转化路径验证，阻碍了其广泛部署。

Method: 采用行动研究方法，基于已有VA方法论本体（信息论分析权衡、系统优化工作流），通过跨领域案例研究，由不同背景的VA研究者对多个假设进行实践验证并反馈改进建议。

Result: 呈现了集体分析结果，总结了该理论方法论的优势、可行性和实践中遇到的障碍，并提出了具体的解决方案路线图。

Conclusion: 该理论方法论在实践中具备可用性和优势，但存在若干障碍需克服；论文提出的路线图为VA方法论从理论走向实践提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: The principle of visual analytics (VA) is to provide integrated workflows where human-centric processes (e.g., visualization and interaction) and machine-centric processes (e.g., statistics and algorithms) complement each other. To implement this principle in practice, it is necessary to reason about the trade-offs among different processes and make optimal use of them in a workflow. Building on an existing ontology of the methodology for analyzing such trade-offs information-theoretically and for optimizing VA workflows systematically, we investigate ways to transform this methodology from theory to practice. In particular, we adopted the action research method. Through case studies in different application domains, VA researchers with different background knowledge and experiences offered their answers to several hypotheses about using the methodology in practice and proposed ways forward. In this paper, we present our collective analysis, the strengths and feasibility of this theory-based methodology, as well as the obstacles to its broad deployment in practice. To address these challenges, we outline a roadmap to remove such obstacles.

</details>


### [350] [Visualizing "We the People": Bridging the Perception Gap through Pluralistic Data Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24635)
*Lisa Schirch,Beth Goldberg*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文批判了传统数据可视化中二元对立图形加剧政治极化的弊端，提出借助AI驱动平台的多元化设计策略可以呈现观点分布与群体共性。通过分析2025年9月Jigsaw与Napolitan研究所开展的"我们人民"审议项目（覆盖2400多名美国公民、435个国会选区），展示了AI如何将长文本参与者输入综合为交互式"观点景观"，从而人性化多元视角并揭示隐藏的广泛共识。论文认为，从对比突出的可视化框架转向分布聚焦的交互模型，是一种低成本、高可扩展性的干预手段，有助于弥合感知鸿沟并培育更具韧性的协作民主文化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据叙事依赖二元对立图形，简化群体内部分歧并抹杀模糊性与共同价值观，无意中助长"我们vs他们"的对立思维，加剧政治极化。因此需要探索AI赋能的多元化可视化设计来改善这一状况。

Method: 采用案例分析法，以"We the People"审议项目为实例。该项目利用AI技术将2400多名参与者关于自由与平等的长文本输入综合为交互式"观点景观"地图，展示高维意见空间中的共识与分歧区域。

Result: AI赋能的交互式观点可视化能够呈现多元化视角、人性化不同立场，并揭示出被二元叙事掩盖的广泛实质性共识区域。

Conclusion: 从对比突出的视觉框架转向分布聚焦的交互模型，是一种高度可扩展、低成本的干预方式，能够弥合群体间的感知分歧，培育更具韧性和协作性的民主文化。

Abstract: Traditional visual data storytelling relies on binary graphics that depict two simplified groups in conflict. This can increase political polarization by oversimplifying intra-group disagreements and erasing ambiguity and shared ideas or values. This can inadvertently foster "us versus them" thinking. Intentional, pluralistic design choices for AI-enabled digital platforms can produce visualizations that emphasize nuance, opinion distribution, and intergroup commonalities. To demonstrate this potential, we examine deliberative technologies that map high-dimensional opinion spaces and highlight areas of both consensus and dissensus. The paper highlights the We the People deliberation conducted by Jigsaw and the Napolitan Institute in September 2025, which engaged over 2,400 Americans across all 435 congressional districts in an AI-supported, asynchronous dialogue regarding freedom and equality. By utilizing AI to synthesize long-form, text-based participant inputs into interactive "opinion landscapes," the initiative provided an alternative format for pluralistic data storytelling that humanized diverse viewpoints and revealed hidden areas of substantial broad consensus. The paper concludes that shifting from divisive, contrast-heavy visual frameworks to distribution-focused, interactive models represents a highly scalable, low-cost intervention capable of bridging perceptual gaps and cultivating a more resilient, collaborative democratic culture.

</details>


### [351] [SupplyNet: Supporting Visual Exploratory Learning in Supply Chain via Contextual Multi-Agent Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24694)
*Yanjia Li,Kelcy Kexin Han,Tianrui Hu,Yi-Fan Cao,Huamin Qu,Sicheng Song*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SupplyNet是一个基于上下文图LLM多智能体框架的 gamified 可视化供应链模拟系统，通过交互式网络视图、分支时间线和任务导向分析控制台，将模拟转化为可操作的决策空间，用户研究表明该系统能提升参与度并帮助理解供应链动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有供应链管理模拟系统呈现抽象、不透明的数据，使学习者不堪重负并阻碍主动探索，需要更丰富、更具适应性的模拟环境。

Method: 基于上下文图的多智能体LLM框架构建供应链动态模型，通过分层挑战提供响应式反馈；集成三个可视化组件：交互式网络视图（系统状态）、分支时间线（假设探索与比较）、任务导向分析控制台（结构化性能分解）。

Result: 用户研究表明SupplyNet提升了用户参与度，并增强了用户对供应链动态的感知理解。

Conclusion: 将上下文多智能体模拟与可视化相结合，有助于提升操作理解能力，展示了该方向在供应链管理教学中的潜力。

Abstract: Simulation has long supported supply chain management instruction by letting learners observe network behavior and test decision strategies. Recent progress in LLM-driven agents opens new possibilities for richer, more adaptive simulations, but many existing systems still present abstract, opaque data that overwhelms learners and discourages active exploration. We introduce \textit{SupplyNet}, a gamified visual simulation system built on a contextual graph-based LLM multi-agent framework that models interdependent supply chain dynamics and provides responsive feedback through tiered challenges. \textit{SupplyNet} turns the simulation into a manipulable decision space by integrating an interactive network view of system state, a branching timeline for "what-if" exploration and comparison, and a task-oriented analysis console for structured performance breakdowns. Together, these visual components support counterfactual exploration, causal tracing, and comparative reasoning about outcomes. A user study suggests that \textit{SupplyNet} increases engagement and supports users' perceived understanding of supply chain dynamics, highlighting the potential of pairing contextual multi-agent simulation with visualization to advance operational comprehension.

</details>


### [352] [SciFi-VIS: Way Out There -- How SciFi and Visualization Influence Each Other](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24731)
*Ulrik Günther,Julián Méndez,Gabriela Molina León,Samuel Pantze,Mario Romero,Abdulhaq Adetunji Salako,Annalena Ulschmid*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出在IEEE VIS 2026上举办混合式半日研讨会，旨在促进可视化研究者与科幻创作者之间的双向交流，通过多种创意形式建立系统性理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可视化与科幻创作社区长期存在双向影响关系，但缺乏系统性的理解和正式交流平台。研讨会旨在填补这一空白，促进跨学科合作。

Method: 采用混合式半日研讨会形式，包含主题演讲、闪电演讲、头脑风暴、跨社区评议、亲和图映射及主题讨论等多种互动环节。

Result: 预期产出包括对两社区关系的系统性理解，以及激发未来可视化研究与科幻创作交叉领域的创新研究方向。

Conclusion: 该研讨会为可视化与科幻创作之间建立正式交流渠道提供了框架，有望推动两个领域的协同发展。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid half-day workshop at IEEE VIS 2026, calling for participation from visualization researchers and science fiction creators in order to develop a systematic understanding of the two-way relationship these communities have long shared. We invite submissions of creative formats showcasing connections and inspiring future research. Our workshop plan includes a keynote, lightning talks, brainstorming, cross-community critique, affinity mapping, and discussion around identified themes.

</details>


### [353] [Virtual Simulation for Mental Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24826)
*Anna Fang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何利用虚拟仿真技术（包括基于智能体的建模和具身化控制空间）在心理健康领域进行安全实验，通过以人为本的设计方法，在不带来现实风险的前提下测试和训练心理健康干预策略，从而构建可扩展、安全且有效的用户中心系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康干预措施失败可能导致持久的负面后果，而现有技术干预虽具可及性和规模化潜力，但缺乏安全试验的途径。因此，需要在虚拟环境中进行可控实验，以避免现实世界中的风险。

Method: 采用以人为中心的方法，结合两种虚拟仿真途径：(1) 基于智能体的建模（Agent-Based Modeling）评估社区层面的干预结果；(2) 通过具身化、可控的虚拟空间进行个体层面的心理健康训练。系统设计由对现实世界需求的实证理解驱动。

Result: 通过虚拟仿真系统，证明了可以在心理健康领域安全地测试和练习干预策略，从而避免现实世界中的失败风险，同时保持干预的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 虚拟仿真是心理健康领域安全实验的有力工具，能够弥合实验室研究与现实应用之间的鸿沟，为设计安全、可扩展、以用户为中心的心理健康干预系统开辟新路径。

Abstract: Poorly designed interventions or those deployed without adequate safeguards can harm the communities they aim to serve, thus exacerbating existing vulnerabilities and leaving individuals unsupported. This is especially the case for the mental health context, where there is a growing trend of relying on technological interventions due to their accessibility and ability to deliver large-scale support. However, the mental health context is also particularly sensitive to change and risks of failure are dire; at their worst, failures in mental health interventions can result in lasting negative outcomes for individuals and tragic losses as people fall through the cracks. Thus, enabling safe ways to experiment in the mental health context is vital to allow both individuals and communities to engage with new interventions without risk of their real-world consequences. Virtual simulation, which uses virtual environments to replicate real-world interactions, processes, and behaviors, offers a promising opportunity for enabling safe, controlled experimentation with its ability to accurately replicate social situations, fears, stressors, and the potential outcomes of specific interactions. This work explores how simulation approaches can support emerging mental health processes through (1) evaluating community-level outcomes using agent-based modeling and (2) individual training in the mental health context through embodied, controlled spaces. I demonstrate this use of virtual simulation systems through a grounded human-centered approach, where system design is guided by empirical understanding of current real-world needs and challenges. By leveraging simulation to create environments where mental health strategies can be safely tested and practiced, this work aims to open new possibilities for designing scalable, user-centered systems that are effective and safe.

</details>


### [354] [It's Complicated: On the Design and Evaluation of AI-Powered AAC Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24854)
*Blade Frisch,Will Wade,Dylan Gaines,Michelle Kinsella,Betts Peters,Tamara Broderick,Keith Vertanen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI如何增强AAC（辅助与替代沟通）系统的功能，指出当前评估指标难以捕捉AAC用户作为交叉性个体的复杂需求，分析了六个AAC问题空间及AI的应用潜力，并提出了更鲁棒的、考虑用户交叉性细微差异的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI驱动的AAC界面评估方法难以充分反映用户作为交叉性个体（intersectional beings）的多面性与细微需求，需要更全面的评估框架。

Method: 通过分析六个AAC问题空间的复杂性，探讨AI在这些空间中的应用方式，并提出考虑用户交叉性差异的更鲁棒的评估方法。

Result: 揭示了AAC问题空间的复杂性与AI应用的潜力，并提出了更具包容性的评估方案。

Conclusion: 需要采用更全面的、考虑用户交叉性特征的评估方法，以更好地驱动AI在AAC系统中的应用。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) can enhance what people who use augmentative and alternative communication (AAC) are able to do with their systems. However, evaluating AI-powered AAC interfaces can be difficult. People are intersectional beings and current evaluation metrics can struggle to capture the multifaceted and nuanced desires people may have for their AAC. We explore the complicated nature of six AAC problem spaces, explore how AI might be used in these spaces, and suggest more robust methods of evaluation that take the intersectional nuances of people into account. We also discuss broader issues that arise across these problem spaces and how they could be addressed using our proposed evaluation methods.

</details>


### [355] ["Zooming In" on Agentic Web Browsers as Assistive Technologies: A Case Study with a Low-Vision Technology Expert](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24870)
*Laura Colazzo,Giuseppe Anzillotti*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过一项低视力技术专家的案例研究，探讨了基于大语言模型的自主网络浏览器（AWB）作为辅助技术对视觉障碍用户的支持效果。研究发现，尽管存在当前局限，但AWB能提供流畅灵活的网络导航体验，有望显著提升可访问性并降低网络交互障碍，其影响可能超越辅助技术范畴，延伸至更广泛的代理用户体验设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于LLM的自主网络浏览器作为辅助技术，帮助视觉障碍用户更流畅地进行网络导航，从而提升网络可访问性并减少交互障碍。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，邀请一位低视力技术专家参与实验，评估自主网络浏览器在支持视觉障碍用户网络导航中的表现和用户体验。

Result: 自主网络浏览器能够提供流畅且灵活的网络导航体验，尽管存在当前技术局限性，但整体上显著增强了网络可访问性。

Conclusion: AWBs在辅助视觉障碍用户方面具有巨大潜力，能够将网络交互转变为流畅的对话式体验，其设计启示可推广至代理用户体验的广泛领域。

Abstract: Agentic Web Browsers (AWBs), powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), are emerging as autonomous systems capable of navigating the Web on behalf of users. Beyond enhancing productivity, they could also offer significant promise as Assistive Technologies (ATs) for visually-impaired individuals, transforming web interaction into a fluid conversational exchange. In this paper, we present a case study with a low-vision technology expert, examining how AWBs can support visually-impaired users in web navigation. The findings show that, despite the current limitations, the navigation experience is notably fluid and flexible, underscoring the strong potential of AWBs to enhance accessibility and reduce barriers in web interaction, with implications that may extend beyond accessibility to agentic UX more broadly.

</details>
